
===== system/tests/test_ac_gate_family.py =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	"""§20 验收 ID 专属测试——GATE/MRG/CI 族（test_ac_gate_family）。
     4	
     5	覆盖 Codex 方案 §20.1/§20.5 中 GATE-01~08/12、MRG-01~07、CI-05/07/08/09
     6	共 20 条：每条按「注入→期望」逐字对齐方案表格，真实构造场景（真实 git
     7	仓/tmp fixture、真实调用产品码）并断言结果，绝不空转。
     8	
     9	被测正源（不改动任何产品码）：
    10	- system/wenqu_core/source_gate.py    fresh_fetch / merge_base_diff /
    11	                                      affected_scope / execute_gate /
    12	                                      validate_exact_sha（GATE-01~04、
    13	                                      MRG-05/06/07、CI-05 本地等价）
    14	- system/wenqu_core/runner.py         EvidenceStore CAS（GATE-05/06：
    15	                                      symlink/hardlink/根外/哈希篡改拒绝）
    16	- system/wenqu_core/store.py          EventStore 控制事件 seal 守卫
    17	                                      （GATE-12：unsigned flip/adjudication）
    18	- system/wenqu_core/approval_keys.py  HMAC 验签（GATE-12 附属腿）
    19	- system/wenqu_core/bugscan_orchestrator.py
    20	                                      冻结身份/TTL/SourceGateAdapter/
    21	                                      station-result-v2 schema（GATE-07/08、
    22	                                      CI-07/09、MRG-06b）
    23	- system/wenqu_core/gate_aggregator.py
    24	                                      C1~C5 硬约束 + GitHub 状态映射
    25	                                      （MRG-01~04、CI-05a/08）
    26	- system/daemons/auto-merge.sh        P0 封存 stub（MRG-01 执行器腿：不合流）
    27	
    28	本地等价映射说明（CI 族依赖真实 GitHub API 的部分）：
    29	- CI-05 fork/merge_group → SourceGate.validate_exact_sha 对非目标 head 的
    30	  SHA（fork PR SHA、merge_group 临时 SHA）一律 StaleShaError（T-02 旧/异
    31	  SHA 绿灯拒合）；「check 不产生」→ GateAggregator required 站缺失 =
    32	  denominator_shrinkage → BLOCKED（无 SKIP/无静默通过语义）。
    33	- CI-07 未授权 App 自报同名 context → SourceGateAdapter 身份三源一律取
    34	  冻结 manifest 正源、绝不采信 gate_result 自报身份：自报 SHA 与冻结
    35	  不符 → SourceGateShaMismatch（不可信、阻断）。
    36	- CI-08 Wenqu Gate 缺失但 Bugscan 绿 → 站1（源闸）未上报、站2 绿 →
    37	  GateAggregator denominator_shrinkage BLOCKED → GitHub failure（不合流）。
    38	- CI-09 S7 递归聚合/重复发布 → station-result-v2 additionalProperties
    39	  schema 拒绝 + 聚合输出回灌聚合器缺 station_id 结构 → malformed BLOCKED。
    40	
    41	明确未实现（本文件不硬凑、不 xfail，见交付报告「不可实现清单」）：
    42	GATE-09（N=0/超上限非产品参数错误）、GATE-10（N+1 上限无实现）、
    43	GATE-11（异源轮 S 标签追踪无实现）、CI-06（CODEOWNERS/ruleset doctor 无实现）。
    44	
    45	独立运行：python3 system/tests/test_ac_gate_family.py [--json OUT.json]
    46	exit 0 = 全部用例绿；--json 额外输出逐 ID 结果数组 {id, test, passed}。
    47	"""

    92	def _ok(cond: bool, message: str) -> None:
    93	    if not cond:
    94	        raise AssertionError(message)
    95	
    96	
    97	def _expect_raise(exc_type: type, fn: Callable[..., Any], *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Exception:
    98	    """调用 fn，期望抛出 exc_type；返回异常对象供断言细节。"""
    99	    try:
   100	        fn(*args, **kwargs)
   101	    except exc_type as exc:  # type: ignore[misc]
   102	        return exc
   103	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   104	        raise AssertionError(
   105	            f"expected {exc_type.__name__}, got {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
   106	        ) from exc
   107	    raise AssertionError(f"expected {exc_type.__name__} to be raised, but call returned")
   108	
   109	
   110	@contextlib.contextmanager
   111	def _tmp(root: Optional[Path] = None):
   112	    """测试临时目录：外部传入（main 调度）或自建自清（pytest/独立调用兼容）。"""
   113	    if root is not None:
   114	        yield Path(root)
   115	        return
   116	    own = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="ac-gate-family-")
   117	    try:
   118	        yield Path(own)
   119	    finally:
   120	        shutil.rmtree(own, ignore_errors=True)
   121	
   122	
   123	def _git(repo: Path, *argv: str, check: bool = True) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
   124	    proc = subprocess.run(
   125	        ["git", *argv], cwd=str(repo), capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
   126	    )
   127	    if check and proc.returncode != 0:
   128	        raise RuntimeError(
   129	            f"git {' '.join(argv)} failed rc={proc.returncode}: {proc.stderr.strip()}"
   130	        )
   131	    return proc
   132	
   133	
   134	def _commit(repo: Path, files: Dict[str, str], message: str) -> None:
   135	    for name, content in files.items():
   136	        target = repo / name
   137	        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   138	        target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
   139	    _git(repo, "add", "-A")
   140	    _git(repo, "commit", "-m", message)
   141	
   142	
   143	def _head(repo: Path) -> str:
   144	    return _git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip().lower()
   145	
   146	
   147	def _make_repo(root: Path, *, behavioral_commit: bool = True) -> Path:
   148	    """真实 git fixture：main 两提交 + 定格在基线的 bare origin。
   149	
   150	    提交1：README.md（基线）；提交2（可选）：app.py + notes.md 混合变更。
   151	    origin 是提交1 时刻的 bare 克隆——本地 head 前进不影响 origin/main，
   152	    保证 merge-base diff 恒为「基线→head」的真实变更集。
   153	    """
   154	    repo = root / f"repo-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
   155	    repo.mkdir(parents=True)
   156	    _git(repo, "init")
   157	    # 隔离本机全局 git 配置（签名/hooks 不参与注入场景）
   158	    _git(repo, "config", "user.email", "gate-family@example.com")
   159	    _git(repo, "config", "user.name", "gate-family-tester")
   160	    _git(repo, "config", "commit.gpgsign", "false")
   161	    _git(repo, "config", "core.hooksPath", "/dev/null")
   162	    _commit(repo, {"README.md": "baseline\n"}, "base")
   163	    _git(repo, "branch", "-M", "main")
   164	    origin = root / f"{repo.name}-origin.git"
   165	    _git(repo, "clone", "--bare", ".", str(origin))  # 远端定格在提交1
   166	    _git(repo, "remote", "add", "origin", str(origin))
   167	    _git(repo, "fetch", "origin")  # refs/remotes/origin/main -> 提交1
   168	    if behavioral_commit:
   169	        _commit(repo, {"app.py": "print('behavior')\n", "notes.md": "doc\n"}, "change")
   170	    return repo
   171	
   172	
   173	def _legacy(station: str, outcome: str, sha: str, env: str = "test") -> Dict[str, str]:
   174	    """GateAggregator legacy 契约条目（station/outcome/sha/environment）。"""
   175	    return {"station": station, "outcome": outcome, "sha": sha, "environment": env}

   178	def _freeze_manifest(
   179	    now: datetime,
   180	    *,
   181	    commit_sha: str = "1" * 40,
   182	    run_id: str = "gatefam-run",
   183	    ruleset: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   184	    scope: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   185	) -> "bo.RunManifest":
   186	    """站0 冻结件（FAST 档）——SourceGateAdapter/复用拒绝测试的身份正源。"""
   187	    planner = bo.BugscanPlanner()
   188	    plan = planner.plan("FAST")
   189	    return planner.freeze_run_manifest(
   190	        plan,
   191	        run_id=run_id,
   192	        project_id="gate-family",
   193	        commit_sha=commit_sha,
   194	        environment="local",
   195	        scope=scope if scope is not None else ["src/**"],
   196	        ruleset=ruleset if ruleset is not None else {"version": 1},
   197	        data_config={"profile": "default"},
   198	        now=now,
   199	    )
   200	
   201	
   202	def _gate_result(
   203	    commit_sha: str, ended: datetime, *, exit_code: int = 0, started: Optional[datetime] = None
   204	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   205	    """SourceGateAdapter 消费的源闸结果载荷（components 全 COMPLETED）。"""
   206	    start = started if started is not None else ended - timedelta(minutes=10)
   207	    return {
   208	        "commit_sha": commit_sha,
   209	        "exit_code": exit_code,
   210	        "tool": {"name": "ci-fast", "version": "1"},
   211	        "started_at": start.isoformat(),
   212	        "ended_at": ended.isoformat(),
   213	        "components": [
   214	            {"name": "lint", "status": "COMPLETED", "exit_code": 0},
   215	            {"name": "unit", "status": "COMPLETED", "exit_code": 0},
   216	        ],
   217	    }
   218	
   219	
   220	def _github_failure(agg: GateAggregator, aggregate: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, str]:
   221	    return agg.to_github_status(aggregate)

   227	def test_GATE_01_fetch_fail_or_missing_base_ref_is_error(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   228	    """GATE-01｜注入：base ref 不存在/fetch 失败｜期望：ERROR/BLOCKED。"""
   229	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   230	        # 腿一：remote 指向不存在的路径 → git fetch 非零 → ERROR(2)，绝不 SKIP
   231	        repo = _make_repo(td)
   232	        broken = td / f"nowhere-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
   233	        _git(repo, "remote", "add", "broken", str(broken))
   234	        out_json = td / "g01a.json"
   235	        gate = SourceGate(str(repo), remote="broken", result_path=str(out_json))
   236	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   237	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_ERROR, f"fetch 失败须 exit 2，got {result['exit_code']}")
   238	        _ok(result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", "fetch 失败须 execution_status=ERROR")
   239	        _ok(result["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", "ERROR 时不得给策略判定")
   240	        _ok(result["error"]["type"] == "SourceGateError", "错误类型须为 SourceGateError")
   241	        _ok("fetch" in result["error"]["message"], "错误信息须指向 fetch")
   242	        _ok(out_json.exists(), "失败证据工件必须照常落盘")
   243	        # 腿二：fetch 成功但 base ref 不存在 → verify 阶段 ERROR(2)
   244	        gate2 = SourceGate(str(repo), base_ref="origin/definitely-missing-ref")
   245	        exc = _expect_raise(SourceGateError, gate2.fresh_fetch)
   246	        _ok(exc.execution_status == "ERROR" and exc.exit_code == EXIT_ERROR,
   247	            f"base ref 不存在须 ERROR/2，got {exc.execution_status}/{exc.exit_code}")
   248	
   249	
   250	def test_GATE_02_zero_tests_scope_not_pure_docs_is_blocked(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   251	    """GATE-02｜注入：零测试、scope 未证明纯文档｜期望：BLOCKED。"""
   252	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   253	        repo = _make_repo(td)  # 提交2：app.py + notes.md（行为+文档混合）
   254	        # 影响图只认 src/*.py，app.py 不可解析 → 升级 full；未配置全量套件 → BLOCKED
   255	        gate = SourceGate(
   256	            str(repo),
   257	            impact_graph={"src/*.py": [["true"]]},
   258	            result_path=str(td / "g02.json"),
   259	        )
   260	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   261	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_BLOCKED, f"须 BLOCKED exit 3，got {result['exit_code']}")
   262	        _ok(result["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", "须 execution_status=BLOCKED")
   263	        _ok(result["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", "BLOCKED 不得带策略判定")
   264	        _ok("FULL gate" in result["error"]["message"],
   265	            "缺全量套件报错须点名 FULL gate（零测试绝不静默放行）")
   266	        scope = result["scope"]
   267	        _ok(scope["mode"] == "full" and scope["escalated"], "不可解析影响图须升级 full")
   268	        _ok("app.py" in scope["behavioral_files"], "app.py 是行为变更")
   269	        _ok("notes.md" in scope["non_behavioral_files"], "notes.md 归非行为面")
   270	        _ok(result["policy_verdict"] != "NOT_APPLICABLE",
   271	            "scope 未证明纯文档，不得记 NOT_APPLICABLE（唯一合法『无测试』）")
   272

   274	def test_GATE_03_missing_runner_or_dependency_is_error(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   275	    """GATE-03｜注入：test runner/依赖缺失｜期望：ERROR。"""
   276	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   277	        repo = _make_repo(td)
   278	        # 腿一：测试器可执行不存在于 PATH → ERROR(2)（不是 SKIP）
   279	        missing_tool = f"wenqu-no-such-tester-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
   280	        gate = SourceGate(
   281	            str(repo),
   282	            impact_graph={"app.py": [[missing_tool]]},
   283	            result_path=str(td / "g03a.json"),
   284	        )
   285	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   286	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_ERROR, f"缺测试器须 ERROR 2，got {result['exit_code']}")
   287	        _ok(result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", "缺测试器须 ERROR")
   288	        _ok("missing on PATH" in result["error"]["message"]
   289	            and "not SKIP" in result["error"]["message"],
   290	            "报错须显式声明 ERROR, not SKIP")
   291	        # 腿二：required_paths 依赖目录缺失（如 node_modules）→ ERROR(2)
   292	        gate2 = SourceGate(
   293	            str(repo),
   294	            impact_graph={"app.py": [["true"]]},
   295	            required_paths=["node_modules"],
   296	            result_path=str(td / "g03b.json"),
   297	        )
   298	        result2 = gate2.execute_gate()
   299	        _ok(result2["exit_code"] == EXIT_ERROR, "缺依赖路径须 ERROR 2")
   300	        _ok("node_modules" in result2["error"]["message"], "报错须点名缺失依赖")
   301	        _ok(result2["error"]["detail"].get("missing_paths") == ["node_modules"],
   302	            "missing_paths 证据须落账")
   303

   305	def test_GATE_04_inner_exit7_selfreport_zero_findings_fails(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   306	    """GATE-04｜注入：内层 exit 7、自报零 finding｜期望：assertion FAIL/BLOCKED。"""
   307	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   308	        repo = _make_repo(td)
   309	        # 注入体：内层工具自报零 finding（stdout JSON 一片绿）却 exit 7
   310	        payload_line = json.dumps({"findings": 0, "verdict": "PASS"})
   311	        script = td / "fake_scanner.py"
   312	        script.write_text(
   313	            "import json, sys\n"
   314	            f"print({payload_line!r})\n"
   315	            "sys.exit(7)\n",
   316	            encoding="utf-8",
   317	        )
   318	        gate = SourceGate(
   319	            str(repo),
   320	            impact_graph={"app.py": [["python3", str(script)]]},
   321	            result_path=str(td / "g04.json"),
   322	        )
   323	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   324	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_FAIL, f"内层 exit 7 须 FAIL exit 1，got {result['exit_code']}")
   325	        _ok(result["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED" and result["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL",
   326	            "真实退出码驱动的判定必须是 COMPLETED/FAIL")
   327	        checks = result["checks"]
   328	        _ok(len(checks) == 1 and checks[0]["exit_code"] == 7,
   329	            "checks 须记录真实 returncode=7（TrustedRunner 唯一退出码来源）")
   330	        expected_stdout_sha = hashlib.sha256((payload_line + "\n").encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
   331	        _ok(checks[0]["stdout_sha256"] == expected_stdout_sha,
   332	            "自报『零 finding』的 stdout 已入证据，但不得据此放行（自报不作数）")
   333	
   334	
   335	def test_GATE_05_artifact_symlink_hardlink_outside_root_blocked(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   336	    """GATE-05｜注入：artifact symlink/hardlink/根外｜期望：BLOCKED（拒绝）。"""
   337	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   338	        cas_root = td / "cas"
   339	        store = EvidenceStore(str(cas_root))
   340	
   341	        def _evidence(n: int) -> Evidence:
   342	            return Evidence(
   343	                argv=("tool", f"--case={n}"),
   344	                cwd=str(td),
   345	                actual_exit_code=0,
   346	                stdout_sha256=hashlib.sha256(f"out{n}".encode()).hexdigest(),
   347	                stderr_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest(),
   348	                argv_digest=hashlib.sha256(f"argv{n}".encode()).hexdigest(),
   349	                timestamp=float(n),
   350	                fresh_until=float(n) + 1,
   351	            )
   352	
   353	        ev_symlink = _evidence(1)
   354	        d_symlink = EvidenceStore.digest_for(ev_symlink)
   355	        shard = cas_root / d_symlink[:2]
   356	        shard.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   357	        outside = td / "outside-secret.json"
   358	        outside.write_text("root-external payload\n", encoding="utf-8")
   359	        os.symlink(outside, shard / f"{d_symlink}.json")  # 根外 symlink 占位
   360	        exc = _expect_raise(IntegrityError, store.put, ev_symlink)
   361	        _ok("symlink" in str(exc), "symlink 占位必须 IntegrityError 拒绝写入")
   362	
   363	        ev_hard = _evidence(2)
   364	        d_hard = EvidenceStore.digest_for(ev_hard)
   365	        hard_shard = cas_root / d_hard[:2]
   366	        hard_shard.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   367	        link_src = td / "hardlink-source.json"
   368	        link_src.write_text("alias payload\n", encoding="utf-8")
   369	        os.link(link_src, hard_shard / f"{d_hard}.json")  # st_nlink=2 预置
   370	        exc2 = _expect_raise(IntegrityError, store.put, ev_hard)
   371	        _ok("hardlink" in str(exc2), "预置 hardlink（st_nlink>1）必须拒绝")
   372	
   373	        # 根外/穿越：digest 必须是 64-hex，任何路径形 digest 直接拒绝
   374	        for bad in ("../evil", "..%2f", "a" * 63, "/etc/passwd"):
   375	            exc3 = _expect_raise(IntegrityError, store.get, bad)
   376	            _ok("invalid digest" in str(exc3), f"digest 穿越 {bad!r} 必须在寻址层拒绝")
   377	        store.close()
   378	
   379	
   380	def test_GATE_06_artifact_hash_tamper_blocked(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   381	    """GATE-06｜注入：artifact hash 篡改｜期望：BLOCKED（拒绝消费）。"""
   382	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   383	        # 腿一：CAS 内容篡改 → get() 哈希复核拒绝
   384	        store = EvidenceStore(str(td / "cas"))
   385	        ev = Evidence(
   386	            argv=("tool",),
   387	            cwd=str(td),
   388	            actual_exit_code=0,
   389	            stdout_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"out").hexdigest(),
   390	            stderr_sha256=hashlib.sha256(b"").hexdigest(),
   391	            argv_digest=hashlib.sha256(b"argv").hexdigest(),
   392	            timestamp=1.0,
   393	            fresh_until=2.0,
   394	        )
   395	        digest = store.put(ev)
   396	        stored_path = td / "cas" / digest[:2] / f"{digest}.json"
   397	        stored_path.write_text('{"tampered": true}', encoding="utf-8")  # 攻击者改字节
   398	        exc = _expect_raise(IntegrityError, store.get, digest)
   399	        _ok("hash mismatch" in str(exc), "内容寻址复核必须检出篡改并拒绝")
   400	        store.close()
   401	        # 腿二：冻结 manifest 篡改 → load() manifest_hash 复核拒绝
   402	        now = datetime.now(_UTC)
   403	        manifest = _freeze_manifest(now)
   404	        path = td / "run-manifest.json"
   405	        manifest.save(path)
   406	        raw = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   407	        raw["identity"]["commit_sha"] = "9" * 40  # 篡改身份
   408	        path.write_text(json.dumps(raw), encoding="utf-8")
   409	        exc2 = _expect_raise(bo.ManifestFreezeError, bo.RunManifest.load, path)
   410	        _ok("manifest_hash mismatch" in str(exc2), "冻结件被篡改必须拒绝加载")
   411	
   412	
   413	def test_GATE_07_commit_env_scope_ruleset_mismatch_blocked(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   414	    """GATE-07｜注入：commit/env/scope/ruleset 不符｜期望：BLOCKED。"""
   415	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   416	        sha = "a" * 40
   417	        # 腿一：commit SHA 不符 → C2 证据绑定违规 → 整线 BLOCKED
   418	        agg = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="staging")
   419	        agg.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", "c" * 40, "staging"))
   420	        verdict = agg.aggregate()
   421	        _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED, "SHA 不符必须整线 BLOCKED")
   422	        _ok("sha_evidence_violation" in verdict["reasons"][0], "须记 sha_evidence_violation")
   423	        # 腿二：environment 不符 → C5 → BLOCKED
   424	        agg2 = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="staging")
   425	        agg2.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", sha, "production"))
   426	        verdict2 = agg2.aggregate()
   427	        _ok(verdict2["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED, "环境不符必须整线 BLOCKED")
   428	        _ok(any("environment_evidence_violation" in r for r in verdict2["reasons"]),
   429	            "须记 environment_evidence_violation")
   430	        # 腿三：ruleset/scope 变化 → 冻结件不可复用（不得静默换规则继续跑）
   431	        now = datetime.now(_UTC)
   432	        m_ruleset_a = _freeze_manifest(now, ruleset={"version": 1})
   433	        path = td / "run-manifest.json"
   434	        m_ruleset_a.save(path)
   435	        m_ruleset_b = _freeze_manifest(now, ruleset={"version": 2})
   436	        exc = _expect_raise(bo.ManifestFreezeError, m_ruleset_b.save, path)
   437	        _ok("DIFFERENT frozen manifest" in str(exc), "ruleset 不符的冻结件不得覆写复用")
   438	        m_scope_b = _freeze_manifest(now, scope=["tests/**"])
   439	        exc2 = _expect_raise(bo.ManifestFreezeError, m_scope_b.save, path)
   440	        _ok("DIFFERENT frozen manifest" in str(exc2), "scope 不符的冻结件不得覆写复用")
   441

   443	def test_GATE_08_evidence_past_ttl_refused(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   444	    """GATE-08｜注入：evidence 过 TTL｜期望：BLOCKED（拒绝消费）。"""
   445	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   446	        now = datetime.now(_UTC)
   447	        manifest = _freeze_manifest(now)
   448	        adapter = bo.SourceGateAdapter(manifest, now=now)
   449	        ttl_days = bo.default_registry().ttl_for(bo.SourceGateAdapter.STATION_ID)
   450	        _ok(ttl_days == 30, "站1 源闸 TTL 应为 general 30 天上限")
   451	        # 对照组：TTL 内的证据可正常适配为 PASS
   452	        fresh = adapter.adapt(_gate_result(manifest.commit_sha, now - timedelta(days=1)))
   453	        _ok(fresh["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", "TTL 内证据应正常消费（对照）")
   454	        # 注入：ended_at + TTL < now → EvidenceStaleError（铁律 4 fail-closed）
   455	        stale_gate = _gate_result(manifest.commit_sha, now - timedelta(days=ttl_days + 1))
   456	        exc = _expect_raise(bo.EvidenceStaleError, adapter.adapt, stale_gate)
   457	        _ok("aged out" in str(exc) and "TTL" in str(exc), "过期证据报错须点名 TTL 时效")

   460	def test_GATE_12_unsigned_flip_or_adjudication_blocked(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   461	    """GATE-12｜注入：unsigned flip/adjudication｜期望：BLOCKED（拒绝）。"""
   462	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   463	        # 腿一：未带内部 seal 的控制事件（终态翻转/审批裁定）裸 append → 一律 ValueError
   464	        store = EventStore(str(td / "events.db"))
   465	        for etype in ("RUN_COMPLETED", "APPROVAL_GRANTED", "FINDING_CLOSED", "SUPERSEDED"):
   466	            unsigned = {"event_type": etype, "run_id": "r1", "verdict": "PASS"}
   467	            exc = _expect_raise(ValueError, store.append, unsigned)
   468	            _ok("must be injected via RunManager._emit" in str(exc),
   469	                f"unsigned flip/adjudication（{etype}）必须被 seal 守卫拒绝")
   470	        _, inserted = store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "note": "ordinary"})
   471	        _ok(inserted, "普通事件不受影响（对照组）")
   472	        store.close()
   473	        # 腿二：审批 envelope 无签名/伪签名 → 验签失败（fail-closed）
   474	        ring = ApprovalKeyring.generate("key_g12")
   475	        envelope = {"key_id": "key_g12", "decision": "APPROVE", "scope": "merge"}
   476	        good_sig = sign_envelope(ring.get("key_g12"), envelope)
   477	        _ok(ring.verify({**envelope, "signature": good_sig}), "正确签名应通过（对照）")
   478	        _ok(ring.verify(envelope) is False, "无签名 envelope 必须验签失败")
   479	        _ok(ring.verify({**envelope, "signature": "0" * 64}) is False, "伪签名必须验签失败")
   480	        tampered = {**envelope, "decision": "APPROVE_ALL"}
   481	        _ok(ring.verify({**tampered, "signature": good_sig}) is False,
   482	            "签名后篡改 payload 必须验签失败")
   483

   488	def test_MRG_01_fast_green_security_red_no_merge(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   489	    """MRG-01｜注入：Fast 绿、Security 红｜期望：不合流。"""
   490	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   491	        sha = "a" * 40
   492	        agg = GateAggregator(required_stations={"1", "2"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   493	        agg.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", sha))   # Fast（站1 源闸）绿

   497	        _ok(any("station_hard_failure" in r for r in verdict["reasons"]), "须记 station_hard_failure")

   501	            "GitHub 双口径必须是 failure（不合流）")

   503	        proc = subprocess.run(
   504	            ["bash", str(AUTO_MERGE_STUB), "ignored"],
   505	            capture_output=True, text=True, check=False,
   506	        )
   507	        _ok(proc.returncode == 1 and "permanently disabled" in proc.stderr,
   508	            "auto-merge stub 必须拒绝执行（不合流）")

   530	        sha = "a" * 40
   531	        # 腿一：旧 SHA 的 check 证据 → C2 绑定违规 → BLOCKED

   550	def test_MRG_04_pending_skipped_neutral_cancelled_no_merge(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   551	    """MRG-04｜注入：pending/skipped/neutral/cancelled｜期望：不合流。"""
   552	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   553	        sha = "a" * 40
   554	        # legacy outcome 归一：非绿非软枚举（PENDING/SKIPPED/NEUTRAL/CANCELLED）一律硬失败
   555	        for bad_outcome in ("PENDING", "SKIPPED", "NEUTRAL", "CANCELLED"):
   556	            agg = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   557	            agg.add(_legacy("1", bad_outcome, sha))
   558	            verdict = agg.aggregate()
   559	            _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED,
   560	                f"{bad_outcome} 不得视作绿/软——必须整线 BLOCKED")
   561	            _ok(agg.to_github_status(verdict)["conclusion"] == "failure",
   562	                f"{bad_outcome} 的 GitHub 结论必须 failure")
   563	        # v2 契约腿：execution_status=CANCELLED（check run 被取消）→ 铁律2 硬失败
   564	        agg2 = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   565	        agg2.add({
   566	            "schema_version": "2.0",
   567	            "station_id": 1,
   568	            "execution_status": "CANCELLED",
   569	            "policy_verdict": "NOT_APPLICABLE",
   570	            "identity": {"commit_sha": sha, "environment": "test"},
   571	        })
   572	        verdict2 = agg2.aggregate()
   573	        _ok(verdict2["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED, "v2 CANCELLED 必须硬失败")
   574	        _ok(verdict2["stations"]["1"]["outcome"] == BLOCKED, "站级 outcome 须归一为 BLOCKED")

   577	def test_MRG_05_head_moves_during_aggregation_invalidates(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   578	    """MRG-05｜注入：head 在聚合后改变｜期望：旧结论失效。"""
   579	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   580	        repo = _make_repo(td)
   581	        # 注入体：『测试命令』本身就是一次 git commit——内层全绿（exit 0）但 head 移动
   582	        gate = SourceGate(
   583	            str(repo),
   584	            full_test_commands=[["git", "commit", "--allow-empty", "-m", "head-moved-mid-run"]],
   585	            result_path=str(td / "m05.json"),
   586	        )
   587	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   588	        _ok(result["checks"] and result["checks"][0]["exit_code"] == 0,
   589	            "内层命令真实退出码为 0（若无 head 复核将产生假绿）")
   590	        _ok(result["head_stable"] is False, "head 稳定性复核必须判 False")
   591	        _ok(result["head_sha"] != result["head_sha_final"], "起跑前后 head SHA 必须不同")
   592	        _ok(result["error"]["type"] == "StaleShaError", "必须以 StaleShaError 失效本次全部证据")
   593	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_ERROR and result["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED",
   594	            "旧结论失效：exit 2 + NOT_EVALUATED，绝不得记 PASS")

   597	def test_MRG_06_remote_429_empty_json_timeout_circuit_breaker(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   598	    """MRG-06｜注入：GitHub 429/空 JSON/超时｜期望：断路，不合流。"""
   599	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   600	        # 腿一：上游超时（远端不可达/限流的本地等价）→ TIMEOUT(124) 断路
   601	        repo = _make_repo(td, behavioral_commit=False)
   602	        gate = SourceGate(
   603	            str(repo), git_timeout=0.00001, result_path=str(td / "m06.json")
   604	        )
   605	        result = gate.execute_gate()
   606	        _ok(result["exit_code"] == EXIT_TIMEOUT, f"超时须断路 exit 124，got {result['exit_code']}")
   607	        _ok(result["execution_status"] == "TIMEOUT", "execution_status 须 TIMEOUT")
   608	        # 腿二：空 JSON 载荷 → 适配器 fail-closed 拒绝（绝不猜）
   609	        now = datetime.now(_UTC)
   610	        adapter = bo.SourceGateAdapter(_freeze_manifest(now), now=now)
   611	        exc = _expect_raise(bo.GateResultMalformed, adapter.adapt, {})
   612	        _ok("missing fields" in str(exc), "空 JSON 必须报 GateResultMalformed")
   613	        # 腿三：聚合层收到空 JSON/非对象 → malformed → BLOCKED（不合流）
   614	        agg = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha="a" * 40, environment="test")
   615	        agg.add({})
   616	        verdict = agg.aggregate()
   617	        _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED and verdict["counts"]["malformed"] == 1,
   618	            "空 JSON 站结果必须记 malformed 并整线 BLOCKED")
   619	        _ok(agg.to_github_status(verdict)["conclusion"] == "failure", "GitHub 结论必须 failure")

   622	def test_MRG_07_head_changed_after_final_validation_atomic_reject(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   623	    """MRG-07｜注入：最后校验后 head 变化｜期望：expected-SHA 原子拒绝。"""
   624	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   625	        repo = _make_repo(td)
   626	        gate = SourceGate(str(repo))
   627	        head_now = _head(repo)
   628	        parent = _git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD~1").stdout.strip().lower()
   629	        _ok(gate.validate_exact_sha(head_now) == head_now, "当前 head 精确匹配应放行（对照）")
   630	        # 最后校验后 head 又前进：旧校验结论（parent 的绿灯）必须被原子拒绝
   631	        exc = _expect_raise(StaleShaError, gate.validate_exact_sha, parent)
   632	        _ok(exc.exit_code == EXIT_ERROR, "StaleShaError 必须 ERROR 传播")
   633	        _ok(exc.detail.get("check_sha") == parent and exc.detail.get("head_sha") == head_now,
   634	            "拒绝证据须同时落 check_sha 与当前 head_sha（expected-SHA 原子比对）")
   635	        # head 再变一次：上一轮刚验过的 head 也立刻失效
   636	        _git(repo, "commit", "--allow-empty", "-m", "moves-again")
   637	        exc2 = _expect_raise(StaleShaError, gate.validate_exact_sha, head_now)
   638	        _ok(exc2.detail.get("head_sha") == _head(repo), "复核必须取现场重解的 fresh head")
   639

   644	def test_CI_05_fork_merge_group_or_missing_check_explicit_failure(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   645	    """CI-05｜注入：fork、merge_group、check 不产生｜期望：明确 FAILURE/BLOCKED。"""
   646	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   647	        # 腿一：check 不产生（required 站零上报）→ 明确 BLOCKED，绝不静默通过
   648	        agg = GateAggregator(required_stations={"1", "2"}, target_sha="a" * 40, environment="test")

   651	        _ok("denominator_shrinkage" in verdict["reasons"][0], "须点名 denominator_shrinkage")
   652	        _ok(verdict["counts"]["required_missing"] == 2, "缺报数须如实=2")
   653	        _ok(agg.to_github_status(verdict)["conclusion"] == "failure", "必须发布明确 failure")
   654	        # 腿二/三：fork PR head SHA、merge_group 临时 SHA ≠ 目标 head → 原子拒绝
   655	        repo = _make_repo(td, behavioral_commit=False)
   656	        gate = SourceGate(str(repo))

   658	        fork_sha = ("deadc0de" * 5)[:40]
   659	        merge_group_sha = ("f00dcafe" * 5)[:40]
   660	        for label, foreign in (("fork", fork_sha), ("merge_group", merge_group_sha)):
   661	            exc = _expect_raise(StaleShaError, gate.validate_exact_sha, foreign)
   662	            _ok(exc.detail.get("head_sha") == head,
   663	                f"{label} 的异源 SHA 必须被拒，且证据绑定真实 head")

   692	    """CI-08｜注入：Wenqu Gate 缺失但 Bugscan 绿｜期望：不合流。"""
   693	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   694	        sha = "a" * 40
   695	        # 站1=Wenqu Gate（源闸）、站2=Bugscan（静态与供应链）：仅站2 上报且绿
   696	        agg = GateAggregator(required_stations={"1", "2"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   697	        agg.add(_legacy("2", "PASS", sha))
   698	        verdict = agg.aggregate()
   699	        _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED,
   700	            "Wenqu Gate 缺失时 Bugscan 再绿也不得合流（C3 分母缩水）")
   701	        _ok("missing required stations [1]" in verdict["reasons"][0],
   702	            "须点名缺失的 required 站=站1")
   703	        _ok(verdict["counts"]["required_missing"] == 1, "缺报数须=1（仅站1）")
   704	        status = agg.to_github_status(verdict)
   705	        _ok(status["state"] == "failure" and status["conclusion"] == "failure",
   706	            "GitHub 状态必须 failure（不合流）")
   707	
   708	
   709	def test_CI_09_recursive_aggregate_or_duplicate_publish_rejected(root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   710	    """CI-09｜注入：S7 把 Wenqu Required Gate 当自身输入、重复发布或递归聚合｜期望：schema/DAG 校验拒绝并发布 FAILURE。"""
   711	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   712	        ts = "2026-10-08T00:00:00+00:00"
   713	        # 腿一：schema 拒绝——station-result 携带非法输入引用字段（S7 吃 Required Gate）
   714	        good = bo.build_station_result(
   715	            run_id="ci09run",
   716	            station_id=7,
   717	            attempt_id="att-ci09",
   718	            execution_status="COMPLETED",
   719	            policy_verdict="PASS",
   720	            identity={
   721	                "project_id": "gate-family",
   722	                "commit_sha": "1" * 40,
   723	                "environment": "local",
   724	                "scope_hash": "2" * 64,
   725	                "ruleset_hash": "3" * 64,
   726	                "data_config_hash": "4" * 64,
   727	            },
   728	            tool={"name": "runtime-sentinel", "version": "1"},
   729	            execution={
   730	                "argv_digest": "5" * 64,
   731	                "started_at": ts,
   732	                "ended_at": ts,
   733	                "timeout_s": 60,
   734	                "actual_exit_code": 0,
   735	                "expected_exit_set": [0],
   736	                "assertion_verdict": "PASS",
   737	            },
   738	            coverage={"denominator": 3, "scanned": 3},
   739	            finding_ids=[],
   740	            artifacts=[{"cas_digest": "sha256:" + "6" * 64, "size": 16}],
   741	        )
   742	        bo.validate_station_result(good)  # 对照：合法结果通过
   743	        recursive = dict(good)
   744	        recursive["consumes_wenqu_required_gate"] = True  # S7 递归引用注入
   745	        exc = _expect_raise(bo.StationResultValidationError, bo.validate_station_result, recursive)
   746	        _ok("additional properties" in str(exc), "schema 必须拒绝非法输入引用（additionalProperties=false）")
   747	        # 腿二：递归聚合——聚合输出回灌聚合器（无 station 结构）→ malformed BLOCKED
   748	        sha = "a" * 40
   749	        agg_inner = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   750	        agg_inner.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", sha))
   751	        aggregate_output = agg_inner.aggregate()
   752	        agg_outer = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   753	        agg_outer.add(aggregate_output)  # DAG 违规：聚合结果不是站点结果
   754	        verdict = agg_outer.aggregate()
   755	        _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED and verdict["counts"]["malformed"] == 1,
   756	            "聚合输出不得作为站点结果再入聚合（DAG 校验拒绝）")
   757	        _ok(agg_outer.to_github_status(verdict)["conclusion"] == "failure",
   758	            "拒绝时必须发布 failure 而非静默")
   759	        # 腿三：重复发布不同内容（同站名异证据）→ 冲突 BLOCKED；幂等重发不产生第二绿
   760	        agg_dup = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=sha, environment="test")
   761	        agg_dup.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", sha))
   762	        agg_dup.add(_legacy("1", "PASS", sha))          # 幂等重发：忽略，不双绿
   763	        agg_dup.add(_legacy("1", "FAIL", sha))          # 异内容重发：冲突
   764	        verdict_dup = agg_dup.aggregate()
   765	        _ok(verdict_dup["counts"]["duplicates_ignored"] == 1, "幂等重发只记 duplicate")
   766	        _ok(verdict_dup["aggregate_outcome"] == BLOCKED and verdict_dup["counts"]["conflicts"] == 1,
   767	            "异内容重复发布必须冲突 BLOCKED")
   768	
   769

   771	# 运行器
   772	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   773	_TEST_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^test_(GATE|MRG|CI)_(\d{2})_")
   774	
   775	# 不可实现清单（产品未实现该语义，不硬凑；详见模块 docstring 与交付报告）
   776	NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE: Dict[str, str] = {
   777	    "GATE-09": "N=-1 已被 tests/acceptance.sh（--convergence -1 拒绝）覆盖；"
   778	               "N=0 在 cli/wenqu 是合法『关闭收敛要求』开关而非参数错误、N 超上限无上限校验——注入面后两态无产品语义",
   779	    "GATE-10": "超过 N+1 轮 BLOCKED 等人工裁定：仓内无可执行的轮数上限/人工裁定实现（仅 dashboard 文案）",
   780	    "GATE-11": "同命令换 S 标签不计异源轮：无可执行的异源轮来源追踪/命令身份判定实现",
   781	    "CI-06": "CODEOWNERS 存在但 ruleset 未强制 → doctor ERROR：hardening-doctor.sh 体检 launchd/哨兵/CI 卡死，"
   782	             "无 CODEOWNERS/ruleset 校验语义",
   783	}
   784	
   785	
   786	def _collect_tests() -> List[Tuple[str, Callable[..., None]]]:
   787	    return [
   788	        (name, fn)
   789	        for name, fn in sorted(globals().items())
   790	        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn) and _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name)
   791	    ]
   792	
   793	
   794	def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
   795	    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
   796	    json_out: Optional[str] = None
   797	    if "--json" in argv:
   798	        json_out = argv[argv.index("--json") + 1]
   799	    tests = _collect_tests()
   800	    print("=" * 72)
   801	    print("§20 GATE/MRG/CI 族专属验收测试（实现 20 条；跳过 4 条见 NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE）")
   802	    for tid, reason in NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE.items():
   803	        print(f"  SKIP {tid}: {reason}")
   804	    print("-" * 72)
   805	    records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   806	    failed: List[str] = []
   807	    for name, fn in tests:
   808	        match = _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name)
   809	        assert match is not None
   810	        test_id = f"{match.group(1)}-{match.group(2)}"
   811	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ac-gate-family-") as td:
   812	            try:
   813	                fn(Path(td))
   814	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": True})
   815	                print(f"  PASS {test_id}  {name}")
   816	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   817	                failed.append(name)
   818	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": False})
   819	                print(f"  FAIL {test_id}  {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
   820	    print("-" * 72)
   821	    print(f"结果: {len(records) - len(failed)}/{len(records)} passed"
   822	          f"{'' if not failed else '；失败: ' + ', '.join(failed)}")
   823	    if json_out is not None:
   824	        out_path = Path(json_out)
   825	        out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   826	        out_path.write_text(
   827	            json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
   828	        )
   829	        print(f"逐 ID 结果已写: {out_path}")
   830	    print("=" * 72)
   831	    return 0 if not failed else 1
   832	
   833	
   834	if __name__ == "__main__":
   835	    raise SystemExit(main())

     6	共 16 条：每条按方案表格「注入/期望」逐字对齐，真实注入（真实 SQLite 临时
     7	事件库 + approval-v2 全信封 + fixtures/approval-keys.json 测试密钥的真实
     8	HMAC-SHA256 签发）与真实断言（消费/拒绝/事件流/终态），绝不 xfail 硬凑。
     9	
    10	被测正源（不改动任何产品码）：
    11	- system/wenqu_core/wenqu_pipeline.py   ApprovalBroker（结构封闭+HMAC 验签
    12	  +envelope 全字段 exact 绑定+TTL+state_version CAS+nonce 事件正源一次性

    14	  complete_run/register_finding/reserve_action/start_action/commit_action/
    15	  fail_action/reconcile_receipt）、ActionSaga（授权消费链+receipt 分级）
    16	- system/wenqu_core/approval_keys.py    ApprovalKeyring/sign_envelope
    17	  （key 撤销=移出 keyring 即未知 key 拒绝）
    18	- system/wenqu_core/gate_aggregator.py  technical_eligible 语义（CONDITIONAL
    19	  永不上绿；COND-01/03/04 的聚合层半边）
    20	- system/schemas/finding-event-v2.schema.json
    21	  RISK_REVOKED/RISK_INVALIDATED 转移表（RISK-01 的 REVOKED/INVALIDATED +
    22	  立即重开 + 修复链入口半边；经 jsonschema Draft7 真实校验冻结 schema）
    23	
    24	语义映射边界（诚实声明，非硬凑）：
    25	- AUTH-06「无 user-presence」：approval-v2 无独立 user-presence 位（如硬件
    26	  token UV），产品最接近语义是 decision!=approve（非明确用户批准决定，
    27	  conditional/deny 不足以消费）→ 一律拒。
    28	- COND-01「技术 context failure」/COND-03「success/AUTHORIZED_CONDITIONAL」：
    29	  产品落地面为 gate_aggregator（technical_eligible=False + GitHub failure；
    30	  CONDITIONAL 绝不上绿）+ wenqu_pipeline complete_run（无 risk 授权 fail-closed
    31	  拒绝 / 有精确授权才 COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL 且 verdict 仍 CONDITIONAL）。
    32	- RISK-01「追加 REVOKED/INVALIDATED」：RunManager 层无主动撤销 API；撤销/
    33	  失效转移表冻结于 finding-event-v2.schema.json（RISK_REVOKED←ACCEPTED_RISK、
    34	  RISK_INVALIDATED←ACCEPTED_RISK→OPEN 立即重开、FIX_STARTED←OPEN 修复链），
    35	  以 jsonschema 对冻结 schema 的真实校验断言；管线半边（finding 面改变 →
    36	  已消费授权在 complete_run 判定点失效、run 保持可修复）同步实证。
    37	- 方案 §20 STOP 行注（L330）：STOP-01~03 须逐类型验证九类全覆盖「错误类型/
    38	  旧版本/重放审批仍拒」「有授权但客观条件未恢复仍拒」「客观条件已恢复但无
    39	  授权仍拒」——本文件逐字执行。
    40

   143	def _ok(cond: bool, message: str) -> None:
   144	    if not cond:
   145	        raise AssertionError(message)
   146	
   147	
   148	def _raises(exc_type: type, fn: Callable[[], Any], *,
   149	            contains: Optional[str] = None) -> Exception:
   150	    """执行 fn 并断言抛出 exc_type（可选断言错误文本包含 contains）。"""
   151	    try:
   152	        fn()
   153	    except exc_type as exc:  # type: ignore[misc]
   154	        if contains is not None and contains not in str(exc):
   155	            raise AssertionError(
   156	                f"期望 {exc_type.__name__} 含 {contains!r}，实得: {exc}") from exc
   157	        return exc
   158	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   159	        raise AssertionError(
   160	            f"期望 {exc_type.__name__}，实得 {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}") from exc
   161	    raise AssertionError(f"期望 {exc_type.__name__}，但调用未抛错")
   162

   164	@contextlib.contextmanager
   165	def _sandbox(tag: str):
   166	    """每用例独立临时目录（真实 SQLite 文件库，拒绝 :memory:）。"""
   167	    td = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f"ac-auth-cond-{tag}-")
   168	    try:
   169	        yield Path(td)
   170	    finally:
   171	        shutil.rmtree(td, ignore_errors=True)

   174	def _iso(epoch: float) -> str:
   175	    return time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime(epoch))

   178	def _mgr(td: Path, name: str = "events.db", *, keyring: ApprovalKeyring = KEYRING,
   179	         store: Optional[EventStore] = None) -> Tuple[EventStore, RunManager]:
   180	    st = store if store is not None else EventStore(str(td / name))
   181	    return st, RunManager(st, keyring=keyring)

   183	
   184	def _sign(approval: Dict[str, Any], *, secret: str = SECRET_A,
   185	          key_id: str = KEY_A) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   186	    """对 envelope 重算 HMAC 签名（覆盖旧 signature；返回深拷贝）。"""
   187	    ap = copy.deepcopy(approval)
   188	    ap["key_id"] = key_id
   189	    ap.pop("signature", None)
   190	    ap["signature"] = sign_envelope(secret, ap)
   191	    return ap
   192	
   193	
   194	def _base_envelope(*, approval_type: str, run_id: str, task_id: str,
   195	                   stop_type: str, stage: str, version: int,
   196	                   environment: str = ID_STAGING["environment"],
   197	                   scope: str = ID_STAGING["scope_hash"],
   198	                   policy: str = ID_STAGING["policy_hash"],
   199	                   ruleset: str = ID_STAGING["ruleset_hash"],
   200	                   watermark: str = ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   201	                   stop_event_id: str = "n/a-not-a-waiting-anchor",
   202	                   actor: str = "ac-auth-cond-signer",
   203	                   issued_at: str = "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z",
   204	                   expires_at: str = "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z",
   205	                   nonce: Optional[str] = None,
   206	                   decision: str = "approve",
   207	                   key_id: str = KEY_A,
   208	                   payload: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   209	    """approval-v2 全信封（_APPROVAL_REQUIRED_TOP 逐字段；未签名的基底）。"""
   210	    return {
   211	        "schema_version": "2.0",
   212	        "approval_id": f"apr_ac_{approval_type}_{os.urandom(4).hex()}",
   213	        "approval_type": approval_type,
   214	        "key_id": key_id,
   215	        "run_id": run_id,
   216	        "task_id": task_id,
   217	        "stop_event_id": stop_event_id,
   218	        "stop_type": stop_type,
   219	        "stage": stage,
   220	        "environment": environment,
   221	        "authorized_scope": scope,
   222	        "policy_hash": policy,
   223	        "ruleset_hash": ruleset,
   224	        "input_watermark": watermark,
   225	        "expected_state_version": version,
   226	        "actor": actor,
   227	        "issued_at": issued_at,
   228	        "expires_at": expires_at,
   229	        "nonce": nonce or f"nonce-ac-{approval_type}-{os.urandom(8).hex()}",
   230	        "decision": decision,
   231	        "signature": "",
   232	        "payload": payload if payload is not None else {},
   233	    }
   234	
   235	
   236	def _resume_approval(run_id: str, task_id: str, *, stop_event_id: str,
   237	                     stop_type: str, version: int,
   238	                     preconditions: Dict[str, Any],
   239	                     stage: str = "S1_REQUIREMENT", **over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   240	    """resume 类审批：payload 绑定客观前置条件内容哈希。"""
   241	    ap = _base_envelope(
   242	        approval_type="resume", run_id=run_id, task_id=task_id,
   243	        stop_type=stop_type, stage=stage, version=version,
   244	        stop_event_id=stop_event_id,
   245	        payload={
   246	            "stop_event_id": stop_event_id,
   247	            "stop_type": stop_type,
   248	            "objective_precondition_hash":
   249	                SM.objective_precondition_hash(preconditions),
   250	        })
   251	    for k, v in over.items():
   252	        ap[k] = v
   253	    return _sign(ap)
   254	
   255	
   256	def _risk_approval(run_id: str, task_id: str, *, version: int,
   257	                   fingerprints: List[str], severity: str = "MEDIUM",
   258	                   stage: str = "S7_GATE", **over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   259	    """risk 类审批（COND 族/AUTH 族共用基底；over 覆盖任意信封字段）。"""
   260	    ap = _base_envelope(
   261	        approval_type="risk", run_id=run_id, task_id=task_id,
   262	        stop_type="scope", stage=stage, version=version,
   263	        payload={
   264	            "finding_fingerprints": list(fingerprints),
   265	            "severity": severity,
   266	            "reason": "ac-auth-cond 风险接受理由-低风险可接受项",
   267	            "compensating_controls": ["ctrl-ac-1", "ctrl-ac-2"],
   268	            "expiry": "2099-12-31T00:00:00Z",
   269	        })
   270	    for k, v in over.items():
   271	        ap[k] = v
   272	    return _sign(ap)
   273	
   274	
   275	def _action_approval(run_id: str, task_id: str, *, version: int,
   276	                     stage: str = "S1_REQUIREMENT",
   277	                     **over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   278	    """prod_write 类 action 审批（reserve_action 必传的授权）。"""
   279	    ap = _base_envelope(
   280	        approval_type="prod_write", run_id=run_id, task_id=task_id,
   281	        stop_type="prod-write", stage=stage, version=version,
   282	        payload=dict(PROD_WRITE_PAYLOAD))
   283	    for k, v in over.items():
   284	        ap[k] = v
   285	    return _sign(ap)
   286	
   287	
   288	def _external_receipt(reserved: Dict[str, Any], run_id: str,
   289	                      *, watermark: str = ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   290	                      outcome: str = "COMMITTED",
   291	                      external_ref: str = "ext-ac-action-77",
   292	                      **over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   293	    """action-execution-receipt v1.0 外部正源回执（测试密钥 HMAC 签发）。"""
   294	    rc: Dict[str, Any] = {
   295	        "schema_version": "1.0",
   296	        "receipt_type": "action-execution-receipt",
   297	        "action_id": reserved["action_id"],
   298	        "run_id": run_id,
   299	        "action_type": reserved["action_type"],
   300	        "action_target": reserved["action_target"],
   301	        "watermark": watermark,
   302	        "outcome": outcome,
   303	        "external_ref": external_ref,
   304	        "ts": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z",
   305	        "key_id": KEY_A,
   306	        "signature": "",
   307	    }
   308	    for k, v in over.items():
   309	        rc[k] = v
   310	    rc.pop("signature", None)
   311	    rc["signature"] = sign_envelope(SECRET_A, rc)
   312	    return rc
   313	
   314	
   315	def _waiting_run(mgr: RunManager, task_id: str, stop_type: str,
   316	                 ident: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
   317	    """创建 run → 启动 S1 → 触发指定类型停等（返回 run_id 与停等锚点）。"""
   318	    run_id = mgr.create_run(task_id, ident)["run_id"]
   319	    mgr.advance_stage(run_id)
   320	    w = mgr.raise_waiting(run_id, stop_type, f"ac-auth-cond 停等注入-{stop_type}")
   321	    return run_id, w
   322	
   323	
   324	def _soft_terminal_run(mgr: RunManager, task_id: str,
   325	                       ident: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   326	    """S1..S6 PASS、S7 CONDITIONAL（软终态）——COND 族的『CONDITIONAL run』。"""
   327	    run_id = mgr.create_run(task_id, ident)["run_id"]
   328	    for _ in range(6):
   329	        mgr.advance_stage(run_id, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   330	                          policy_verdict="PASS")
   331	    mgr.advance_stage(run_id, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   332	                      policy_verdict="CONDITIONAL")
   333	    return run_id
   334	
   335	
   336	def _count_events(store: EventStore, run_id: str, event_type: str) -> int:
   337	    n = 0
   338	    for (_seq, _h, payload) in store._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001
   339	            "SELECT seq, event_hash, payload FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq ASC"):
   340	        ev = json.loads(payload)
   341	        if ev.get("run_id") == run_id and ev.get("event_type") == event_type:
   342	            n += 1
   343	    return n
   344	
   345	
   346	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   347	# AUTH 族（§20.1 AUTH-01~03 + §20.5 AUTH-04~06）
   348	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   349	def test_AUTH_01_resume_without_authorization_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   350	    """AUTH-01｜注入：无授权 resume｜期望：拒绝。"""
   351	    with _sandbox("auth01") as td:
   352	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   353	        run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, "task_auth01", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   354	        # 腿一：WAITING 中绕过授权直接推进 —— advance_stage 必拒并指向 resume
   355	        exc = _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.advance_stage(run_id),
   356	                      contains="resume_waiting")
   357	        _ok("WAITING" in str(exc), "拒绝理由须点名 WAITING 态")
   358	        # 腿二：无任何审批（approval=None）调 resume —— 结构层即拒
   359	        _raises(ApprovalRejected,
   360	                lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, None, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]),
   361	                contains="not a JSON object")
   362	        # 腿三：攻击者自造信封（无登记密钥的伪签发）—— 未知 key 拒
   363	        rogue = _base_envelope(
   364	            approval_type="resume", run_id=run_id, task_id="task_auth01",
   365	            stop_type="prod-write", stage="S1_REQUIREMENT",
   366	            version=mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version,
   367	            stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"], key_id="key_ai_forged_1",
   368	            payload={"stop_event_id": w["stop_event_id"], "stop_type": "prod-write",
   369	                     "objective_precondition_hash":
   370	                         SM.objective_precondition_hash(VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"])})
   371	        forged = _sign(rogue, secret=os.urandom(32).hex(), key_id="key_ai_forged_1")
   372	        _raises(ApprovalRejected,
   373	                lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, forged, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]),
   374	                contains="key_id")
   375	        # 期望核对：三路注入全部拒绝后 run 仍 WAITING、停等锚点未变、零消费
   376	        st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   377	        _ok(st.state == "WAITING" and st.waiting is not None, "无授权不得离开 WAITING")
   378	        _ok(st.waiting.stop_event_id == w["stop_event_id"], "停等锚点不可被扰动")
   379	        _ok(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id) == [], "零授权消费")
   380	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   381	        store.close()
   382	
   383	
   384	def test_AUTH_02_expired_replay_scope_widening_stale_version_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   385	    """AUTH-02｜注入：过期/重放/扩范围/旧 state_version｜期望：拒绝。"""
   386	    with _sandbox("auth02") as td:
   387	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   388	        now = time.time()
   389	
   390	        # 腿一：过期（issued/expires 均已过去，结构合法）→ TTL 窗口外拒
   391	        run1, w1 = _waiting_run(mgr, "task_auth02a", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   392	        v1 = mgr.get_run(run1).state_version
   393	        expired = _resume_approval(
   394	            run1, "task_auth02a", stop_event_id=w1["stop_event_id"],
   395	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v1,
   396	            preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"],
   397	            issued_at="2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", expires_at="2026-01-02T00:00:00Z")
   398	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   399	            run1, expired, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]),
   400	            contains="TTL")
   401	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run1).state == "WAITING", "过期审批不得恢复 run")
   402	
   403	        # 腿二：重放（合法消费一次后同 nonce 再消费）→ 一次性消费拒
   404	        ok = _resume_approval(
   405	            run1, "task_auth02a", stop_event_id=w1["stop_event_id"],
   406	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v1,
   407	            preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"],
   408	            issued_at=_iso(now - 60))
   409	        _ok(mgr.resume_waiting(run1, ok, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"])["resumed"],
   410	            "对照组：合法授权恢复成功（fail-closed 非 fail-everything）")
   411	        _raises(ApprovalReuseError, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   412	            run1, ok, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]), contains="nonce")
   413	
   414	        # 腿三：扩范围（authorized_scope 超出 run scope_hash，重签有效）→ 拒
   415	        run2, w2 = _waiting_run(mgr, "task_auth02b", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   416	        v2 = mgr.get_run(run2).state_version
   417	        widened = _resume_approval(
   418	            run2, "task_auth02b", stop_event_id=w2["stop_event_id"],
   419	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v2,
   420	            preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"],
   421	            authorized_scope="scope-ac-001-plus-extra")
   422	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   423	            run2, widened, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]),
   424	            contains="authorized_scope")
   425	
   426	        # 腿四：旧 state_version（v-1 与 v+1 各一，重签有效）→ CAS 拒
   427	        for stale in (v2 - 1, v2 + 1):
   428	            ap = _resume_approval(
   429	                run2, "task_auth02b", stop_event_id=w2["stop_event_id"],
   430	                stop_type="prod-write", version=stale,
   431	                preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"])
   432	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda a=ap: mgr.resume_waiting(
   433	                run2, a, VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]),
   434	                contains="state_version")
   435	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run2).state == "WAITING", "扩范围/旧版本注入后仍 WAITING")
   436	        _ok(len(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run2)) == 0, "腿三/四零消费")
   437	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   438	        store.close()
   439	
   440	
   441	def test_AUTH_03_ai_self_issued_high_risk_approval_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   442	    """AUTH-03｜注入：AI/runner 尝试自行签发高风险批准｜期望：拒绝。"""
   443	    with _sandbox("auth03") as td:
   444	        # 腿一：AI 自建密钥自签（结构/HMAC 全合法但 key 未登记）→ 未知 key 拒
   445	        store1, mgr1 = _mgr(td, "a.db")
   446	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr1, "task_auth03a", ID_STAGING)
   447	        mgr1.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_auth03_1", "fp-auth03", "HIGH", "P1")
   448	        v = mgr1.get_run(run_id).state_version
   449	        self_key = ApprovalKeyring.generate("key_ai_selfsign_1")
   450	        ap_self = _risk_approval(
   451	            run_id, "task_auth03a", version=v, fingerprints=["fp-auth03"],
   452	            severity="HIGH", actor="ai-runner-selfissued")
   453	        ap_self = _sign(ap_self, secret=self_key.get("key_ai_selfsign_1"),
   454	                        key_id="key_ai_selfsign_1")
   455	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr1.authorize_risk(run_id, ap_self),
   456	                contains="key_id")
   457	        _ok(mgr1.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id) == [], "自签审批零消费")
   458	        store1.close()
   459	
   460	        # 腿二：即便拿到登记密钥，HIGH/CRITICAL 高风险内容仍机器拒绝（无人工豁免）
   461	        store2, mgr2 = _mgr(td, "b.db")
   462	        run2 = _soft_terminal_run(mgr2, "task_auth03b", ID_STAGING)
   463	        mgr2.register_finding(run2, "fnd_auth03_2", "fp-high-2", "HIGH", "P1")
   464	        v2 = mgr2.get_run(run2).state_version
   465	        for sev in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL"):
   466	            ap = _risk_approval(run2, "task_auth03b", version=v2,
   467	                                fingerprints=["fp-high-2"], severity=sev,
   468	                                actor="ai-runner-with-leaked-key")
   469	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda a=ap: mgr2.authorize_risk(run2, a),
   470	                    contains="不可风险接受")
   471	        _ok(mgr2.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run2) == [], "高风险内容零消费")
   472	        store2.close()
   473	
   474	        # 腿三：AI/runner 无审批直接预留高风险动作（prod-write）→ 必拒
   475	        store3, mgr3 = _mgr(td, "c.db")
   476	        run3 = mgr3.create_run("task_auth03c", ID_STAGING)["run_id"]
   477	        mgr3.advance_stage(run3)
   478	        _raises(ActionError,
   479	                lambda: mgr3.reserve_action(run3, "prod-write", "db://prod/erp#ai"),
   480	                contains="requires")
   481	        _ok(not mgr3.get_run(run3).actions, "零动作预留")
   482	        store3.close()
   483	
   484	        # 对照组：同一登记密钥签发的合法低风险授权可消费（证明拒的是内容/身份而非密钥存在性）
   485	        store4, mgr4 = _mgr(td, "d.db")
   486	        run4 = _soft_terminal_run(mgr4, "task_auth03d", ID_STAGING)
   487	        mgr4.register_finding(run4, "fnd_auth03_4", "fp-low-4", "LOW", "P3")
   488	        v4 = mgr4.get_run(run4).state_version
   489	        legal = _risk_approval(run4, "task_auth03d", version=v4,
   490	                               fingerprints=["fp-low-4"], severity="LOW")
   491	        _ok(mgr4.authorize_risk(run4, legal)["consumed"] is True, "对照组消费成功")
   492	        store4.close()
   493

   495	def test_AUTH_04_canonical_payload_unicode_audience_byte_change_fails(root: Path) -> None:
   496	    """AUTH-04｜注入：canonical payload/Unicode/audience 任一字节改变｜期望：验签失败。"""
   497	    with _sandbox("auth04") as td:
   498	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   499	        run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, "task_auth04", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   500	        v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   501	        pre = VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]
   502	        # 带 Unicode 的信封（actor/payload 均含 CJK）——canonical JSON 以
   503	        # ensure_ascii=False 原样签名，任何一字节改动都必须验签失败
   504	        base = _resume_approval(
   505	            run_id, "task_auth04", stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   506	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v, preconditions=pre,
   507	            actor="授权人-超哥①")
   508	        _ok(len(base["actor"]) > 4 and any(ord(c) > 0x2E80 for c in base["actor"]),
   509	            "对照组信封须真实携带 Unicode")
   510	
   511	        def tampered(**over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   512	            bad = copy.deepcopy(base)
   513	            for k, val in over.items():
   514	                bad[k] = val
   515	            return bad  # 不重签 —— 签名仍是对原 canonical 字节的 HMAC
   516	
   517	        # Unicode 一字符翻转（超→趫）：canonical 字节改变 → 验签失败
   518	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   519	            run_id, tampered(actor="授权人-趫哥①"), pre), contains="signature verification failed")
   520	        # audience 半边一：environment（受众环境）staging→production → 验签失败
   521	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   522	            run_id, tampered(environment="production"), pre), contains="signature verification failed")
   523	        # audience 半边二：authorized_scope 尾部加一字节 → 验签失败
   524	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   525	            run_id, tampered(authorized_scope=ID_STAGING["scope_hash"] + "X"), pre),
   526	            contains="signature verification failed")
   527	        # canonical 载荷字节：expires_at 一位数字改动（仍合法 date-time）→ 验签失败
   528	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   529	            run_id, tampered(expires_at="2098-01-01T00:00:00Z"), pre),
   530	            contains="signature verification failed")
   531	        # 键序/规范化漂移面：整信封重序列化不改值（canonical 化等价）→ 不属于字节改变，应仍可消费
   532	        _ok(mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, copy.deepcopy(base), pre)["resumed"] is True,
   533	            "对照组：未篡改的 Unicode 信封原样消费成功")
   534	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   535	        store.close()
   536	
   537	
   538	def test_AUTH_05_concurrent_double_consume_exactly_one_wins(root: Path) -> None:
   539	    """AUTH-05｜注入：并发双消费 approval｜期望：恰一成功。"""
   540	    with _sandbox("auth05") as td:
   541	        db = str(td / "events.db")
   542	        store = EventStore(db)
   543	        mgr_a = RunManager(store, keyring=KEYRING)
   544	        mgr_b = RunManager(EventStore(db), keyring=KEYRING)  # 独立连接的第二个消费者
   545	        run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr_a, "task_auth05", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   546	        v = mgr_a.get_run(run_id).state_version
   547	        ap = _resume_approval(
   548	            run_id, "task_auth05", stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   549	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v,
   550	            preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"])
   551	        pre = VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]
   552	
   553	        results: List[Tuple[str, Any]] = []
   554	        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
   555	
   556	        def _consume(tag: str, mgr: RunManager) -> None:
   557	            barrier.wait()
   558	            try:
   559	                rec = mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, copy.deepcopy(ap), dict(pre))
   560	                results.append((tag, rec))
   561	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   562	                results.append((tag, exc))
   563	
   564	        t1 = threading.Thread(target=_consume, args=("A", mgr_a))
   565	        t2 = threading.Thread(target=_consume, args=("B", mgr_b))
   566	        for t in (t1, t2):
   567	            t.start()
   568	        for t in (t1, t2):
   569	            t.join(timeout=60)
   570	        _ok(not t1.is_alive() and not t2.is_alive(), "并发消费不得死锁/挂起")
   571	
   572	        wins = [r for r in results if isinstance(r[1], dict)]
   573	        losses = [r for r in results if isinstance(r[1], ApprovalReuseError)]
   574	        _ok(len(wins) == 1, f"恰一成功（实得 {len(wins)}）: {results!r}")
   575	        _ok(len(losses) == 1, f"另一路必为复用拒绝（实得 {len(losses)}）: {results!r}")
   576	        _ok(wins[0][1]["resumed"] is True, "成功方真实完成 resume")
   577	        # 事件面：WAITING_RESUMED 恰一条、nonce 消费恰一条（全有或全无）
   578	        _ok(_count_events(store, run_id, "WAITING_RESUMED") == 1,
   579	            "WAITING_RESUMED 事件恰一条（不产生双恢复）")
   580	        _ok(len(mgr_a.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id)) == 1, "旁表恰一条消费")
   581	        st = RunManager(EventStore(db), keyring=KEYRING).get_run(run_id)
   582	        _ok(st.state == "RUNNING" and st.waiting is None, "重放终态=已恢复且无停等残留")
   583	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "并发写后事件链完整性保持")
   584	        mgr_a.close()
   585	        mgr_b.close()
   586	        store.close()
   587	
   588	
   589	def test_AUTH_06_key_revoked_clock_rollback_no_user_presence_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   590	    """AUTH-06｜注入：key revoked/clock rollback/无 user-presence｜期望：拒绝。"""
   591	    with _sandbox("auth06") as td:
   592	        now = time.time()
   593	        # 腿一：key revoked —— keyring 移除 key_test_1（仅登记 key_test_2），
   594	        # 用 key_test_1 签发的审批变成未知 key → 拒
   595	        ring_without_a = ApprovalKeyring({"key_test_2": SECRET_B})
   596	        store, mgr = _mgr(td, "a.db", keyring=ring_without_a)
   597	        run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, "task_auth06a", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   598	        v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   599	        pre = VALID_PRECONDITIONS["prod-write"]
   600	        revoked_key_ap = _resume_approval(
   601	            run_id, "task_auth06a", stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   602	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v, preconditions=pre)
   603	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, revoked_key_ap, pre),
   604	                contains="key_id")
   605	        # 对照：仍在册的 key_test_2 签发 → 可消费（撤销语义=移出即失效，非全锁死）
   606	        still_valid = _resume_approval(
   607	            run_id, "task_auth06a", stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   608	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v, preconditions=pre)
   609	        still_valid = _sign(still_valid, secret=SECRET_B, key_id=KEY_B)
   610	        _ok(mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, still_valid, pre)["resumed"] is True,
   611	            "对照组：在册密钥签发可消费")
   612	        store.close()
   613	
   614	        # 腿二：clock rollback —— 消费侧时钟落后于签发时刻（now < issued_at）
   615	        store2, mgr2 = _mgr(td, "b.db")
   616	        run2, w2 = _waiting_run(mgr2, "task_auth06b", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   617	        v2 = mgr2.get_run(run2).state_version
   618	        future_issued = _resume_approval(
   619	            run2, "task_auth06b", stop_event_id=w2["stop_event_id"],
   620	            stop_type="prod-write", version=v2, preconditions=pre,
   621	            issued_at=_iso(now + 3600), expires_at=_iso(now + 7200))
   622	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr2.resume_waiting(
   623	            run2, future_issued, pre), contains="TTL")
   624	        _ok(mgr2.get_run(run2).state == "WAITING", "时钟回拨注入后仍 WAITING")
   625	        store2.close()
   626	
   627	        # 腿三：无 user-presence —— 产品映射为 decision 非明确『approve』
   628	        # （conditional=未定的条件意见，deny=拒绝意见）均不足以消费
   629	        store3, mgr3 = _mgr(td, "c.db")
   630	        run3, w3 = _waiting_run(mgr3, "task_auth06c", "prod-write", ID_STAGING)
   631	        v3 = mgr3.get_run(run3).state_version
   632	        for decision in ("conditional", "deny"):
   633	            ap = _resume_approval(
   634	                run3, "task_auth06c", stop_event_id=w3["stop_event_id"],
   635	                stop_type="prod-write", version=v3, preconditions=pre,
   636	                decision=decision)
   637	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda a=ap: mgr3.resume_waiting(run3, a, pre),
   638	                    contains="approve")
   639	        _ok(mgr3.get_run(run3).state == "WAITING", "非明确批准不得恢复")
   640	        store3.close()
   641	
   642	
   643	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   644	# STOP 族（§20.5；方案 L330 要求九类逐类型全覆盖三态矩阵）
   645	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   646	def test_STOP_01_nine_types_wrong_type_stale_version_replay_all_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   647	    """STOP-01｜注入：九种 stop type 使用错误类型审批（错误类型/旧版本/重放）｜期望：全拒绝。"""
   648	    with _sandbox("stop01") as td:
   649	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   650	        for idx, stop_type in enumerate(NINE_STOP_TYPES):
   651	            task = f"task_stop01_{idx}"
   652	            run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, task, stop_type, ID_STAGING)
   653	            v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   654	            other_type = NINE_STOP_TYPES[(idx + 1) % len(NINE_STOP_TYPES)]
   655	            other_pre = VALID_PRECONDITIONS[other_type]
   656	
   657	            # (a) 错误类型（半边一）：risk 类审批喂 resume API → 路由判别拒
   658	            risk_like = _risk_approval(
   659	                run_id, task, version=v, fingerprints=[], stage="S1_REQUIREMENT",
   660	                stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"])
   661	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   662	                run_id, risk_like, other_pre), contains="resume")
   663	
   664	            # (b) 错误类型（半边二）：resume 审批锚定他类停等（自洽但错型）→ 绑定拒
   665	            wrong_stop = _resume_approval(
   666	                run_id, task, stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   667	                stop_type=other_type, version=v, preconditions=other_pre)
   668	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   669	                run_id, wrong_stop, other_pre), contains="stop_type")
   670	
   671	            # (c) 旧版本：expected_state_version=v-1（重签有效）→ CAS 拒
   672	            stale = _resume_approval(
   673	                run_id, task, stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   674	                stop_type=stop_type, version=v - 1,
   675	                preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type])
   676	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   677	                run_id, stale, VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type]),
   678	                contains="state_version")
   679	
   680	            # (d) 重放：合法消费一次 → 同 nonce 重放 → 一次性拒
   681	            good = _resume_approval(
   682	                run_id, task, stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   683	                stop_type=stop_type, version=v,
   684	                preconditions=VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type])
   685	            _ok(mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, good, VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type])["resumed"],
   686	                f"{stop_type}: 合法授权应恢复（对照）")
   687	            _raises(ApprovalReuseError, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   688	                run_id, good, VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type]), contains="nonce")
   689	            st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   690	            _ok(st.state == "RUNNING" and st.waiting is None,
   691	                f"{stop_type}: 恢复后无停等残留")
   692	        _ok(len(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals()) == len(NINE_STOP_TYPES),
   693	            "九类各恰一次消费")
   694	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "九类矩阵后事件链完整性保持")
   695	        store.close()
   696	
   697	
   698	def test_STOP_02_resource_ext_unavail_not_recovered_but_approved_still_blocked(root: Path) -> None:
   699	    """STOP-02｜注入：resource/ext-unavail 未恢复但已批准｜期望：仍不可 resume。"""
   700	    with _sandbox("stop02") as td:
   701	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   702	        for stop_type in ("resource", "ext-unavail"):
   703	            task = f"task_stop02_{stop_type.replace('-', '_')}"
   704	            run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, task, stop_type, ID_STAGING)
   705	            v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   706	            # 授权按『已恢复世界』签发（客观条件内容哈希绑定到恢复后事实）
   707	            recovered_pre = dict(VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type])
   708	            ap = _resume_approval(
   709	                run_id, task, stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   710	                stop_type=stop_type, version=v, preconditions=recovered_pre)
   711	
   712	            # 注入一：探针未恢复（probe_recovered=False，真 bool）→ 客观条件不满足
   713	            not_recovered = dict(recovered_pre, probe_recovered=False)
   714	            _raises(ApprovalPreconditionError, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   715	                run_id, ap, not_recovered), contains="not satisfied")
   716	            # 注入二：自报字符串 "false"（伪装恢复）→ 非真 bool 拒
   717	            self_reported = dict(recovered_pre, probe_recovered="false")
   718	            _raises(ApprovalPreconditionError, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(
   719	                run_id, ap, self_reported), contains="boolean")
   720	            _ok(mgr.get_run(run_id).state == "WAITING",
   721	                f"{stop_type}: 已批准但未恢复 → 仍 WAITING")
   722	            _ok(len(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id)) == 0,
   723	                f"{stop_type}: 拒绝路径零消费（授权未被烧掉）")
   724	
   725	            # 对照：客观条件真恢复（真 bool True 且与授权哈希一致）→ 放行
   726	            _ok(mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, ap, recovered_pre)["resumed"],
   727	                f"{stop_type}: 恢复+授权齐备 → 放行（必要且充分）")
   728	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   729	        store.close()
   730	
   731	
   732	def test_STOP_03_nine_types_preconditions_met_but_no_authorization_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   733	    """STOP-03｜注入：九种 stop type 客观条件已满足但缺一次性授权｜期望：九类全部拒绝 resume。"""
   734	    with _sandbox("stop03") as td:
   735	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   736	        for idx, stop_type in enumerate(NINE_STOP_TYPES):
   737	            task = f"task_stop03_{idx}"
   738	            run_id, w = _waiting_run(mgr, task, stop_type, ID_STAGING)
   739	            pre = VALID_PRECONDITIONS[stop_type]  # 客观条件已齐备
   740	
   741	            # (a) 客观条件满足也不得绕过授权直接推进
   742	            _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.advance_stage(run_id),
   743	                    contains="resume_waiting")
   744	            # (b) 无授权（approval=None）——客观条件再齐备也拒
   745	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, None, pre),
   746	                    contains="not a JSON object")
   747	            # (c) 伪签名授权（形状合法 64-hex 但 HMAC 不对）→ 验签拒
   748	            v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   749	            forged = _resume_approval(
   750	                run_id, task, stop_event_id=w["stop_event_id"],
   751	                stop_type=stop_type, version=v, preconditions=pre)
   752	            forged["signature"] = "0" * 64
   753	            _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.resume_waiting(run_id, forged, pre),
   754	                    contains="signature verification failed")
   755	
   756	            st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   757	            _ok(st.state == "WAITING" and st.waiting is not None,
   758	                f"{stop_type}: 缺一次性授权 → 九类全部拒绝 resume")
   759	            _ok(st.waiting.stop_type == stop_type, f"{stop_type}: 停等类型不可漂移")
   760	            _ok(len(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id)) == 0,
   761	                f"{stop_type}: 零授权消费")
   762	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   763	        store.close()
   764
