   766	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   767	# RISK 族（§20.5 RISK-01）
   768	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   769	def _finding_schema_validator():
   770	    """载入冻结 finding-event-v2.schema.json（jsonschema 缺失时返回 None）。"""
   771	    try:
   772	        import jsonschema
   773	    except ImportError:
   774	        return None
   775	    schema = json.loads(
   776	        (SYSTEM_DIR / "schemas" / "finding-event-v2.schema.json").read_text(
   777	            encoding="utf-8"))
   778	    return jsonschema.Draft7Validator(schema)
   779	
   780	
   781	def _finding_event(event_type: str, *, state_from: Optional[str],
   782	                   state_to: Optional[str] = None, **over: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   783	    doc: Dict[str, Any] = {
   784	        "schema_version": "2.0",
   785	        "event_type": event_type,
   786	        "finding_id": "fnd_risk01",
   787	        "fingerprint": "fp-risk01",
   788	        "severity": "MEDIUM",
   789	        "priority": "P2",
   790	        "ts": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z",
   791	    }
   792	    if state_from is not None:
   793	        doc["state_from"] = state_from
   794	    if state_to is not None:
   795	        doc["state_to"] = state_to
   796	    doc.update(over)
   797	    return doc
   798	
   799	
   800	def test_RISK_01_revoke_or_invalidate_appends_event_reopens_repairs(root: Path) -> None:
   801	    """RISK-01｜注入：主动撤销或 fingerprint/severity/policy/control 改变｜
   802	    期望：追加 REVOKED/INVALIDATED，立即重开并可进入修复链。"""
   803	    # ── 半边一：冻结 schema 转移表（产品正源资产）真实验证 ──
   804	    validator = _finding_schema_validator()
   805	    if validator is not None:
   806	        # 合法风险接受（对照组：ACCEPTED_RISK←OPEN，MEDIUM/P2+补偿措施）
   807	        validator.validate(_finding_event(
   808	            "ACCEPTED_RISK", state_from="OPEN",
   809	            risk_acceptance={
   810	                "approver": "授权人-超哥", "reason": "ac-auth-cond 低风险接受理由",
   811	                "scope": "scope-ac-001", "compensating_controls": ["ctrl-1"],
   812	                "expiry": "2099-12-31T00:00:00Z"}))
   813	        # 主动撤销：RISK_REVOKED←ACCEPTED_RISK → 追加 REVOKED 合法
   814	        validator.validate(_finding_event("RISK_REVOKED", state_from="ACCEPTED_RISK"))
   815	        # fingerprint/severity/policy/control 改变 → RISK_INVALIDATED 追加且立即重开
   816	        validator.validate(_finding_event(
   817	            "RISK_INVALIDATED", state_from="ACCEPTED_RISK", state_to="OPEN"))
   818	        # 立即重开后可进入修复链：FIX_STARTED←OPEN 合法
   819	        validator.validate(_finding_event("FIX_STARTED", state_from="OPEN"))
   820	        # 负例：非 ACCEPTED_RISK 起点的撤销/失效（未接受先撤销）→ schema 拒
   821	        for bad in (
   822	            _finding_event("RISK_REVOKED", state_from="OPEN"),
   823	            _finding_event("RISK_INVALIDATED", state_from="OPEN", state_to="OPEN"),
   824	            _finding_event("RISK_INVALIDATED", state_from="ACCEPTED_RISK",
   825	                           state_to="CLOSED"),   # 失效必须立即重开为 OPEN，不得关死
   826	            {"schema_version": "2.0", "event_type": "RISK_INVALIDATED",
   827	             "finding_id": "fnd_risk01", "fingerprint": "fp", "severity": "MEDIUM",
   828	             "priority": "P2", "ts": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z"},  # 缺 state_from/state_to
   829	        ):
   830	            errors = list(validator.iter_errors(bad))
   831	            _ok(errors, f"schema 必须拒绝非法撤销/失效转移: {bad.get('event_type')}"
   832	                        f"/state_from={bad.get('state_from')!r}")
   833	    else:
   834	        print("    (note) jsonschema 未安装，schema 转移表断言降级为管线半边")
   835	
   836	    # ── 半边二：管线侧 —— finding 面改变使已消费授权在判定点立即失效，
   837	    #    run 保持可修复（新 attempt 修复链入口可用） ──
   838	    with _sandbox("risk01") as td:
   839	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   840	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_risk01", ID_STAGING)
   841	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_risk01_a", "fp-risk01-a", "MEDIUM", "P2")
   842	        v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   843	        auth = _risk_approval(run_id, "task_risk01", version=v,
   844	                              fingerprints=["fp-risk01-a"])
   845	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, auth)["consumed"] is True, "授权先合法消费")
   846	
   847	        # 注入：授权后 finding 面改变（新增 fingerprint）→ 授权覆盖面失配
   848	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_risk01_b", "fp-risk01-b", "MEDIUM", "P2")
   849	        _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.complete_run(run_id),
   850	                contains="未过期且指纹精确匹配")
   851	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run_id).state == "RUNNING",
   852	            "授权失效后不得完成 CONDITIONAL（立即重开=保持可变）")
   853	        # 可进入修复链：软终态段可开新 attempt（修复链入口）
   854	        out = mgr.advance_stage(run_id)
   855	        st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   856	        _ok(out.get("started_attempt_id", "").startswith("att_"),
   857	            "修复链入口：软终态段可追加新 attempt")
   858	        _ok(st.stages["S7_GATE"].current_attempt.status == "RUNNING",
   859	            "S7 修复 attempt 处于 RUNNING")
   860	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   861	        store.close()
   862	
   863	
   864	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   865	# COND 族（§20.5 COND-01~06）
   866	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   867	def test_COND_01_conditional_without_risk_authorization_not_eligible(root: Path) -> None:
   868	    """COND-01｜注入：CONDITIONAL 无 risk authorization｜期望：technical eligibility=false，技术 context failure。"""
   869	    # 聚合半边：CONDITIONAL 站 → technical_eligible=False + 技术上下文 failure
   870	    agg = GateAggregator({"1", "2"}, target_sha=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   871	                         environment="staging")
   872	    agg.add({"station": "1", "outcome": "PASS", "sha": ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   873	             "environment": "staging"})
   874	    agg.add({"station": "2", "outcome": "CONDITIONAL", "sha": ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   875	             "environment": "staging"})
   876	    verdict = agg.aggregate()
   877	    _ok(verdict["aggregate_outcome"] == CONDITIONAL, "条件站必须保持 CONDITIONAL")
   878	    _ok(verdict["technical_eligible"] is False, "无授权 CONDITIONAL → technical_eligible=false")
   879	    status = agg.to_github_status(verdict)
   880	    _ok(status["state"] == "failure", "技术 context 必须 failure（绝不上绿）")
   881	
   882	    # 管线半边：软终态 run 无 risk 授权 → complete_run fail-closed 拒绝
   883	    with _sandbox("cond01") as td:
   884	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   885	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond01", ID_STAGING)
   886	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond01", "fp-cond01", "MEDIUM", "P2")
   887	        _ok(not mgr.get_run(run_id).risk_authorizations, "未消费任何 risk 授权")
   888	        _ok(mgr.broker.has_unexpired_risk_authorization(run_id) is None,
   889	            "授权查询面为空")
   890	        _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.complete_run(run_id), contains="risk 授权")
   891	        st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   892	        _ok(st.state == "RUNNING" and not st.terminal,
   893	            "无授权 CONDITIONAL 不得完成——保持未完成（fail-closed）")
   894	        _ok(st.stage_statuses()["S7_GATE"] == "COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL",
   895	            "内部判定保持 CONDITIONAL（不洗白为 PASS）")
   896	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   897	        store.close()
   898	
   899	
   900	def test_COND_02_authorization_cross_sha_env_widened_set_replay_rejected(root: Path) -> None:
   901	    """COND-02｜注入：授权跨 SHA/env/扩 finding 集/重放｜期望：拒绝。"""
   902	    with _sandbox("cond02") as td:
   903	        # 三个软终态 run：A=staging/X；B=staging/Y（异 SHA）；C=production/X（异 env）
   904	        id_b = dict(ID_STAGING, commit_sha="b" * 40)
   905	        id_c = dict(ID_STAGING, environment="production")
   906	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   907	        run_a = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond02a", ID_STAGING)
   908	        run_b = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond02b", id_b)
   909	        run_c = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond02c", id_c)
   910	        for r in (run_a, run_b, run_c):
   911	            mgr.register_finding(r, "fnd_cond02", "fp-cond02", "MEDIUM", "P2")
   912	        va = mgr.get_run(run_a).state_version
   913	        template = _risk_approval(run_a, "task_cond02a", version=va,
   914	                                  fingerprints=["fp-cond02"])
   915	
   916	        # 腿一：跨 SHA —— 授权移植到 run B（重签有效，watermark 仍锚 X）→ 拒
   917	        cross_sha = _sign(dict(template, run_id=run_b, task_id="task_cond02b",
   918	                               input_watermark=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"]))
   919	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_b, cross_sha),
   920	                contains="commit_sha")
   921	        # 腿二：跨 env —— 授权移植到 run C（重签有效，env 仍 staging）→ 拒
   922	        cross_env = _sign(dict(template, run_id=run_c, task_id="task_cond02c",
   923	                               environment="staging",
   924	                               input_watermark=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"]))
   925	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_c, cross_env),
   926	                contains="environment")
   927	        # 腿三：扩 finding 集 —— 指纹面声称超出 run 实际可接受集 → 精确匹配拒
   928	        vb = mgr.get_run(run_b).state_version
   929	        widened = _risk_approval(
   930	            run_b, "task_cond02b", version=vb,
   931	            fingerprints=["fp-cond02", "fp-not-registered"],
   932	            input_watermark=id_b["commit_sha"])
   933	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_b, widened),
   934	                contains="精确匹配")
   935	        # 腿四：重放 —— run C 合法消费一次后同 nonce 重放 → 拒
   936	        vc = mgr.get_run(run_c).state_version
   937	        legal_c = _risk_approval(run_c, "task_cond02c", version=vc,
   938	                                 fingerprints=["fp-cond02"],
   939	                                 environment="production",
   940	                                 input_watermark=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"])
   941	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run_c, legal_c)["consumed"] is True, "对照组：合法授权可消费")
   942	        _raises(ApprovalReuseError, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_c, legal_c),
   943	                contains="nonce")
   944	        _ok(len(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_c)) == 1, "恰一次消费")
   945	        for r in (run_a, run_b):
   946	            _ok(mgr.get_run(r).risk_authorizations == [],
   947	                f"{r}: 移植/扩面授权零落账")
   948	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
   949	        store.close()
   950	
   951	
   952	def test_COND_03_authorized_conditional_action_gate_exactly_once(root: Path) -> None:
   953	    """COND-03｜注入：合法低风险接受、其余全 PASS、risk 与 action 两类精确授权｜
   954	    期望：内部仍 CONDITIONAL；技术 context=success/AUTHORIZED_CONDITIONAL；Action Gate 成功后动作恰一次。"""
   955	    # 聚合半边：其余全 PASS + 单条件站 → 整线仍 CONDITIONAL（绝不上绿）
   956	    agg = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   957	                         environment="staging")
   958	    agg.add({"station": "1", "outcome": "CONDITIONAL", "sha": ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
   959	             "environment": "staging"})
   960	    v_agg = agg.aggregate()
   961	    _ok(v_agg["aggregate_outcome"] == CONDITIONAL and not v_agg["technical_eligible"],
   962	        "其余全 PASS 时条件站仍使整线 CONDITIONAL（内部不洗白）")
   963	
   964	    with _sandbox("cond03") as td:
   965	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
   966	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond03", ID_STAGING)
   967	        statuses = mgr.get_run(run_id).stage_statuses()
   968	        _ok(all(statuses[s] == "PASSED" for s in list(statuses)[:6])
   969	            and statuses["S7_GATE"] == "COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL",
   970	            "S1..S6 全 PASS + S7 CONDITIONAL（内部仍 CONDITIONAL）")
   971	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond03", "fp-cond03", "LOW", "P3")
   972	
   973	        # risk 类精确授权（指纹精确匹配可接受面）→ 消费成功
   974	        v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   975	        risk = _risk_approval(run_id, "task_cond03", version=v,
   976	                              fingerprints=["fp-cond03"], severity="LOW")
   977	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, risk)["consumed"] is True, "risk 类精确授权消费")
   978	
   979	        # action 类精确授权（prod_write 审批）→ Action Gate 全链恰一次
   980	        action_ap = _action_approval(run_id, "task_cond03", version=v,
   981	                                     stage="S7_GATE")
   982	        reserved = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "prod-write",
   983	                                      "db://staging/erp#cond03", action_ap)
   984	        _ok(reserved["action_state"] == "RESERVED", "action 授权预留成功")
   985	        mgr.start_action(run_id, reserved["action_id"], receipt="cond03 caller self-report")
   986	        receipt = _external_receipt(reserved, run_id)
   987	        _ok(mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, reserved["action_id"], receipt)["reconciled"]
   988	            is True, "外部正源回执对账通过（Action Gate 成功）")
   989	        committed = mgr.commit_action(run_id, reserved["action_id"], receipt)
   990	        _ok(committed["action_state"] == "COMMITTED"
   991	            and committed["receipt_status"] == RECEIPT_RECONCILED,
   992	            "动作经对账回执恰一次 COMMITTED")
   993	
   994	        # 动作恰一次：终态后不可再动、授权不可复用、旧回执不可移植到新动作
   995	        _raises(ActionError, lambda: mgr.start_action(run_id, reserved["action_id"]))
   996	        _raises(ActionError, lambda: mgr.commit_action(
   997	            run_id, reserved["action_id"], receipt))
   998	        _raises(ApprovalReuseError, lambda: mgr.reserve_action(
   999	            run_id, "prod-write", "db://staging/erp#cond03-2", action_ap))
  1000	        fresh_ap = _action_approval(run_id, "task_cond03", version=v, stage="S7_GATE")
  1001	        second = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "prod-write",
  1002	                                    "db://staging/erp#cond03-2", fresh_ap)
  1003	        mgr.start_action(run_id, second["action_id"])
  1004	        # 旧动作的 attestation 移植到新动作（action_id 不一致）→ Action Gate 拒
  1005	        _raises(ActionError, lambda: mgr.commit_action(
  1006	            run_id, second["action_id"], receipt), contains="action_id")
  1007	        mgr.fail_action(run_id, second["action_id"],
  1008	                        "旧 attestation 不可移植——按失败收口")
  1009	
  1010	        # 技术上下文：授权后的 CONDITIONAL 完成可成立（AUTHORIZED_CONDITIONAL 的
  1011	        # 产品落地面），且 verdict 仍 CONDITIONAL、不升格 SUCCEEDED
  1012	        done = mgr.complete_run(run_id)
  1013	        _ok(done["final_state"] == "COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL",
  1014	            "risk+action 双授权后 COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL 成立")
  1015	        _ok(done["policy_verdict"] == "CONDITIONAL" and done["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED",
  1016	            "完成事件双轴仍 COMPLETED/CONDITIONAL（内部 CONDITIONAL 语义保持）")
  1017	        _ok("risk authorization" in done["terminal_reason"],
  1018	            "终态理由须记录授权在案（AUTHORIZED_CONDITIONAL 语义）")
  1019	        st = RunManager(EventStore(str(td / "events.db")),
  1020	                        keyring=KEYRING).get_run(run_id)
  1021	        _ok(st.actions[reserved["action_id"]]["action_state"] == "COMMITTED",
  1022	            "重放面：动作恰一次 COMMITTED 随事件流重建")
  1023	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
  1024	        store.close()
  1025	
  1026	
  1027	def test_COND_04_p0_p1_risk_acceptance_or_conditional_auth_rejected_stays_blocked(root: Path) -> None:
  1028	    """COND-04｜注入：P0/P1 尝试风险接受或条件授权｜期望：拒绝并保持 BLOCKED。"""
  1029	    with _sandbox("cond04") as td:
  1030	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
  1031	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond04", ID_STAGING)
  1032	        # 登记不可风险接受面：HIGH/P1（severity 轴）与 MEDIUM/P0（priority 轴）
  1033	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond04_sev", "fp-cond04-sev", "HIGH", "P1")
  1034	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond04_pri", "fp-cond04-pri", "MEDIUM", "P0")
  1035	        # 一个可接受项作为对照（证明拒的是 P0/P1 面而非整 run 死锁）
  1036	        mgr.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond04_ok", "fp-cond04-ok", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1037	        v = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
  1038	
  1039	        # 腿一：P0/P1 风险接受（HIGH severity 声明）→ 机器拒绝
  1040	        ap_high = _risk_approval(run_id, "task_cond04", version=v,
  1041	                                 fingerprints=["fp-cond04-sev"], severity="HIGH")
  1042	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, ap_high),
  1043	                contains="不可风险接受")
  1044	        # 腿二：以 MEDIUM 声明夹带 P0/P1 指纹（伪装条件授权）→ 精确匹配拒
  1045	        ap_smuggle = _risk_approval(
  1046	            run_id, "task_cond04", version=v,
  1047	            fingerprints=["fp-cond04-ok", "fp-cond04-pri"], severity="MEDIUM")
  1048	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, ap_smuggle),
  1049	                contains="精确匹配")
  1050	        # 腿三：即便先合法接受了可接受项，complete 仍因 P0/P1 在案面拒绝并保持未完成
  1051	        legal = _risk_approval(run_id, "task_cond04", version=v,
  1052	                               fingerprints=["fp-cond04-ok"], severity="MEDIUM")
  1053	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, legal)["consumed"] is True, "对照项授权消费成功")
  1054	        _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.complete_run(run_id),
  1055	                contains="不得风险接受")
  1056	        st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
  1057	        _ok(st.state == "RUNNING" and not st.terminal,
  1058	            "P0/P1 在案 → CONDITIONAL 完成被阻断（保持 BLOCKED 语义：不得完成）")
  1059	        _ok(st.stage_statuses()["S7_GATE"] == "COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL",
  1060	            "内部判定保持软终态不漂移")
  1061	        # 聚合半边：条件态永不映射绿（保持阻断口径一致）
  1062	        agg = GateAggregator({"1"}, target_sha=ID_STAGING["commit_sha"],
  1063	                             environment="staging")
  1064	        agg.add({"station": "1", "outcome": "CONDITIONAL",
  1065	                 "sha": ID_STAGING["commit_sha"], "environment": "staging"})
  1066	        _ok(agg.to_github_status(agg.aggregate())["state"] == "failure",
  1067	            "CONDITIONAL 技术上下文保持 failure（不上绿=保持阻断）")
  1068	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
  1069	        store.close()
  1070

  1072	def test_COND_05_post_auth_new_finding_ttl_identity_change_invalidated(root: Path) -> None:
  1073	    """COND-05｜注入：授权后新增 finding、TTL 到期、SHA/scope/policy/watermark 变化｜
  1074	    期望：authorization、attestation、context 失效；动作前重验拒绝。"""
  1075	    with _sandbox("cond05") as td:
  1076	        now = time.time()
  1077	        store, mgr = _mgr(td)
  1078	
  1079	        # 腿一：授权后新增 finding → 授权覆盖面失配 → complete 拒（authorization 失效）
  1080	        run1 = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond05a", ID_STAGING)
  1081	        mgr.register_finding(run1, "fnd_cond05_a1", "fp-cond05-a1", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1082	        v1 = mgr.get_run(run1).state_version
  1083	        auth1 = _risk_approval(run1, "task_cond05a", version=v1,
  1084	                               fingerprints=["fp-cond05-a1"])
  1085	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run1, auth1)["consumed"] is True, "授权先消费")
  1086	        mgr.register_finding(run1, "fnd_cond05_a2", "fp-cond05-a2", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1087	        _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.complete_run(run1),
  1088	                contains="未过期且指纹精确匹配")
  1089	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run1).state == "RUNNING", "context 失效 → 不得完成")
  1090	
  1091	        # 腿二：TTL 到期（envelope expires_at=now+2s，消费后睡眠过期）→ 失效
  1092	        run2 = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond05b", ID_STAGING)
  1093	        mgr.register_finding(run2, "fnd_cond05_b", "fp-cond05-b", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1094	        v2 = mgr.get_run(run2).state_version
  1095	        short_ttl = _risk_approval(run2, "task_cond05b", version=v2,
  1096	                                   fingerprints=["fp-cond05-b"],
  1097	                                   issued_at=_iso(now - 10), expires_at=_iso(now + 2))
  1098	        _ok(mgr.authorize_risk(run2, short_ttl)["consumed"] is True, "短 TTL 授权先消费")
  1099	        time.sleep(3)  # 真实等待过期（非 mock）
  1100	        _ok(mgr.broker.has_unexpired_risk_authorization(run2) is None,
  1101	            "TTL 到期 → authorization 失效（事件正源重判）")
  1102	        _raises(PipelineError, lambda: mgr.complete_run(run2), contains="未过期")
  1103	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run2).state == "RUNNING", "TTL 失效后 run 保持未完成")
  1104	
  1105	        # 腿三：scope 变化（新 run 异 scope_hash）→ 旧授权不可移植（context 不可带走的 SHA/scope/watermark 面）
  1106	        # （换新 nonce 重签——否则重放检查先于绑定拒，遮蔽 scope 拒绝面）
  1107	        id_other_scope = dict(ID_STAGING, scope_hash="scope-ac-999")
  1108	        run3 = _soft_terminal_run(mgr, "task_cond05c", id_other_scope)
  1109	        mgr.register_finding(run3, "fnd_cond05_c", "fp-cond05-c", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1110	        v3 = mgr.get_run(run3).state_version
  1111	        transplanted = _sign(dict(
  1112	            auth1, run_id=run3, task_id="task_cond05c",
  1113	            expected_state_version=v3,
  1114	            nonce=f"nonce-ac-transplant-{os.urandom(8).hex()}"))
  1115	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.authorize_risk(run3, transplanted),
  1116	                contains="authorized_scope")
  1117	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run3).risk_authorizations == [], "scope 变化 → 授权零移植")
  1118	
  1119	        # 腿四：动作前重验 —— 授权签署时的 state_version 过期后 reserve 必拒（CAS）
  1120	        run4 = mgr.create_run("task_cond05d", ID_STAGING)["run_id"]
  1121	        mgr.advance_stage(run4)          # v=2，S1 RUNNING
  1122	        stale_action = _action_approval(run4, "task_cond05d", version=2,
  1123	                                        stage="S1_REQUIREMENT")
  1124	        mgr.raise_waiting(run4, "prod-write", "cond05 状态推进使 v=2 → v=3")
  1125	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run4).state_version == 3, "前置：状态版本已前进")
  1126	        _raises(ApprovalRejected, lambda: mgr.reserve_action(
  1127	            run4, "prod-write", "db://staging/erp#cond05", stale_action),
  1128	            contains="state_version")
  1129	        fresh_action = _action_approval(run4, "task_cond05d", version=3,
  1130	                                        stage="S1_REQUIREMENT")
  1131	        _ok(mgr.reserve_action(run4, "prod-write", "db://staging/erp#cond05",
  1132	                               fresh_action)["action_state"] == "RESERVED",
  1133	            "对照组：重验通过的新授权可预留")
  1134	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
  1135	        store.close()
  1136	
  1137	
  1138	def test_COND_06_concurrent_consume_or_replay_attestation_after_failure_once(root: Path) -> None:
  1139	    """COND-06｜注入：并发双消费或动作失败后重放 attestation｜期望：最多一次消费；失败后须新授权。"""
  1140	    with _sandbox("cond06") as td:
  1141	        db = str(td / "events.db")
  1142	        store = EventStore(db)
  1143	        mgr_a = RunManager(store, keyring=KEYRING)
  1144	        mgr_b = RunManager(EventStore(db), keyring=KEYRING)
  1145	        run_id = _soft_terminal_run(mgr_a, "task_cond06", ID_STAGING)
  1146	        mgr_a.register_finding(run_id, "fnd_cond06", "fp-cond06", "MEDIUM", "P2")
  1147	        v = mgr_a.get_run(run_id).state_version
  1148	        ap = _risk_approval(run_id, "task_cond06", version=v,
  1149	                            fingerprints=["fp-cond06"])
  1150	
  1151	        # 腿一：并发双消费 risk 授权 → 最多一次（恰一成功、一复用拒）
  1152	        outcomes: List[Any] = []
  1153	        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
  1154	
  1155	        def _consume(mgr: RunManager) -> None:
  1156	            barrier.wait()
  1157	            try:
  1158	                outcomes.append(mgr.authorize_risk(run_id, copy.deepcopy(ap)))
  1159	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1160	                outcomes.append(exc)
  1161	
  1162	        t1 = threading.Thread(target=_consume, args=(mgr_a,))
  1163	        t2 = threading.Thread(target=_consume, args=(mgr_b,))
  1164	        for t in (t1, t2):
  1165	            t.start()
  1166	        for t in (t1, t2):
  1167	            t.join(timeout=60)
  1168	        _ok(sum(isinstance(o, dict) for o in outcomes) == 1,
  1169	            f"并发双消费恰一成功: {outcomes!r}")
  1170	        _ok(sum(isinstance(o, ApprovalReuseError) for o in outcomes) == 1,
  1171	            f"另一路必为复用拒绝: {outcomes!r}")
  1172	        _ok(len(mgr_a.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id)) == 1, "台账恰一次消费")
  1173	        mgr_a.close()
  1174	        mgr_b.close()
  1175	
  1176	        # 腿二：动作失败后重放 attestation → 终态不再裁决；须新授权才能再动作
  1177	        mgr = RunManager(EventStore(db), keyring=KEYRING)
  1178	        ap2 = _action_approval(run_id, "task_cond06", version=v, stage="S7_GATE")
  1179	        reserved = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "prod-write", "db://staging/erp#cond06",
  1180	                                      ap2)
  1181	        aid = reserved["action_id"]
  1182	        mgr.start_action(run_id, aid, receipt="cond06 started")
  1183	        failed = mgr.fail_action(run_id, aid, "runner crash; outcome unknown")
  1184	        _ok(failed["action_state"] == ACTION_FAILED_UNKNOWN, "动作失败收口 FAILED_UNKNOWN")
  1185	        receipt = _external_receipt(reserved, run_id)  # 事后到达的合法 attestation
  1186	        rec = mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, aid, receipt)
  1187	        _ok(rec["reconciled"] is None and "FAILED_UNKNOWN" in rec["note"],
  1188	            "失败终态后重放 attestation 不再裁决（最多一次消费）")
  1189	        _raises(ActionError, lambda: mgr.commit_action(run_id, aid, receipt),
  1190	                contains="illegal action transition")
  1191	        _raises(ApprovalReuseError, lambda: mgr.reserve_action(
  1192	            run_id, "prod-write", "db://staging/erp#cond06-2", ap2),
  1193	            contains="nonce")  # 失败后旧授权已消费——须新授权
  1194	        ap3 = _action_approval(run_id, "task_cond06", version=v, stage="S7_GATE")
  1195	        renewed = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "prod-write",
  1196	                                     "db://staging/erp#cond06-2", ap3)
  1197	        _ok(renewed["action_state"] == "RESERVED", "失败后凭新授权可再动作（修复路径）")
  1198	        mgr.start_action(run_id, renewed["action_id"])
  1199	        committed = mgr.commit_action(
  1200	            run_id, renewed["action_id"], _external_receipt(renewed, run_id))
  1201	        _ok(committed["action_state"] == "COMMITTED", "新授权链可完整走通")
  1202	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run_id).actions[aid]["action_state"] == ACTION_FAILED_UNKNOWN,
  1203	            "失败动作终态不可复活")
  1204	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "事件链完整性保持")
  1205	        mgr.close()
  1206	        store.close()
  1207	
  1208	
  1209	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1210	# 运行器
  1211	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1212	_TEST_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^test_(AUTH|STOP|RISK|COND)_(\d{2})_")
  1213	
  1214	# 不可实现清单：本四族 16 条均有真实产品语义可注入，无整条不可实现项。
  1215	NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE: Dict[str, str] = {}
  1216	
  1217	# 诚实映射边界（非整条跳过；语义映射说明见模块 docstring）
  1218	MAPPING_NOTES: Dict[str, str] = {
  1219	    "AUTH-06": "「无 user-presence」映射为 decision!=approve（approval-v2 无独立 UV 位）",
  1220	    "RISK-01": "REVOKED/INVALIDATED/立即重开/修复链半边以冻结 finding-event-v2.schema.json "
  1221	               "（jsonschema 真实验证）断言；管线半边验证 finding 面改变使已消费授权失效且 run 可修复",
  1222	    "COND-01": "「技术 context failure」= gate_aggregator technical_eligible=False+GitHub failure "
  1223	               "＋ complete_run 无 risk 授权 fail-closed",
  1224	    "COND-03": "「success/AUTHORIZED_CONDITIONAL」产品落地面 = COMPLETED_CONDITIONAL 终态"
  1225	               "（verdict 仍 CONDITIONAL，terminal_reason 记授权在案）",
  1226	}
  1227	
  1228	
  1229	def _collect_tests() -> List[Tuple[str, Callable[..., None]]]:
  1230	    return [
  1231	        (name, fn)
  1232	        for name, fn in sorted(globals().items())
  1233	        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn) and _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name)
  1234	    ]
  1235	
  1236	
  1237	def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
  1238	    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
  1239	    json_out: Optional[str] = None
  1240	    if "--json" in argv:
  1241	        json_out = argv[argv.index("--json") + 1]
  1242	    tests = _collect_tests()
  1243	    print("=" * 72)
  1244	    print("§20 AUTH/STOP/RISK/COND 四族专属验收测试（16 条；不可实现 0 条）")
  1245	    for tid, note in MAPPING_NOTES.items():
  1246	        print(f"  NOTE {tid}: {note}")
  1247	    print("-" * 72)
  1248	    records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1249	    failed: List[str] = []
  1250	    for name, fn in tests:
  1251	        match = _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name)
  1252	        assert match is not None
  1253	        test_id = f"{match.group(1)}-{match.group(2)}"
  1254	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ac-auth-cond-run-") as td:
  1255	            try:
  1256	                fn(Path(td))
  1257	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": True})
  1258	                print(f"  PASS {test_id}  {name}")
  1259	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1260	                failed.append(name)
  1261	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": False})
  1262	                print(f"  FAIL {test_id}  {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
  1263	    print("-" * 72)
  1264	    print(f"结果: {len(records) - len(failed)}/{len(records)} passed"
  1265	          f"{'' if not failed else '；失败: ' + ', '.join(failed)}")
  1266	    if json_out is not None:
  1267	        out_path = Path(json_out)
  1268	        out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1269	        out_path.write_text(
  1270	            json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  1271	        print(f"逐 ID 结果已写: {out_path}")
  1272	    print("=" * 72)
  1273	    return 0 if not failed else 1
  1274	
  1275	
  1276	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1277	    raise SystemExit(main())

===== system/tests/test_ac_act_run.py =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	"""§20 验收 ID 专属测试——ACT/RUN 族（test_ac_act_run）。
     4	
     5	覆盖 Codex 方案 §20.1/§20.5 中 ACT-01~06、RUN-01~03 共 9 条：每条按
     6	「注入→期望」逐字对齐方案 §20 表格（正源：evidence/00-baseline/
     7	codex-external/方案.md），真实构造场景（真实事件库/tmp fixture、真实调用
     8	产品码、真实子进程）并断言结果，绝不空转。
     9	
    10	被测正源（不改动任何产品码；均为第五波加固后最终语义）：
    11	- system/wenqu_core/wenqu_pipeline.py
    12	    ActionSaga/RunManager（reserve_action 必传 action 类 HMAC 审批、
    13	    _ACTION_APPROVAL_TYPE 封闭映射、receipt PROVISIONAL/RECONCILED 分级、
    14	    commit 只收外部验签回执、FAILED_UNKNOWN、重放授权消费链校验）
    15	    —— ACT-01~06 全部六条。
    16	- system/wenqu_core/runner.py
    17	    TrustedRunner（run token 注入 + 超时三层击杀 killpg+进程树+token 全
    18	    系统扫杀、env 白名单剥除、allowlist opt-in）与 EvidenceStore CAS
    19	    —— RUN-01/02/03。
    20	- system/wenqu_core/approval_keys.py + tests/fixtures/approval-keys.json
    21	    测试密钥范式：审批/外部回执均以 fixtures 密钥真 HMAC-SHA256 签发。
    22	
    23	本地等价映射说明（§20 条目语义 → 本仓被测体语义）：
    24	- ACT-01「context 可表技术 PASS，controller 不动作」→ RunManager 终判可
    25	  表达技术 PASS（七段全 PASSED → SUCCEEDED），但 ActionSaga 侧零事件：
    26	  无 exact merge/release 授权时 reserve_action 全拒（零审批消费、零
    27	  ACTION_* 事件）；技术 PASS run 终态后 controller 永不动作。
    28	- ACT-02「action、method、head/merge-group、artifact/env 任一不符 →
    29	  Action Authorization Gate failure」→ reserve_action 授权门（F4-AUTH-001）
    30	  的各不符腿：action_type↔approval_type 映射不符 / method-artifact 字节
    31	  篡改破坏 HMAC / head=watermark 不符 / env 不符，全拒且零落账。
    32	- ACT-03「check 发布前/后 crash 或回执丢失 → outbox 幂等恢复；同一
    33	  check_run 更新，不产生伪双绿」→ control_outbox 事件幂等（event_id
    34	  INSERT OR IGNORE）、仅复制事件流的崩溃恢复重放一致、同 nonce/同
    35	  action 的重复推进被拒（绝不双 COMMITTED）、无授权消费链的 RESERVED
    36	  重放即 Corruption。
    37	- ACT-04「merge/release 调用前/后 crash 或回执丢失 → 先查询外部事实；
    38	  COMMITTED 或 FAILED_UNKNOWN；绝不盲重试」→ reconcile_receipt（只读
    39	  查询外部事实）先行，COMMITTED 凭验签回执落账；对账不了/外部声明
    40	  FAILED → fail_action=FAILED_UNKNOWN 终态；终态与一次性 nonce 封死
    41	  一切重试路径。
    42	- ACT-05「外部陈旧绿、dispatcher/App 失联 → direct merge 被角色/ruleset
    43	  拒绝；动作保持 BLOCKED」→ 陈旧水印回执拒绝、伪签名回执拒绝、自报
    44	  回执拒绝——动作停留 STARTED/PROVISIONAL（不可 COMMITTED）；ruleset/
    45	  policy 不符的 direct merge 授权在 reserve 门被拒。
    46	- ACT-06「payload 使用错误 discriminator 或缺专属字段 → schema 拒绝」→
    47	  ApprovalBroker.validate_structure 的 payload 判别封闭（键集=该类专属
    48	  字段全集）经 reserve_action 路径逐类型验证。
    49	- RUN-01「被测程序读取 Keychain/SSH agent/生产配置 → 隔离拒绝」→
    50	  TrustedRunner env 白名单剥除：SSH_AUTH_SOCK/生产配置路径等敏感环境
    51	  通道在子进程环境里不存在（隔离拒绝——已实现的进程环境通道半边）。
    52	- RUN-02「timeout 后子孙进程 → 全部终止，无孤儿写盘」→ 超时三层击杀：
    53	  同组子进程（killpg）、setsid 逃逸孙进程（进程树+token 扫杀）、
    54	  double-fork 后被 PID 1 收养的 daemon（token 全系统扫杀）全部死亡，
    55	  三个延迟 marker 均不落盘。
    56	- RUN-03「fork bomb/输出爆量/网络外连 → 配额/allowlist 阻断」→
    57	  allowlist（opt-in）前置阻断：未列名可执行一律 PermissionError、零
    58	  进程产生（marker 不落盘）。
    59	
    60	明确未实现的注入子面（不硬凑、不 xfail，见 BOUNDARIES 登记）：
    61	容器级隔离（Keychain 直读/SSH agent socket 直连/任意路径文件读）、
    62	输出爆量字节配额、网络 egress allowlist、真实 GitHub check_run 发布
    63	outbox、dispatcher/App 失联心跳检测——产品中无对应语义，逐条理由见
    64	模块级 BOUNDARIES。
    65	
    66	独立运行：python3 system/tests/test_ac_act_run.py [--json OUT.json]
    67	exit 0 = 全部用例绿；默认把逐 ID 结果数组写入
    68	evidence/04-unit-property-mutation/ac-act-run.json。
    69	"""

   183	# 通用小工具

   185	def _ok(cond: bool, message: str) -> None:
   186	    if not cond:
   187	        raise AssertionError(message)
   188	
   189	
   190	def _expect_raise(
   191	    exc_type: type, fn: Callable[..., Any], *args: Any, **kwargs: Any
   192	) -> Exception:
   193	    """调用 fn，期望抛出 exc_type；返回异常对象供断言细节。"""
   194	    try:
   195	        fn(*args, **kwargs)
   196	    except exc_type as exc:  # type: ignore[misc]
   197	        return exc
   198	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   199	        raise AssertionError(
   200	            f"expected {exc_type.__name__}, got {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
   201	        ) from exc
   202	    raise AssertionError(
   203	        f"expected {exc_type.__name__} to be raised, but call returned")
   204

   206	@contextlib.contextmanager
   207	def _tmp(root: Optional[Path] = None):
   208	    if root is not None:
   209	        yield Path(root)
   210	        return
   211	    own = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="ac-act-run-")
   212	    try:
   213	        yield Path(own)
   214	    finally:
   215	        shutil.rmtree(own, ignore_errors=True)

   218	def _fresh(root: Path, db_name: str = "events.db") -> Tuple[EventStore, RunManager]:
   219	    store = EventStore(str(root / db_name))

   222	
   223	def _s1_running_run(mgr: RunManager, task: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:
   224	    """create + 启动 S1 → RUNNING，S1 attempt RUNNING，state_version=2。"""
   225	    run_id = mgr.create_run(task, IDENT)["run_id"]
   226	    mgr.advance_stage(run_id)
   227	    state = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   228	    _ok(state.state == "RUNNING" and state.state_version == 2,
   229	        f"fixture run 应为 RUNNING/v2，got {state.state}/v{state.state_version}")
   230	    return run_id, state.state_version
   231	
   232	
   233	def _all_pass_run(mgr: RunManager, task: str) -> str:
   234	    """七段全 PASSED → complete_run → SUCCEEDED（技术 PASS 的 context 表达）。"""
   235	    run_id = mgr.create_run(task, IDENT)["run_id"]
   236	    for _ in range(7):
   237	        mgr.advance_stage(run_id, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   238	                          policy_verdict="PASS")
   239	    done = mgr.complete_run(run_id)
   240	    _ok(done["final_state"] == "SUCCEEDED", f"应 SUCCEEDED，got {done}")
   241	    return run_id

   244	def _event_payloads(mgr: RunManager) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   245	    rows = mgr._db._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001 —— 测试读事件正源
   246	        "SELECT payload FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq ASC").fetchall()
   247	    return [json.loads(r[0]) for r in rows]
   248	
   249	
   250	def _count_events(mgr: RunManager, event_type: str, run_id: str) -> int:
   251	    return sum(1 for ev in _event_payloads(mgr)
   252	               if ev.get("event_type") == event_type
   253	               and ev.get("run_id") == run_id)
   254	
   255	
   256	def _assert_zero_action_side(mgr: RunManager, run_id: str, where: str) -> None:
   257	    """授权门拒绝后必须零落账：零审批消费、零 ACTION_* 事件、零 action 聚合。"""
   258	    for etype in ("APPROVAL_CONSUMED", "ACTION_AUTH_RESERVED", "ACTION_STARTED",
   259	                  "ACTION_COMMITTED", "ACTION_FAILED_UNKNOWN"):
   260	        n = _count_events(mgr, etype, run_id)
   261	        _ok(n == 0, f"{where}: {etype} 应为 0，got {n}")
   262	    _ok(not mgr.get_run(run_id).actions, f"{where}: actions 聚合应为空")
   263	    _ok(mgr.broker.consumed_approvals(run_id=run_id) == [],
   264	        f"{where}: 审批消费台账应为空")
   265	
   266	
   267	def _sign_ap(ap: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   268	    ap = dict(ap)
   269	    ap.pop("signature", None)
   270	    ap["signature"] = sign_envelope(SECRET, ap)
   271	    return ap
   272	
   273	
   274	def _make_approval(
   275	    run_id: str, task: str, state_version: int, approval_type: str, *,
   276	    stage: str = "S1_REQUIREMENT", nonce: Optional[str] = None,
   277	    payload: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, **over: Any,
   278	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   279	    """action 类 approval-v2 审批（全信封 + fixtures 密钥真 HMAC 签名）。
   280	
   281	    over 覆盖项在签名前应用（得到"签名有效但内容不符"的变体）；
   282	    签名后再篡改字段由调用方自行 dict(..., field=...) 得到。
   283	    """
   284	    ap: Dict[str, Any] = {
   285	        "schema_version": "2.0",
   286	        "approval_id": f"apr_actrun_{approval_type}_{os.urandom(4).hex()}",
   287	        "approval_type": approval_type,
   288	        "key_id": KEY_ID,
   289	        "run_id": run_id,
   290	        "task_id": task,
   291	        "stop_event_id": "n/a-action-reserve",
   292	        "stop_type": _STOP_TYPE_OF[approval_type],
   293	        "stage": stage,
   294	        "environment": IDENT["environment"],
   295	        "authorized_scope": IDENT["scope_hash"],
   296	        "policy_hash": IDENT["policy_hash"],
   297	        "ruleset_hash": IDENT["ruleset_hash"],
   298	        "input_watermark": IDENT["commit_sha"],
   299	        "expected_state_version": state_version,
   300	        "actor": "approver-human",
   301	        "issued_at": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z",
   302	        "expires_at": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z",
   303	        "nonce": nonce or f"nonce-actrun-{approval_type}-{os.urandom(8).hex()}",
   304	        "decision": "approve",
   305	        "signature": "",
   306	        "payload": dict(payload if payload is not None
   307	                        else _PAYLOADS[approval_type]),
   308	    }
   309	    ap.update(over)
   310	    return _sign_ap(ap)
   311	
   312	
   313	def _make_receipt(
   314	    action: Dict[str, Any], run_id: str, *, watermark: Optional[str] = None,
   315	    outcome: str = "COMMITTED", external_ref: str = "ext-fact-001",
   316	    **over: Any,
   317	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   318	    """外部正源回执（action-execution-receipt v1.0 + fixtures 密钥 HMAC）。
   319	
   320	    action 传 reserve_action 的返回 dict（action_id/action_type/
   321	    action_target 一致性绑定）。over 在签名前应用。
   322	    """
   323	    rc: Dict[str, Any] = {
   324	        "schema_version": "1.0",
   325	        "receipt_type": "action-execution-receipt",
   326	        "action_id": action["action_id"],
   327	        "run_id": run_id,
   328	        "action_type": action["action_type"],
   329	        "action_target": action["action_target"],
   330	        "watermark": watermark if watermark is not None else IDENT["commit_sha"],
   331	        "outcome": outcome,
   332	        "external_ref": external_ref,
   333	        "ts": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z",
   334	        "key_id": KEY_ID,
   335	        "signature": "",
   336	    }
   337	    rc.update(over)
   338	    rc = dict(rc)
   339	    rc.pop("signature", None)
   340	    rc["signature"] = sign_envelope(SECRET, rc)
   341	    return rc
   342	
   343	
   344	def _copy_events_to_new_store(src_store: EventStore, dst_path: Path) -> EventStore:
   345	    """仅复制 pipeline_events 到新库（模拟 outbox/事件正源的崩溃恢复）。"""
   346	    dst = EventStore(str(dst_path))
   347	    rows = src_store._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001
   348	        "SELECT event_id, payload, prev_event_hash, event_hash, created_at "
   349	        "FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq ASC").fetchall()
   350	    dst._conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")  # noqa: SLF001
   351	    for row in rows:
   352	        dst._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001
   353	            "INSERT INTO pipeline_events (event_id, payload, prev_event_hash, "
   354	            "event_hash, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", row)
   355	    dst._conn.execute("COMMIT")  # noqa: SLF001
   356	    return dst
   357	
   358	
   359	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   360	# ACT-01｜注入：technical PASS，但无 exact merge/release action authorization
   361	#         期望：context 可表技术 PASS，controller 不动作
   362	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   363	def test_ACT_01_technical_pass_without_exact_authorization_controller_holds(
   364	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   365	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   366	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   367	
   368	        # -- 注入半边：run 达成 technical PASS（context 侧可表达） --
   369	        run_a = _all_pass_run(mgr, "task_act01_pass")
   370	        state_a = mgr.get_run(run_a)
   371	        _ok(state_a.state == "SUCCEEDED" and state_a.terminal,
   372	            "技术 PASS 必须可表达为 SUCCEEDED 终判（context 可表技术 PASS）")
   373	        # controller 半边：无 exact action authorization → 零动作
   374	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run_a, "technical PASS run")
   375	        # 无授权预留 → ActionError；即使带上合法签发的 merge 审批，
   376	        # 技术 PASS run 已终态——controller 依旧不动作（TerminalRunError）
   377	        target = "repo://erp-main/pr-42"
   378	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.reserve_action, run_a, "merge",
   379	                            target)
   380	        _ok("requires" in str(exc) or "审批" in str(exc),
   381	            f"无授权预留必须拒绝：{exc}")
   382	        good_merge = _make_approval(run_a, "task_act01_pass",
   383	                                    state_a.state_version, "merge")
   384	        _expect_raise(PipelineError, mgr.reserve_action, run_a, "merge",
   385	                      target, good_merge)
   386	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run_a, "terminal reserve attempt 后")
   387	
   388	        # -- 同一 run 非终态对照组：technical 绿 ≠ 动作授权，仍需 exact 审批 --
   389	        run_b = _all_pass_run(mgr, "task_act01_live")
   390	        # run_b 已终态；另建一个停在 S1 RUNNING 的活 run 验证 controller 门
   391	        run_c, v_c = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act01_live2")
   392	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.reserve_action, run_c, "release",
   393	                      "prod://erp/rel-77")
   394	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run_c, "活 run 无授权预留后")
   395	
   396	        # exact authorization 到位后 controller 才动作（精确授权半边，非全面禁止）
   397	        rel_ap = _make_approval(run_c, "task_act01_live2", v_c, "release")
   398	        r = mgr.reserve_action(run_c, "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", rel_ap)
   399	        _ok(r["action_state"] == "RESERVED", "exact 授权预留应成功")
   400	        _ok(_count_events(mgr, "APPROVAL_CONSUMED", run_c) == 1,
   401	            "预留成功应恰消费一次授权")
   402	        _ok(_count_events(mgr, "ACTION_AUTH_RESERVED", run_c) == 1,
   403	            "预留成功应恰落一条 RESERVED 事件")
   404	        # run_a/run_b（technical PASS）始终零动作
   405	        for rid in (run_a, run_b):
   406	            _ok(_count_events(mgr, "APPROVAL_CONSUMED", rid) == 0
   407	                and _count_events(mgr, "ACTION_AUTH_RESERVED", rid) == 0,
   408	                f"technical PASS run {rid} controller 不得动作")
   409	
   410	
   411	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   412	# ACT-02｜注入：action、method、head/merge-group、artifact/env 任一不符
   413	#         期望：Action Authorization Gate failure，拒绝动作
   414	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   415	def test_ACT_02_any_mismatch_fails_action_authorization_gate(
   416	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   417	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   418	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   419	        run_id, v = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act02")
   420	        target = "repo://erp-main/pr-42"
   421	
   422	        # 腿 1（action 不符）：ddl 审批喂 prod-write action → 类型映射拒绝
   423	        wrong_type = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "ddl")
   424	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   425	                            "prod-write", "db://prod/erp#batch-1", wrong_type)
   426	        _ok("approval_type" in str(exc), f"类型不符拒绝：{exc}")
   427	
   428	        # 腿 2（action 词表外）：未知 action_type 携带合法 release 审批 → 拒
   429	        rel_ap = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "release")
   430	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.reserve_action, run_id, "deploy",
   431	                            "prod://erp/deploy-9", rel_ap)
   432	        _ok("unknown action_type" in str(exc), f"未知 action 拒绝：{exc}")
   433	
   434	        # 腿 3（method 不符）：merge 审批签名后篡改 merge_method → HMAC 拒
   435	        tampered_method = dict(_make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "merge"))
   436	        tampered_method["payload"] = dict(tampered_method["payload"],
   437	                                          merge_method="squash")
   438	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   439	                            "merge", target, tampered_method)
   440	        _ok("signature" in str(exc), f"method 篡改必须验签失败：{exc}")
   441	
   442	        # 腿 4（head/merge-group 不符）：水印非本 run head → 绑定拒绝
   443	        wrong_head = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "merge",
   444	                                    input_watermark="b" * 40)
   445	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   446	                            "merge", target, wrong_head)
   447	        _ok("commit_sha" in str(exc) or "input_watermark" in str(exc),
   448	            f"head 不符拒绝：{exc}")
   449	        # 非 40-hex 水印（结构层即拒，不存在跳过比较的旁路）
   450	        bad_shape = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "merge",
   451	                                   input_watermark="not-a-sha")
   452	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   453	                            "merge", target, bad_shape)
   454	        _ok("40-hex" in str(exc), f"水印形状拒绝：{exc}")
   455	
   456	        # 腿 5（artifact 不符）：release 审批签名后篡改 artifact_sha256 → HMAC 拒
   457	        tampered_artifact = dict(_make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v,
   458	                                                "release"))
   459	        tampered_artifact["payload"] = dict(
   460	            tampered_artifact["payload"], artifact_sha256="f" * 64)
   461	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   462	                            "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", tampered_artifact)
   463	        _ok("signature" in str(exc), f"artifact 篡改必须验签失败：{exc}")
   464	
   465	        # 腿 6（env 不符）：签名的 release 审批 environment=production（异环境）→ 拒
   466	        wrong_env = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act02", v, "release",
   467	                                   environment="production")
   468	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   469	                            "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", wrong_env)
   470	        _ok("environment" in str(exc), f"env 不符拒绝：{exc}")
   471	
   472	        # 期望半边：Action Authorization Gate failure —— 全部拒绝且零落账
   473	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run_id, "六条不符注入后")
   474	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "拒绝路径不得留任何事件（链完好）")
   475	
   476	
   477	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   478	# ACT-03｜注入：check 发布前/后 crash 或回执丢失
   479	#         期望：outbox 幂等恢复；同一 check_run 更新，不产生伪双绿
   480	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   481	def test_ACT_03_outbox_idempotent_recovery_no_double_green(
   482	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   483	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   484	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   485	        run_id, v = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act03")
   486	        ap = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act03", v, "release")
   487	        reserved = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", ap)
   488	        aid = reserved["action_id"]
   489	        mgr.start_action(run_id, aid, receipt="publish attempt 1")
   490	
   491	        # -- 腿 1（发布后 crash → 事件流恢复）：仅复制事件正源重建管理器，
   492	        #    重放必须还原同一动作面（outbox 幂等恢复），同 nonce 不得二次占位
   493	        recovered_store = _copy_events_to_new_store(
   494	            store, td / f"recovered-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.db")
   495	        mgr_rc = RunManager(recovered_store, keyring=KEYRING)
   496	        st_rc = mgr_rc.get_run(run_id)
   497	        _ok(st_rc.actions[aid]["action_state"] == "STARTED",
   498	            "崩溃恢复重放应还原 STARTED")
   499	        _ok(st_rc.actions[aid]["receipt_status"] == RECEIPT_PROVISIONAL,
   500	            "恢复重放应还原 PROVISIONAL 分级")
   501	        _ok(st_rc.consumed_nonce_types, "恢复重放应还原授权消费链")
   502	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalReuseError, mgr_rc.reserve_action, run_id,
   503	                            "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", ap)
   504	        _ok("不可复用" in str(exc), f"恢复后同授权不得再预留：{exc}")
   505	
   506	        # -- 腿 2（at-least-once 重投）：同一 sealed outbox 事件二次 append
   507	        #    → event_id 幂等键命中，零新行、重放不变（不产生第二个绿）
   508	        started_payload = next(
   509	            ev for ev in _event_payloads(mgr)
   510	            if ev.get("event_type") == "ACTION_STARTED")
   511	        before_rows = mgr._db._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001
   512	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events").fetchone()[0]
   513	        from wenqu_core.wenqu_pipeline import _PipelineDb  # noqa: E402
   514	        _h2, inserted = _PipelineDb.append_event(
   515	            mgr._db._conn, dict(started_payload))  # noqa: SLF001
   516	        after_rows = mgr._db._conn.execute(  # noqa: SLF001
   517	            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events").fetchone()[0]
   518	        _ok(inserted is False, "重复投递同一 outbox 事件必须被幂等忽略")
   519	        _ok(before_rows == after_rows, "幂等忽略不得新增事件行")
   520	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run_id).actions[aid]["action_state"] == "STARTED",
   521	            "重复投递后重放状态不变")
   522	
   523	        # -- 腿 3（同一 check/action 更新，不产生伪双绿）：先查询外部事实，
   524	        #    COMMITTED 恰一次；重复 commit / 重复 start 全拒
   525	        good = _make_receipt(reserved, run_id)
   526	        first = mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, aid, good)
   527	        again = mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, aid, good)
   528	        _ok(first["reconciled"] is True and again["reconciled"] is True,
   529	            "同一回执重复对账幂等（同一 check_run 更新）")
   530	        mgr.commit_action(run_id, aid, good)
   531	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, aid, good)
   532	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.start_action, run_id, aid)
   533	        _ok(_count_events(mgr, "ACTION_COMMITTED", run_id) == 1,
   534	            "ACTION_COMMITTED 必须恰一条（无伪双绿）")
   535	        mgr_after = RunManager(store, keyring=KEYRING)
   536	        st_after = mgr_after.get_run(run_id)
   537	        _ok(st_after.actions[aid]["action_state"] == "COMMITTED"
   538	            and st_after.actions[aid]["receipt_status"] == RECEIPT_RECONCILED,
   539	            "最终重放：恰一次 COMMITTED 且为 RECONCILED 分级")
   540	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "outbox 事件链完好")
   541	
   542	        # -- 腿 4（发布前 crash 的中间态守卫）：授权消费与 RESERVED 必须同事务
   543	        #    原子成对——伪造"无 APPROVAL_CONSUMED 的 RESERVED"直插事件流，
   544	        #    重放即 Corruption（outbox 恢复不接受断链中间态）
   545	        from wenqu_core.wenqu_pipeline import _seal_event  # noqa: E402
   546	        run2, v2 = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act03_guard")
   547	        st2 = mgr.get_run(run2)
   548	        forged = mgr._base_event(  # noqa: SLF001
   549	            "ACTION_AUTH_RESERVED", st2, "COMPLETED", "NOT_EVALUATED",
   550	            actor="attacker", state_version=st2.state_version,
   551	            action_id="act_forge_act03", action_type="merge",
   552	            action_target="repo://erp-main/pr-1",
   553	            approval_id="apr_actrun_merge_forged",
   554	            nonce_digest=hashlib.sha256(b"nonce-forged-act03").hexdigest())
   555	        _PipelineDb.append_event(mgr._db._conn,  # noqa: SLF001
   556	                                 _seal_event(run2, forged))
   557	        _expect_raise(PipelineCorruptionError, mgr.get_run, run2)
   558	
   559	
   560	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   561	# ACT-04｜注入：merge/release 调用前/后 crash 或回执丢失
   562	#         期望：先查询外部事实；COMMITTED 或 FAILED_UNKNOWN；绝不盲重试
   563	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   564	def test_ACT_04_query_external_fact_committed_or_failed_unknown(
   565	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   566	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   567	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   568	        run_id, v = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act04")
   569	
   570	        # -- 动作 1（release）：调用发出后回执丢失 → 先查询外部事实（对账不了）
   571	        #    → FAILED_UNKNOWN 终态；绝不盲重试（nonce 已消费、终态封死）
   572	        ap1 = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act04", v, "release")
   573	        a1 = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", ap1)
   574	        mgr.start_action(run_id, a1["action_id"],
   575	                         receipt="call issued; receipt lost in crash")
   576	        lost = _make_receipt(a1, run_id)          # 从未到达的回执形态
   577	        lost["signature"] = "0" * 64              # 不可验签 → 查询外部事实失败
   578	        rec = mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, a1["action_id"], lost)
   579	        _ok(rec["reconciled"] is False,
   580	            "回执丢失/不可验签时查询外部事实必须判对账不了")
   581	        _ok(mgr.get_run(run_id).actions[a1["action_id"]]["action_state"]
   582	            == "STARTED", "对账不了不得篡改动作状态")
   583	        f = mgr.fail_action(run_id, a1["action_id"], "receipt lost after call")
   584	        _ok(f["action_state"] == "FAILED_UNKNOWN",
   585	            "对账不了必须收口 FAILED_UNKNOWN（绝不猜 COMMITTED）")
   586	        # 绝不盲重试：终态后任何推进全拒；同一授权不得重新发起调用
   587	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.start_action, run_id, a1["action_id"])
   588	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, a1["action_id"],
   589	                      _make_receipt(a1, run_id))
   590	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.fail_action, run_id, a1["action_id"])
   591	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalReuseError, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   592	                            "release", "prod://erp/rel-77", ap1)
   593	        _ok("不可复用" in str(exc), f"同授权重发调用必须拒绝：{exc}")
   594	
   595	        # -- 动作 2（merge）：外部事实声明 COMMITTED → 查询通过后恰一次落账
   596	        v_now = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   597	        ap2 = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act04", v_now, "merge")
   598	        a2 = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "merge", "repo://erp-main/pr-42", ap2)
   599	        mgr.start_action(run_id, a2["action_id"], receipt="merge issued")
   600	        fact2 = _make_receipt(a2, run_id, outcome="COMMITTED",
   601	                              external_ref="gh-merge-42")
   602	        rec2 = mgr.reconcile_receipt(run_id, a2["action_id"], fact2)
   603	        _ok(rec2["reconciled"] is True and rec2["outcome"] == "COMMITTED",
   604	            "外部正源声明 COMMITTED → 先查询后落账")
   605	        c2 = mgr.commit_action(run_id, a2["action_id"], fact2)
   606	        _ok(c2["action_state"] == "COMMITTED"
   607	            and c2["receipt_status"] == RECEIPT_RECONCILED,
   608	            "凭外部事实落 COMMITTED（RECONCILED 分级）")
   609	
   610	        # -- 动作 3（ddl）：外部事实声明 FAILED → 不得 COMMITTED，
   611	        #    走 fail_action → FAILED_UNKNOWN
   612	        v_now = mgr.get_run(run_id).state_version
   613	        ap3 = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act04", v_now, "ddl")
   614	        a3 = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "ddl", "db://staging/erp", ap3)
   615	        mgr.start_action(run_id, a3["action_id"])
   616	        fact3 = _make_receipt(a3, run_id, outcome="FAILED",
   617	                              external_ref="ddl-exec-9")
   618	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id,
   619	                            a3["action_id"], fact3)
   620	        _ok("FAILED_UNKNOWN" in str(exc),
   621	            f"外部声明 FAILED 不得 COMMITTED：{exc}")
   622	        _ok(mgr.fail_action(run_id, a3["action_id"])[
   623	                "action_state"] == "FAILED_UNKNOWN", "FAILED → FAILED_UNKNOWN")
   624	
   625	        # -- 重放终态面：三动作全部闭口于 {FAILED_UNKNOWN, COMMITTED}，
   626	        #    无悬挂 STARTED/RESERVED（调用后必收口，绝不悬置盲等）
   627	        st = RunManager(store, keyring=KEYRING).get_run(run_id)
   628	        states = {aid_: act["action_state"] for aid_, act in st.actions.items()}
   629	        _ok(states[a1["action_id"]] == "FAILED_UNKNOWN"
   630	            and states[a2["action_id"]] == "COMMITTED"
   631	            and states[a3["action_id"]] == "FAILED_UNKNOWN",
   632	            f"saga 终态面不符：{states}")
   633	        _ok(all(s in ("COMMITTED", "FAILED_UNKNOWN")
   634	                for s in states.values()), f"不得残留非终态动作：{states}")
   635	
   636	
   637	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   638	# ACT-05｜注入：外部陈旧绿、dispatcher/App 失联
   639	#         期望：direct merge 被角色/ruleset 拒绝；动作保持 BLOCKED
   640	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   641	def test_ACT_05_stale_green_or_dispatcher_loss_keeps_action_blocked(
   642	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   643	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   644	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   645	        run_id, v = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act05")
   646	        ap = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act05", v, "merge")
   647	        reserved = mgr.reserve_action(run_id, "merge",
   648	                                      "repo://erp-main/pr-42", ap)
   649	        aid = reserved["action_id"]
   650	        mgr.start_action(run_id, aid, receipt="merge started; awaiting receipt")
   651	
   652	        # -- 腿 1（外部陈旧绿）：旧 SHA 的验签回执 → 拒绝；动作保持 BLOCKED
   653	        stale = _make_receipt(reserved, run_id, watermark="b" * 40,
   654	                              external_ref="gh-merge-OLD")
   655	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, aid, stale)
   656	        _ok("watermark" in str(exc), f"陈旧水印回执必须拒绝：{exc}")
   657	        # 跨 run 移花接木回执 → 同样拒绝
   658	        other_run, _ = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act05_other")
   659	        cross = _make_receipt(reserved, other_run)
   660	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, aid, cross)
   661	        _ok("run" in str(exc), f"跨 run 回执必须拒绝：{exc}")
   662	
   663	        # -- 腿 2（dispatcher/App 失联，回执不可得）：自报字符串/伪签名回执
   664	        #    全拒——动作停留 STARTED+PROVISIONAL（BLOCKED，不可 COMMITTED）
   665	        _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, aid,
   666	                      "receipt:self-reported:ok")
   667	        forged = _make_receipt(reserved, run_id)
   668	        forged["signature"] = "0" * 64
   669	        exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, mgr.commit_action, run_id, aid,
   670	                            forged)
   671	        _ok("signature" in str(exc) or "验签" in str(exc),
   672	            f"伪签名回执必须拒绝：{exc}")
   673	        st = mgr.get_run(run_id)
   674	        _ok(st.actions[aid]["action_state"] == "STARTED",
   675	            "失联/陈旧绿下动作必须保持 BLOCKED（STARTED 非终态）")
   676	        _ok(st.actions[aid]["receipt_status"] == RECEIPT_PROVISIONAL,
   677	            "回执分级必须停留 PROVISIONAL，不得自宣 RECONCILED")
   678	        _ok(_count_events(mgr, "ACTION_COMMITTED", run_id) == 0,
   679	            "不得产生任何 COMMITTED 事件（伪绿为零）")
   680	
   681	        # -- 腿 3（direct merge 被角色/ruleset 拒绝）：ruleset/policy 不符的
   682	        #    授权在 reserve 门即拒（零动作、零消费）
   683	        run2, v2 = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act05_rules")
   684	        bad_ruleset = _make_approval(run2, "task_act05_rules", v2, "merge",
   685	                                     ruleset_hash="rules-EVIL-unapproved")
   686	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run2,
   687	                            "merge", "repo://erp-main/pr-43", bad_ruleset)
   688	        _ok("ruleset" in str(exc), f"ruleset 不符必须拒绝：{exc}")
   689	        bad_policy = _make_approval(run2, "task_act05_rules", v2, "merge",
   690	                                    policy_hash="policy-EVIL-unapproved")
   691	        exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run2,
   692	                            "merge", "repo://erp-main/pr-43", bad_policy)
   693	        _ok("policy" in str(exc), f"policy（角色面）不符必须拒绝：{exc}")
   694	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run2, "direct merge 被拒后")
   695	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "拒绝路径链完好")
   696	
   697	
   698	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   699	# ACT-06｜注入：merge/release/DDL/prod-write payload 使用错误 discriminator
   700	#         或缺专属字段
   701	#         期望：schema 拒绝
   702	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   703	def test_ACT_06_wrong_discriminator_or_missing_fields_schema_rejected(
   704	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   705	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   706	        store, mgr = _fresh(td)
   707	        run_id, v = _s1_running_run(mgr, "task_act06")
   708	
   709	        # (类型, 缺失专属字段, 错误 discriminator 的来源类型)
   710	        matrix = [
   711	            ("merge", "merge_method", "release"),
   712	            ("release", "artifact_sha256", "ddl"),
   713	            ("ddl", "backup_id", "prod_write"),
   714	            ("prod_write", "idempotency_key", "merge"),
   715	        ]
   716	        for approval_type, missing_field, wrong_src in matrix:
   717	            action_type = next(
   718	                at for at, ap_ in _ACTION_TO_APPROVAL.items()
   719	                if ap_ == approval_type and "-" not in at)
   720	            target = f"tgt://{approval_type}/1"
   721	
   722	            # 注入 a（缺专属字段）：payload 少一个判别键（签名有效）→ schema 拒
   723	            short_payload = {k: val for k, val in
   724	                             _PAYLOADS[approval_type].items()
   725	                             if k != missing_field}
   726	            ap_missing = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act06", v,
   727	                                        approval_type, payload=short_payload)
   728	            exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   729	                                action_type, target, ap_missing)
   730	            _ok("discriminator" in str(exc) and "missing" in str(exc),
   731	                f"{approval_type} 缺 {missing_field} 必须 schema 拒绝：{exc}")
   732	
   733	            # 注入 b（错误 discriminator）：把他类 payload 塞进本类型信封
   734	            # （签名有效）→ 键集封闭拒绝
   735	            foreign_payload = dict(_PAYLOADS[wrong_src])
   736	            ap_foreign = _make_approval(run_id, "task_act06", v,
   737	                                        approval_type, payload=foreign_payload)
   738	            exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, mgr.reserve_action, run_id,
   739	                                action_type, target, ap_foreign)
   740	            _ok("not closed" in str(exc) or "foreign" in str(exc),
   741	                f"{approval_type} 载 {wrong_src} payload 必须键集封闭拒绝：{exc}")
   742	
   743	        # 期望半边：四类型 × 两形态共 8 次注入全部 schema 拒绝，零落账
   744	        _assert_zero_action_side(mgr, run_id, "ACT-06 全部注入后")
   745	        _ok(store.verify_chain()["ok"], "schema 拒绝不得产生事件")
   746	
   747	
   748	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   749	# RUN-01｜注入：被测程序读取 Keychain/SSH agent/生产配置
   750	#         期望：隔离拒绝
   751	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   752	def test_RUN_01_secret_env_channels_isolation_denied(
   753	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   754	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   755	        # 伪造敏感通道：SSH agent socket 文件 + 生产配置文件（含哨兵内容）
   756	        agent_sock = td / "agent.sock"
   757	        agent_sock.write_text("FAKE-AGENT-ENDPOINT", encoding="utf-8")
   758	        prod_cfg = td / "prod-secrets.yml"
   759	        prod_cfg.write_text("DB_PASSWORD: sentinel-prod-secret\n",
   760	                            encoding="utf-8")
   761	        out = td / "env-report.json"
   762	        probe_code = (
   763	            "import os, json\n"
   764	            f"report = {{\n"
   765	            f"  'env_keys': sorted(os.environ.keys()),\n"
   766	            f"  'ssh_agent_sock': os.environ.get('SSH_AUTH_SOCK'),\n"
   767	            f"  'prod_config_path': os.environ.get('WQ_PROD_CONFIG'),\n"
   768	            f"  'prod_config_content': (lambda p: open(p).read()\n"
   769	            f"      if p and os.path.exists(p) else 'NOT-REACHABLE')(\n"
   770	            f"          os.environ.get('WQ_PROD_CONFIG')),\n"
   771	            f"}}\n"
   772	            f"open({str(out)!r}, 'w').write(json.dumps(report))\n"
   773	        )
   774	        old_ssh = os.environ.get("SSH_AUTH_SOCK")
   775	        old_cfg = os.environ.get("WQ_PROD_CONFIG")
   776	        os.environ["SSH_AUTH_SOCK"] = str(agent_sock)
   777	        os.environ["WQ_PROD_CONFIG"] = str(prod_cfg)
   778	        try:
   779	            ev = TrustedRunner().run([sys.executable, "-c", probe_code])
   780	            _ok(ev.actual_exit_code == 0 and not ev.timed_out,
   781	                f"探针应正常执行：exit={ev.actual_exit_code}")
   782	            report = json.loads(out.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   783	            keys = set(report["env_keys"])
   784	            # 期望：隔离拒绝——敏感环境通道在子进程中不存在，配置不可达
   785	            _ok("SSH_AUTH_SOCK" not in keys,
   786	                "SSH agent 通道变量必须被白名单剥除（隔离拒绝）")
   787	            _ok("WQ_PROD_CONFIG" not in keys,
   788	                "生产配置路径变量必须被白名单剥除（隔离拒绝）")
   789	            _ok(report["ssh_agent_sock"] is None,
   790	                "子进程不得看到 SSH agent socket 路径")
   791	            _ok(report["prod_config_path"] is None,
   792	                "子进程不得看到生产配置路径")
   793	            _ok(report["prod_config_content"] == "NOT-REACHABLE",
   794	                "生产配置内容必须不可达（哨兵内容零泄露）")
   795	            _ok("sentinel-prod-secret" not in json.dumps(report),
   796	                "哨兵秘密不得经任何路径进入子进程观测面")
   797	            # 控制腿：白名单基础变量仍在（隔离≠破坏正常执行）
   798	            _ok({"PATH", "HOME"} <= keys, f"白名单基础变量应保留：{keys}")
   799	            _ok(RUN_TOKEN_ENV in keys, "内部 run token 哨兵应在位（扫杀锚点）")
   800	        finally:
   801	            if old_ssh is None:
   802	                os.environ.pop("SSH_AUTH_SOCK", None)
   803	            else:
   804	                os.environ["SSH_AUTH_SOCK"] = old_ssh
   805	            if old_cfg is None:
   806	                os.environ.pop("WQ_PROD_CONFIG", None)
   807	            else:
   808	                os.environ["WQ_PROD_CONFIG"] = old_cfg
   809	
   810	
   811	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   812	# RUN-02｜注入：timeout 后子孙进程
   813	#         期望：全部终止，无孤儿写盘
   814	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   815	_RUN02_ATTACK = """import os, time
   816	DIR = {dir!r}
   817	def delayed(path):
   818	    time.sleep(3)
   819	    with open(path, 'w') as fh:
   820	        fh.write('orphan survived')
   821	
   822	# 形态1：同进程组子进程（killpg 目标面）
   823	p1 = os.fork()
   824	if p1 == 0:
   825	    delayed(DIR + '/M_GROUP_CHILD'); os._exit(0)
   826	# 形态2：setsid 逃逸进程组 + 中间父退出 → 孙进程被收养（进程树快照漏）
   827	p2 = os.fork()
   828	if p2 == 0:
   829	    os.setsid()
   830	    g = os.fork()
   831	    if g == 0:
   832	        delayed(DIR + '/M_SETSID_GRANDCHILD'); os._exit(0)
   833	    os._exit(0)
   834	# 形态3：setsid + double-fork daemon → 被 PID 1 收养（token 扫杀目标面）
   835	p3 = os.fork()
   836	if p3 == 0:
   837	    os.setsid()
   838	    d = os.fork()
   839	    if d == 0:
   840	        delayed(DIR + '/M_DAEMON')
   841	        time.sleep(30); os._exit(0)
   842	    os._exit(0)
   843	time.sleep(60)
   844	"""
   845	
   846	
   847	def _kill_survivors(script_path: Path) -> None:
   848	    """失败兜底：清掉仍引用攻击脚本的进程，避免污染宿主机。"""
   849	    import signal
   850	    try:
   851	        proc = subprocess.run(["ps", "-axo", "pid=,command="],
   852	                              capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
   853	    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
   854	        return
   855	    for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
   856	        if str(script_path) in line:
   857	            pid = line.split()[0]
   858	            if pid.isdigit() and int(pid) != os.getpid():
   859	                try:
   860	                    os.kill(int(pid), signal.SIGKILL)
   861	                except (ProcessLookupError, PermissionError):
   862	                    pass
   863	
   864	
   865	def test_RUN_02_timeout_kills_all_descendants_no_orphan_writes(
   866	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   867	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   868	        script = td / f"attack_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.py"
   869	        script.write_text(_RUN02_ATTACK.format(dir=str(td)),
   870	                          encoding="utf-8")
   871	        markers = [td / "M_GROUP_CHILD", td / "M_SETSID_GRANDCHILD",
   872	                   td / "M_DAEMON"]
   873	        try:
   874	            ev = TrustedRunner(timeout=1.0).run([sys.executable, str(script)])
   875	            _ok(ev.timed_out is True, "超时必须被如实记录（证据不丢）")
   876	            _ok(ev.actual_exit_code is None,
   877	                f"超时证据的退出码应为 None：{ev}")
   878	            time.sleep(3.6)  # 覆盖三个孙进程 t+3s 的写盘窗口
   879	            survived = [m.name for m in markers if m.exists()]
   880	            _ok(not survived, f"孤儿写盘被实锤：{survived}")
   881	            # 全部终止：不得残留任何引用攻击脚本的进程
   882	            proc = subprocess.run(["ps", "-axo", "pid=,command="],
   883	                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
   884	            leftovers = [ln.strip() for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()
   885	                         if str(script) in ln]
   886	            _ok(not leftovers, f"存活的子孙进程：{leftovers}")
   887	        finally:
   888	            _kill_survivors(script)
   889	
   890	
   891	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   892	# RUN-03｜注入：fork bomb/输出爆量/网络外连
   893	#         期望：配额/allowlist 阻断
   894	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   895	def test_RUN_03_fork_bomb_blast_network_blocked_by_allowlist(
   896	        root: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
   897	    with _tmp(root) as td:
   898	        runner = TrustedRunner(allowlist=["/bin/echo"])
   899	
   900	        def _blocked(name: str, argv: List[str], marker: Path) -> None:
   901	            exc = _expect_raise(PermissionError, runner.run, argv)
   902	            expected_msg = f"executable not in allowlist: {argv[0]!r}"
   903	            _ok(str(exc) == expected_msg,
   904	                f"{name} 阻断消息不符：{exc!r} != {expected_msg!r}")
   905	            _ok(not marker.exists(),
   906	                f"{name} 载荷必须零执行（marker 不得落盘）")
   907	
   908	        # 注入 a（fork bomb 形态）：受限 fork 循环载荷——allowlist 未列名
   909	        # python → 前置阻断，零进程产生
   910	        m_bomb = td / "BOMB_RAN"
   911	        bomb_code = (
   912	            f"open({str(m_bomb)!r}, 'w').write('boom')\n"
   913	            "import os, time\n"
   914	            "for _ in range(5):\n"
   915	            "    if os.fork() == 0:\n"
   916	            "        time.sleep(30); os._exit(0)\n"
   917	            "time.sleep(30)\n"
   918	        )
   919	        _blocked("fork bomb", [sys.executable, "-c", bomb_code], m_bomb)
   920	
   921	        # 注入 b（输出爆量形态）：stdout 灌爆载荷——同样被 allowlist 阻断
   922	        m_blast = td / "BLAST_RAN"
   923	        blast_code = (
   924	            f"open({str(m_blast)!r}, 'w').write('blast')\n"
   925	            "import sys\n"
   926	            "for _ in range(1024):\n"
   927	            "    sys.stdout.write('A' * 1048576)\n"
   928	        )
   929	        _blocked("输出爆量", [sys.executable, "-c", blast_code], m_blast)
   930	
   931	        # 注入 c（网络外连形态）：urllib 外传载荷——allowlist 阻断于执行前
   932	        m_net = td / "NET_RAN"
   933	        net_code = (
   934	            f"open({str(m_net)!r}, 'w').write('dial')\n"
   935	            "import urllib.request\n"
   936	            "urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:1/exfil')\n"
   937	        )
   938	        _blocked("网络外连", [sys.executable, "-c", net_code], m_net)
   939	
   940	        # 注入 d（外部二进制形态）：仓外网络工具不在 allowlist → 阻断
   941	        curl = shutil.which("curl")
   942	        if curl is not None:
   943	            m_bin = td / "CURL_RAN"
   944	            _blocked("外部网络二进制", [curl, "http://127.0.0.1:1/"],
   945	                     m_bin)
   946	
   947	        # 控制腿：allowlist 内的合法执行不受影响（阻断≠全禁）
   948	        ev = runner.run(["/bin/echo", "allowlist-ok"])
   949	        _ok(ev.actual_exit_code == 0 and not ev.timed_out,
   950	            f"allowlist 内执行应正常：{ev}")
   951	
   952	
   953	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   954	# 未实现注入子面登记（诚实边界；不硬凑、不 xfail）
   955	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   956	BOUNDARIES: Dict[str, str] = {
   957	    "RUN-01": (
   958	        "容器级隔离未实现：macOS Keychain 直读、SSH agent Unix socket 直连、"
   959	        "任意路径生产配置文件读不在 TrustedRunner 防线内（全仓无 sandboxexec/"
   960	        "seatbelt/文件系统边界语义）；已实现半边=env 白名单剥除——进程环境通道"
   961	        "（SSH_AUTH_SOCK/配置路径变量）在子进程中不存在，隔离拒绝以该腿验证"),
   962	    "RUN-03": (
   963	        "输出爆量字节配额（stdout 上限截断）与网络 egress allowlist 无产品实现"
   964	        "（TrustedRunner 只有可执行 allowlist opt-in 与超时击杀）；三条注入形态"
   965	        "均经验证的事实是：allowlist 未列名可执行一律 PermissionError 前置阻断、"
   966	        "零进程产生——『配额阻断』仅此腿成立"),
   967	    "ACT-03": (
   968	        "真实 GitHub check_run 发布 outbox（CI check 发布通道）不在本仓被测体"
   969	        "（无 GitHub API client）；本地等价=ActionSaga control_outbox 事件幂等"
   970	        "（event_id INSERT OR IGNORE）+ APPROVAL_CONSUMED/ACTION_AUTH_RESERVED "
   971	        "同事务原子 + 重放授权消费链守卫"),
   972	    "ACT-05": (
   973	        "dispatcher/App 失联检测（heartbeat/熔断）无独立实现；本地等价=回执"
   974	        "不可得时动作停留 STARTED/PROVISIONAL 不可 COMMITTED（BLOCKED），"
   975	        "陈旧水印/跨 run/伪签名回执全部拒绝"),
   976	}
   977	
   978	
   979	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   980	# 运行器
   981	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   982	_TEST_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^test_(ACT|RUN)_(\d{2})_")
   983	
   984	
   985	def _collect_tests() -> List[Tuple[str, Callable[..., None]]]:
   986	    return [
   987	        (name, fn)
   988	        for name, fn in sorted(globals().items())
   989	        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn)
   990	        and _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name) is not None
   991	    ]
   992	
   993	
   994	def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
   995	    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
   996	    json_out: Optional[str] = None
   997	    if "--json" in argv:
   998	        json_out = argv[argv.index("--json") + 1]
   999	    tests = _collect_tests()
  1000	    print("=" * 72)
  1001	    print("§20 ACT/RUN 族专属验收测试（实现 9 条：ACT-01~06 + RUN-01~03）")
  1002	    for tid, reason in BOUNDARIES.items():
  1003	        print(f"  BOUNDARY {tid}: {reason}")
  1004	    print("-" * 72)
  1005	    records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  1006	    failed: List[str] = []
  1007	    for name, fn in tests:
  1008	        match = _TEST_NAME_RE.match(name)
  1009	        assert match is not None
  1010	        test_id = f"{match.group(1)}-{match.group(2)}"
  1011	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ac-act-run-") as td:
  1012	            try:
  1013	                fn(Path(td))
  1014	                rec = {"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": True}
  1015	                if test_id in BOUNDARIES:
  1016	                    rec["boundaries"] = BOUNDARIES[test_id]
  1017	                records.append(rec)
  1018	                print(f"  PASS {test_id}  {name}")
  1019	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1020	                failed.append(name)
  1021	                rec = {"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": False,
  1022	                       "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
  1023	                if test_id in BOUNDARIES:
  1024	                    rec["boundaries"] = BOUNDARIES[test_id]
  1025	                records.append(rec)
  1026	                print(f"  FAIL {test_id}  {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
  1027	    print("-" * 72)
  1028	    print(f"结果: {len(records) - len(failed)}/{len(records)} passed"
  1029	          f"{'' if not failed else '；失败: ' + ', '.join(failed)}")
  1030	    out_path = Path(json_out) if json_out else ARTIFACT_PATH
  1031	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1032	    out_path.write_text(
  1033	        json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n",
  1034	        encoding="utf-8")
  1035	    print(f"逐 ID 结果已写: {out_path}")
  1036	    print("=" * 72)
  1037	    return 0 if not failed else 1
  1038	
  1039	
  1040	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1041	    raise SystemExit(main())

   131	# 基础设施

   133	PASS_N, FAIL_N = 0, 0
   134	RESULTS = []          # [(test_id, name, ok, detail_dict)]
