   136	
   137	class TmpDir:
   138	    def __init__(self, prefix):
   139	        self.prefix = prefix
   140	        self.path = None
   141	
   142	    def __enter__(self):
   143	        self.path = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=self.prefix)
   144	        return self.path
   145	
   146	    def __exit__(self, *exc):
   147	        shutil.rmtree(self.path, ignore_errors=True)
   148	
   149	
   150	def expect(exc, fn):
   151	    try:
   152	        fn()
   153	    except exc:
   154	        return
   155	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
   156	        raise AssertionError(
   157	            f"expected {exc.__name__}, got {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
   158	    raise AssertionError(f"expected {exc.__name__}, nothing raised")
   159	
   160	
   161	def raw_conn(db_path):
   162	    """打开裸 sqlite 连接做对抗性 SQL（绕过 EventStore 侧 API）。"""
   163	    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
   164	    conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=10000")
   165	    return conn
   166	
   167	
   168	def rows_of(conn, sql, args=()):
   169	    return conn.execute(sql, args).fetchall()
   170	
   171	
   172	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   173	# STATE-01：任意 stage/status → schema 拒绝
   174	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   175	def test_STATE_01_arbitrary_stage_status_schema_rejected():
   176	    """注入：任意 stage/status 组合（合法名/未知名/空/超长/含换行与穿越串）。
   177	
   178	    期望（被测体已实现面）：这些值要进事件账本只能走管线控制事件
   179	    （RUN_*/STAGE_*/ATTEMPT_*/WAITING_*/APPROVAL_*/ACTION_*/FINDING_* /
   180	    SUPERSEDED），而公共 EventStore.append 对控制类型一律 ValueError——
   181	    即 store 的 schema 边界对「任意 stage/status 的状态写入」整体拒绝；
   182	    连伪造 seal 字段也绕不过（守卫按 event_type 判定）。
   183	    """
   184	    stages = ["S1", "S7", "nonexistent-stage", "", "x" * 5000,
   185	              "../secrets", "/etc/passwd", "a\nb", "站七"]
   186	    statuses = ["PASS", "FAIL", "RUNNING", "PASSED", "WAITING", "", "WEIRD",
   187	                "x" * 5000, "PASSED\nRUNNING"]
   188	    control_types = ["STAGE_ATTEMPT_RECORDED", "STAGE_STATUS", "RUN_STATUS",
   189	                     "ATTEMPT_STATUS", "RUN_RUNNING"]
   190	    checked = 0
   191	    with TmpDir("ac-s1-") as td:
   192	        store = EventStore(os.path.join(td, "ev.db"))
   193	        try:
   194	            for etype in control_types:
   195	                for stage in stages:
   196	                    for status in statuses:
   197	                        ev = {"event_type": etype, "stage": stage,
   198	                              "status": status, "attempt": "a-1"}
   199	                        expect(ValueError, lambda ev=ev: store.append(ev))
   200	                        checked += 1
   201	            # 伪造 seal 字段也绕不过守卫（守卫只看 event_type）
   202	            for forged in ({"event_type": "STAGE_PASSED", "seal": "f" * 64},
   203	                           {"event_type": "SUPERSEDED", "seal": "0" * 64},
   204	                           {"event_type": "RUN_RUNNING", "seal": "ab"}):
   205	                expect(ValueError, lambda ev=forged: store.append(ev))
   206	                checked += 1
   207	            # 对照组：普通（非控制）事件不受影响——拒绝面精确针对状态写入路径
   208	            h, ins = store.append({"event_type": "OBSERVATION",
   209	                                   "stage": "S1", "status": "PASS",
   210	                                   "note": "ordinary event is fine"})
   211	            assert ins is True
   212	            # 任意 stage/status 没有经任何路径落库（对照组仅 1 行）
   213	            assert store.head() == (h, 1)
   214	            assert store.verify_chain()["ok"] is True
   215	        finally:
   216	            store.close()
   217	    assert checked == len(control_types) * len(stages) * len(statuses) + 3
   218	    return {"rejected_combinations": checked,
   219	            "control_types": control_types,
   220	            "boundary": "attempt 状态枚举校验在 wenqu_pipeline（本文件禁 import）"}
   221	
   222	
   223	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   224	# STATE-03：100×100 并发写 → 零丢失/重复
   225	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   226	def test_STATE_03_concurrent_100x100_no_loss_no_dup():
   227	    """注入：100 个并发 writer × 各 100 条不同事件（真实多连接 WAL 并发）。
   228	
   229	    期望：零丢失（10000 行全在）、零重复（10000 个唯一 event_id，
   230	    每条恰好 inserted=True 一次）、链仍单一线性且 verify_chain 通过。
   231	    附加子场景：20 个 writer 并发追加同一内容 → 恰好 1 行（幂等不双入账）。
   232	    """
   233	    WRITERS, PER_WRITER = 100, 100
   234	    with TmpDir("ac-s3-") as td:
   235	        db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   236	        EventStore(db).close()          # 预建 schema，再放开并发
   237	        inserted = [0] * WRITERS
   238	        errors = []
   239	
   240	        def writer(w):
   241	            try:
   242	                s = EventStore(db)
   243	                try:
   244	                    for i in range(PER_WRITER):
   245	                        _, ins = s.append({"kind": "load", "writer": w, "i": i})
   246	                        if ins:
   247	                            inserted[w] += 1
   248	                finally:
   249	                    s.close()
   250	            except Exception as e:      # noqa: BLE001
   251	                errors.append(f"writer{w}:{type(e).__name__}:{e}")
   252	
   253	        threads = [threading.Thread(target=writer, args=(w,))
   254	                   for w in range(WRITERS)]
   255	        for t in threads:
   256	            t.start()
   257	        for t in threads:
   258	            t.join()
   259	        assert not errors, f"并发写抛错（即丢失面）: {errors[:3]}"
   260	
   261	        store = EventStore(db)
   262	        try:
   263	            head, total = store.head()
   264	            assert total == WRITERS * PER_WRITER, f"丢失/缺行: {total}"
   265	            assert sum(inserted) == WRITERS * PER_WRITER, \
   266	                f"inserted 计数不符: {sum(inserted)}"
   267	            uniq_ids = rows_of(
   268	                store._conn,
   269	                "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT event_id) FROM pipeline_events")[0][0]
   270	            assert uniq_ids == WRITERS * PER_WRITER, f"重复行: {uniq_ids}"
   271	            verify = store.verify_chain()
   272	            assert verify["ok"] and verify["length"] == WRITERS * PER_WRITER
   273	            assert verify["head"] == head
   274	        finally:
   275	            store.close()
   276	
   277	        # 子场景：同内容并发重放 → 恰好 1 行、1 个 inserted=True、hash 全同
   278	        db2 = os.path.join(td, "idem.db")
   279	        EventStore(db2).close()
   280	        results, ierr = [], []
   281	
   282	        def idem_writer():
   283	            try:
   284	                s = EventStore(db2)
   285	                try:
   286	                    results.append(s.append({"same": "retry-payload", "n": 1}))
   287	                finally:
   288	                    s.close()
   289	            except Exception as e:      # noqa: BLE001
   290	                ierr.append(type(e).__name__)
   291	
   292	        threads = [threading.Thread(target=idem_writer) for _ in range(20)]
   293	        for t in threads:
   294	            t.start()
   295	        for t in threads:
   296	            t.join()
   297	        assert not ierr, f"幂等并发抛错: {ierr}"
   298	        assert sum(1 for _, ins in results if ins) == 1
   299	        assert len({h for h, _ in results}) == 1
   300	        s2 = EventStore(db2)
   301	        try:
   302	            assert s2.head()[1] == 1
   303	        finally:
   304	            s2.close()
   305	    return {"writers": WRITERS, "per_writer": PER_WRITER,
   306	            "total_rows": WRITERS * PER_WRITER,
   307	            "distinct_event_ids": WRITERS * PER_WRITER,
   308	            "chain_verified": True,
   309	            "same_content_concurrent_rows": 1}
   310	
   311	
   312	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   313	# STATE-04：事务各点 kill -9 → 全有或全无
   314	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   315	# 子进程脚本：包装 sqlite3.connect 拦截 execute，在事务的确定性点位对自身发
   316	# SIGKILL（真 kill -9，不是异常模拟）；timer 模式在随机延时后自杀。
   317	_KILL_CHILD_SRC = r'''
   318	import os, signal, sys, threading
   319	import sqlite3
   320	import wenqu_core.store as st
   321	
   322	db, mode, pre, delay = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), float(sys.argv[4])
   323	
   324	class WrapConn:
   325	    def __init__(self, real):
   326	        self._real = real
   327	        self._begins = 0
   328	    def execute(self, sql, *a):
   329	        cur = self._real.execute(sql, *a)
   330	        up = sql.upper()
   331	        if up.startswith("BEGIN"):
   332	            self._begins += 1
   333	            if self._begins > pre and mode == "after_begin":
   334	                os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)
   335	        elif mode == "after_insert" and up.startswith("INSERT") and self._begins > pre:
   336	            os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)
   337	        elif mode == "after_commit" and up.startswith("COMMIT") and self._begins > pre:
   338	            os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)
   339	        return cur
   340	    def __getattr__(self, name):
   341	        return getattr(self._real, name)
   342	
   343	_orig = sqlite3.connect
   344	sqlite3.connect = lambda *a, **kw: WrapConn(_orig(*a, **kw))
   345	
   346	if mode == "timer":
   347	    threading.Timer(delay, lambda: os.kill(os.getpid(), signal.SIGKILL)).start()
   348	
   349	s = st.EventStore(db)
   350	for i in range(pre):
   351	    s.append({"pre": i})
   352	if mode == "timer":
   353	    for i in range(50):
   354	        s.append({"batch": i})
   355	else:
   356	    s.append({"doomed": True})
   357	print("survived")
   358	'''
   359	
   360	
   361	def _run_kill_child(db, mode, pre, delay=0.0):
   362	    env = dict(os.environ)
   363	    env["PYTHONPATH"] = SYSTEM_DIR + os.pathsep + env.get("PYTHONPATH", "")
   364	    return subprocess.run(
   365	        [sys.executable, "-c", _KILL_CHILD_SRC, db, mode, str(pre), str(delay)],
   366	        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, env=env)
   367	
   368	
   369	def test_STATE_04_kill9_all_or_nothing():
   370	    """注入：事务各点 kill -9（SIGKILL，真崩溃，非异常模拟）。
   371	
   372	    确定性点位（对第 pre+1 条 doomed append）：
   373	      after_begin  —— BEGIN IMMEDIATE 刚执行完
   374	      after_insert —— INSERT 已执行、未 COMMIT
   375	      after_commit —— COMMIT 已执行、未返回调用方
   376	    随机点位：timer 模式在 50 条批量写入中随机自杀，重复 8 轮。
   377	    期望（全有或全无）：重开库后哈希链始终可验证；行数只会是
   378	    pre（事务未提交，WAL 回收丢弃）或 pre+1（已提交），绝无撕裂半行；
   379	    批量场景行数落在 [0,50] 且链完好。
   380	    """
   381	    points = {}
   382	    for mode in ("after_begin", "after_insert", "after_commit"):
   383	        with TmpDir("ac-s4-") as td:
   384	            db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   385	            pre = 3
   386	            p = _run_kill_child(db, mode, pre)
   387	            assert p.returncode == -9, \
   388	                f"{mode}: 子进程未死于 SIGKILL (rc={p.returncode})"
   389	            store = EventStore(db)
   390	            try:
   391	                total = store.head()[1]
   392	                verify = store.verify_chain()
   393	                doomed = rows_of(
   394	                    store._conn,
   395	                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events "
   396	                    "WHERE payload LIKE '%doomed%'")[0][0]
   397	            finally:
   398	                store.close()
   399	            if mode == "after_commit":
   400	                assert total == pre + 1 and doomed == 1, \
   401	                    f"{mode}: 已提交事务丢失 total={total} doomed={doomed}"
   402	            else:
   403	                assert total == pre and doomed == 0, \
   404	                    f"{mode}: 未提交事务泄漏 total={total} doomed={doomed}"
   405	            assert verify["ok"] is True, f"{mode}: 崩溃后链断裂"
   406	            points[mode] = {"rows": total, "chain_ok": True,
   407	                            "committed": mode == "after_commit"}
   408	
   409	    rng = random.Random(20261008)      # 固定种子，证据可复现
   410	    timer_observed = []
   411	    for round_no in range(8):
   412	        with TmpDir("ac-s4t-") as td:
   413	            db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   414	            delay = rng.uniform(0.005, 0.25)
   415	            p = _run_kill_child(db, "timer", 0, delay)
   416	            assert p.returncode == -9, f"timer r{round_no}: rc={p.returncode}"
   417	            store = EventStore(db)
   418	            try:
   419	                total = store.head()[1]
   420	                verify = store.verify_chain()
   421	            finally:
   422	                store.close()
   423	            assert 0 <= total <= 50, f"timer r{round_no}: 行数越界 {total}"
   424	            assert verify["ok"] is True, f"timer r{round_no}: 链断裂 @ {total} 行"
   425	            timer_observed.append({"round": round_no, "delay_s": round(delay, 3),
   426	                                   "rows": total, "chain_ok": True})
   427	    return {"deterministic_points": points,
   428	            "timer_rounds": timer_observed,
   429	            "verdict": "每次崩溃后：链完好，行数=已提交事务数（全有或全无）"}
   430	
   431	
   432	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   433	# STATE-06：WAITING 终态残留 → SUPERSEDED 事件，不静默删
   434	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   435	def test_STATE_06_waiting_residue_superseded_not_silently_deleted():
   436	    """注入：账本里留有 WAITING 终态残留事件行；尝试静默消除它。
   437	
   438	    期望（被测体已实现面）：
   439	      1) 残留行不可 DELETE/UPDATE（静默删除路径被 append-only 触发器关死）；
   440	      2) SUPERSEDED 是控制事件类型——裸 append 伪造『已被接管』被拒；
   441	      3) 唯一出路是追加新事件（账本延长，残留行逐字节保留、链可验证）。
   442	    """
   443	    with TmpDir("ac-s6-") as td:
   444	        db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   445	        store = EventStore(db)
   446	        try:
   447	            store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "stage": "S5",
   448	                          "residue": "attempt waiting since kill -9"})
   449	            residue_snapshot = rows_of(
   450	                store._conn, "SELECT seq, payload, event_hash FROM pipeline_events")
   451	            # 1) 静默删/改两条路全拒
   452	            expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: store._conn.execute(
   453	                "DELETE FROM pipeline_events WHERE payload LIKE '%waiting%'"))
   454	            expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: store._conn.execute(
   455	                "UPDATE pipeline_events SET payload = '{}' "
   456	                "WHERE payload LIKE '%waiting%'"))
   457	            # 裸连接同样拒（触发器在 schema 层，不依赖 EventStore API）
   458	            conn = raw_conn(db)
   459	            try:
   460	                expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: conn.execute(
   461	                    "DELETE FROM pipeline_events"))
   462	            finally:
   463	                conn.close()
   464	            # 2) 伪造 SUPERSEDED 控制事件被拒（覆盖旧残留必须走带 seal 的内部口）
   465	            expect(ValueError, lambda: store.append(
   466	                {"event_type": "SUPERSEDED", "residue_ref": "S5"}))
   467	            expect(ValueError, lambda: store.append(
   468	                {"event_type": "WAITING_SUPERSEDED", "residue_ref": "S5"}))
   469	            # 3) 正道：追加接管事件；残留行原样保留
   470	            h2, ins = store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "stage": "S5",
   471	                                    "superseded_by": "run-2"})
   472	            assert ins is True
   473	            after = rows_of(
   474	                store._conn, "SELECT seq, payload, event_hash FROM pipeline_events")
   475	            assert after[:len(residue_snapshot)] == residue_snapshot, \
   476	                "残留行被改动（不允许）"
   477	            assert store.head() == (h2, 2)
   478	            assert store.verify_chain()["ok"] is True
   479	        finally:
   480	            store.close()
   481	    return {"silent_delete": "rejected (append-only trigger)",
   482	            "silent_update": "rejected (append-only trigger)",
   483	            "forged_superseded_events": "rejected (control-event guard)",
   484	            "residue_rows_preserved": 1,
   485	            "boundary": "SUPERSEDED 控制事件的实际签发在 RunManager（禁 import）"}
   486	
   487	
   488	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   489	# STATE-07：普通事件把 PASSED 改回 RUNNING → 拒绝
   490	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   491	def test_STATE_07_normal_event_cannot_flip_passed_to_running():
   492	    """注入：已落账的 PASSED 证据；三种『改回 RUNNING』的尝试。
   493	
   494	    期望（被测体已实现面）：
   495	      1) UPDATE 旧行把 PASSED 换成 RUNNING → 触发器拒绝；
   496	      2) DELETE 旧行再补一条 RUNNING 版本 → DELETE 拒绝；
   497	      3) 普通调用方伪造状态转移事件（event_type 为 RUNNING/ATTEMPT_*/…）
   498	        → 控制事件守卫拒绝（状态转移只能走带 seal 的内部口）；
   499	      4) 事后账本完好：PASSED 行原样、链可验证。
   500	    """
   501	    with TmpDir("ac-s7-") as td:
   502	        db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   503	        store = EventStore(db)
   504	        try:
   505	            store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "stage": "S3",
   506	                          "status": "PASSED", "evidence": "att-1"})
   507	            before = rows_of(store._conn, "SELECT payload FROM pipeline_events")
   508	            # 1) UPDATE 改状态
   509	            expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: store._conn.execute(
   510	                "UPDATE pipeline_events SET payload = replace(payload, "
   511	                "'PASSED', 'RUNNING')"))
   512	            # 2) DELETE 后重写
   513	            expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: store._conn.execute(
   514	                "DELETE FROM pipeline_events WHERE payload LIKE '%PASSED%'"))
   515	            # 3) 伪造 RUNNING 状态转移控制事件（普通事件无转移语义，只能冒充控制类型）
   516	            for forged in ({"event_type": "RUN_RUNNING"},
   517	                           {"event_type": "ATTEMPT_RESUMED", "status": "RUNNING"},
   518	                           {"event_type": "STAGE_STATUS", "stage": "S3",
   519	                            "status": "RUNNING"}):
   520	                expect(ValueError, lambda ev=forged: store.append(ev))
   521	            after = rows_of(store._conn, "SELECT payload FROM pipeline_events")
   522	            assert after == before, "PASSED 证据被改动"
   523	            assert store.head()[1] == 1
   524	            assert store.verify_chain()["ok"] is True
   525	            # 仍是 PASSED
   526	            assert rows_of(store._conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events "
   527	                          "WHERE payload LIKE '%PASSED%'")[0][0] == 1
   528	            assert rows_of(store._conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events "
   529	                          "WHERE payload LIKE '%RUNNING%'")[0][0] == 0
   530	        finally:
   531	            store.close()
   532	    return {"update_path": "rejected", "delete_rewrite_path": "rejected",
   533	            "forged_transition_events": "rejected",
   534	            "passed_evidence_intact": True,
   535	            "boundary": "转移表级拒绝（InvalidTransitionError）在 wenqu_pipeline"}
   536	
   537	
   538	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   539	# STATE-08：FAILED 重试 → 新 attempt，旧证据不覆盖
   540	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   541	def test_STATE_08_failed_retry_new_attempt_old_evidence_intact():
   542	    """注入：一次 FAILED attempt 的证据事件；随后发起『重试』。
   543	
   544	    期望（被测体已实现面）：
   545	      1) 用新 attempt 数据覆盖旧证据行 → UPDATE 拒绝；
   546	      2) 正当重试路径 = 追加一条新 attempt 事件（新内容、新 event_id/hash）；
   547	      3) 旧 FAILED 证据逐字节不变（payload+event_hash 快照对比），
   548	         新旧 attempt 同时在账、链可验证——旧证据不覆盖。
   549	    """
   550	    with TmpDir("ac-s8-") as td:
   551	        db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   552	        store = EventStore(db)
   553	        try:
   554	            store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-1",
   555	                          "result": "FAILED", "log": "exit 1 at S2",
   556	                          "ts": "2026-10-08T00:00:00Z"})
   557	            old = rows_of(store._conn,
   558	                          "SELECT seq, payload, prev_event_hash, event_hash "
   559	                          "FROM pipeline_events")
   560	            # 1) 覆盖旧证据 → 拒绝
   561	            expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda: store._conn.execute(
   562	                "UPDATE pipeline_events SET payload = ? WHERE payload LIKE ?",
   563	                (json.dumps({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-2",
   564	                             "result": "RUNNING"}, sort_keys=True), "%att-1%")))
   565	            # 2) 正当路径：追加新 attempt
   566	            h2, ins = store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-2",
   567	                                    "result": "RUNNING", "retry_of": "att-1",
   568	                                    "ts": "2026-10-08T01:00:00Z"})
   569	            assert ins is True
   570	            # 3) 旧证据不变；新 attempt 落账；链完好
   571	            rows = rows_of(store._conn,
   572	                           "SELECT seq, payload, prev_event_hash, event_hash "
   573	                           "FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq")
   574	            assert rows[0] == old[0], "旧 FAILED 证据被覆盖/改动"
   575	            assert rows[1][1] != old[0][1] and rows[1][3] == h2
   576	            assert store.head() == (h2, 2)
   577	            assert store.verify_chain()["ok"] is True
   578	            both = rows_of(store._conn,
   579	                           "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events "
   580	                           "WHERE payload LIKE '%att-%'")[0][0]
   581	            assert both == 2
   582	        finally:
   583	            store.close()
   584	    return {"overwrite_attempt": "rejected",
   585	            "old_evidence_byte_identical": True,
   586	            "attempts_in_ledger": ["att-1 (FAILED)", "att-2 (new, RUNNING)"],
   587	            "chain_verified": True,
   588	            "boundary": "attempt_id 分配/AttemptImmutabilityError 在 wenqu_pipeline"}
   589	
   590	
   591	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   592	# STATE-10：WAITING/FAILED attempt 直接改回 RUNNING → 拒绝；
   593	#           追加新 attempt 后（projection 由管道层指向它）
   594	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   595	def test_STATE_10_terminal_attempt_no_direct_running_new_attempt_ok():
   596	    """注入：WAITING 残留 attempt 与 FAILED attempt 各一条证据行；
   597	    尝试把它们『直接改回 RUNNING』。
   598	
   599	    期望（被测体已实现面·前半句）：
   600	      1) UPDATE 行改 RUNNING → 拒绝（两行都拒）；
   601	      2) 伪造 ATTEMPT_/RUNNING 控制事件 → 拒绝；
   602	      （后半句）追加新 attempt 事件是允许的出路：新事件落账、
   603	      旧 WAITING/FAILED 行原样保留（projection 读账决定指向——该读侧
   604	      逻辑在 RunManager，本文件不越界重造）。
   605	    """
   606	    with TmpDir("ac-s10-") as td:
   607	        db = os.path.join(td, "ev.db")
   608	        store = EventStore(db)
   609	        try:
   610	            store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-1",
   611	                          "status": "WAITING", "why": "manual-gate"})
   612	            store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-2",
   613	                          "status": "FAILED", "why": "timeout"})
   614	            snap = rows_of(store._conn,
   615	                           "SELECT seq, payload, event_hash FROM pipeline_events "
   616	                           "ORDER BY seq")
   617	            # 1) 两行直接 UPDATE 改 RUNNING → 全拒
   618	            for marker in ("WAITING", "FAILED"):
   619	                expect(sqlite3.IntegrityError, lambda m=marker: store._conn.execute(
   620	                    "UPDATE pipeline_events SET payload = replace(payload, ?, "
   621	                    "'RUNNING')", (m,)))
   622	            # 2) 伪造控制事件改状态 → 拒绝
   623	            for forged in ({"event_type": "ATTEMPT_RESUMED", "attempt": "att-1",
   624	                            "status": "RUNNING"},
   625	                           {"event_type": "RUN_RUNNING", "attempt": "att-2"},
   626	                           {"event_type": "WAITING_RESUMED", "attempt": "att-1"}):
   627	                expect(ValueError, lambda ev=forged: store.append(ev))
   628	            # 3) 正道：追加新 attempt；旧行原样保留
   629	            h3, ins = store.append({"event_type": "NOTE", "attempt": "att-3",
   630	                                    "status": "RUNNING",
   631	                                    "continues_from": ["att-1", "att-2"]})
   632	            assert ins is True
   633	            rows = rows_of(store._conn,
   634	                           "SELECT seq, payload, event_hash FROM pipeline_events "
   635	                           "ORDER BY seq")
   636	            assert rows[0] == snap[0] and rows[1] == snap[1], \
   637	                "WAITING/FAILED 旧行被改动或被删"
   638	            assert store.head() == (h3, 3)
   639	            assert store.verify_chain()["ok"] is True
   640	            still = rows_of(store._conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pipeline_events "
   641	                            "WHERE payload LIKE '%WAITING%'")[0][0]
   642	            assert still == 1
   643	        finally:
   644	            store.close()
   645	    return {"direct_update_to_running": "rejected (WAITING & FAILED rows)",
   646	            "forged_control_transitions": "rejected",
   647	            "new_attempt_appended": "att-3",
   648	            "old_rows_preserved": ["att-1 WAITING", "att-2 FAILED"],
   649	            "boundary": "stage projection 指向新 attempt 在 RunManager（禁 import）"}
   650	
   651	
   652	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   653	# 合成旧账 fixture 构造器（MIG 族共用；均为合成数据，非生产账本）
   654	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   655	def _write_lines(dirpath, name, lines):
   656	    path = os.path.join(dirpath, name)
   657	    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   658	        fh.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
   659	    return path
   660	
   661	
   662	def _sidecar_records(path):
   663	    out = []
   664	    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   665	        for ln in fh:
   666	            ln = ln.strip()
   667	            if ln:
   668	                out.append(json.loads(ln))
   669	    return out
   670	
   671	
   672	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   673	# MIG-01：坏 JSON/未知状态 → quarantine+BLOCKED
   674	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   675	def test_MIG_01_bad_json_unknown_status_quarantine():
   676	    """注入（合成旧账）：坏 JSON、非对象 JSON、缺 status/ts、未知状态；
   677	    对照组：已知别名（含 ERROR→BLOCKED 的 fail-closed 归一）。
   678	
   679	    期望（被测体已实现面）：坏行一律 quarantine（reason 精确），绝不导入
   680	    events 表；migration_runs 记 quarantined 计数；sidecar 逐行留痕；
   681	    ERROR/BLOCKED 行按已知状态 BLOCKED 导入——绝不把坏行洗成绿。
   682	    """
   683	    with TmpDir("ac-m1-") as td:
   684	        src = os.path.join(td, "legacy"); os.makedirs(src)
   685	        lines = [
   686	            '{"status": "PASS", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z", "id": 1}',
   687	            '{"status": "ERROR", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:01:00Z", "id": 2}',
   688	            '{"status": "BLOCKED", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:02:00Z", "id": 3}',
   689	            '{"status": "OK", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:03:00Z", "id": 4}',
   690	            '{"status": "FIXED", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:04:00Z", "id": 5}',
   691	            '{broken json',
   692	            '["not", "an", "object"]',
   693	            '"just a string"',
   694	            '{"status": "PASS"}',
   695	            '{"ts": "2026-10-01T00:05:00Z"}',
   696	            '{"status": "", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:06:00Z"}',
   697	            '{"status": "WEIRD_STATE", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:07:00Z"}',
   698	            '',
   699	            '   ',
   700	        ]
   701	        _write_lines(src, "legacy-a.jsonl", lines)
   702	        db = os.path.join(td, "mig.db")
   703	        mig = LedgerMigrator(src, db)
   704	        report = mig.migrate()
   705	
   706	        c = report["conservation"]
   707	        assert c["lines_total"] == 12, c          # 空白行不计分母
   708	        assert c["imported"] == 5 and c["quarantined"] == 7 and c["duplicates"] == 0
   709	        assert report["conserved"] is True
   710	        assert report["imported_status_counts"] == {
   711	            "BLOCKED": 2, "FIXED_PENDING_VERIFY": 1, "PASS": 2}
   712	        assert report["quarantine_reasons"] == {
   713	            "invalid_json": 1, "missing_field:status": 2, "missing_field:ts": 1,
   714	            "not_a_json_object": 2, "unknown_status:WEIRD_STATE": 1}
   715	
   716	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   717	        try:
   718	            statuses = dict(rows_of(conn, "SELECT status, COUNT(*) FROM events "
   719	                                          "GROUP BY status"))
   720	            assert statuses == {"BLOCKED": 2, "FIXED_PENDING_VERIFY": 1,
   721	                                "PASS": 2}
   722	            bad_in_db = rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events WHERE "
   723	                                "payload LIKE '%WEIRD%' OR payload LIKE "
   724	                                "'%broken%' OR payload LIKE '%not%object%'")[0][0]
   725	            assert bad_in_db == 0, "坏行被导入（不允许）"
   726	            run = rows_of(conn, "SELECT files_scanned, lines_total, imported, "
   727	                              "duplicates, quarantined, conserved FROM "
   728	                              "migration_runs")
   729	            assert run == [(1, 12, 5, 0, 7, 1)]
   730	        finally:
   731	            conn.close()
   732	        recs = _sidecar_records(mig.sidecar_path)
   733	        quarantined = [r for r in recs if r["decision"] == "quarantined"]
   734	        imported = [r for r in recs if r["decision"] == "imported"]
   735	        assert len(quarantined) == 7 and len(imported) == 5
   736	        assert {r["reason"] for r in quarantined} == {
   737	            "invalid_json", "missing_field:status", "missing_field:ts",
   738	            "not_a_json_object", "unknown_status:WEIRD_STATE"}
   739	        # 导入行按已知状态入账：ERROR/BLOCKED 两行均以 BLOCKED 状态落库
   740	        assert sorted(r["status"] for r in imported) == [
   741	            "BLOCKED", "BLOCKED", "FIXED_PENDING_VERIFY", "PASS", "PASS"]
   742	        # 单元面：classify 的 quarantine 判定矩阵
   743	        for bad in ('{nope', '[1]', 'null', '"s"', '{"status":"X","ts":"t"}',
   744	                    '{"status":"PASS"}', '{"ts":"t"}'):
   745	            v = LedgerMigrator.classify(bad)
   746	            assert v["decision"] == "quarantine", bad
   747	        assert LedgerMigrator.classify(
   748	            '{"status": "ERROR", "ts": "t"}')["status"] == "BLOCKED"
   749	    return {"quarantined": 7, "imported": 5,
   750	            "imported_status_counts": {"BLOCKED": 2, "PASS": 2,
   751	                                       "FIXED_PENDING_VERIFY": 1},
   752	            "bad_lines_in_events_table": 0,
   753	            "boundary": "run 级 BLOCKED 聚合在编排层（不在被测体）"}
   754	
   755	
   756	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   757	# MIG-02：缺审批信息的 ACCEPTED → invalid/reopen
   758	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   759	def test_MIG_02_accepted_missing_approval_invalid_reopen():
   760	    """注入（合成旧账）：四类 ACCEPTED 行——缺 ts、缺审批要素、带看似完整的
   761	    审批信息、以及一个对照组合法 PASS 行。
   762	
   763	    期望（被测体已实现面）：ACCEPTED 对迁移器是未知状态——无论审批信息
   764	    缺不缺，一律 invalid 进 quarantine（绝不猜映射成 PASS），零导入；
   765	    quarantine + sidecar 留痕即待人工 reopen 的账（重进正源必须人工再录），
   766	    绝不静默放行、绝不静默丢弃。
   767	    """
   768	    with TmpDir("ac-m2-") as td:
   769	        src = os.path.join(td, "legacy"); os.makedirs(src)
   770	        lines = [
   771	            '{"status": "ACCEPTED", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z", "id": 1}',
   772	            '{"status": "ACCEPTED", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:01:00Z", "id": 2, '
   773	            '"approval": {"approved_by": "u1", "risk": "P3", '
   774	            '"fingerprint": "abc", "expires": "2026-12-01"}}',
   775	            '{"status": "ACCEPTED"}',
   776	            '{"status": "accepted", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:02:00Z", "id": 3}',
   777	            '{"status": "PASS", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:03:00Z", "id": 4}',
   778	        ]
   779	        _write_lines(src, "accepted-ledger.jsonl", lines)
   780	        db = os.path.join(td, "mig.db")
   781	        mig = LedgerMigrator(src, db)
   782	        report = mig.migrate()
   783	
   784	        c = report["conservation"]
   785	        assert c["lines_total"] == 5
   786	        assert c["imported"] == 1 and c["quarantined"] == 4, c
   787	        assert report["quarantine_reasons"] == {
   788	            "missing_field:ts": 1, "unknown_status:ACCEPTED": 3}
   789	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   790	        try:
   791	            db_rows = rows_of(conn, "SELECT status, payload FROM events")
   792	            assert len(db_rows) == 1 and db_rows[0][0] == "PASS"
   793	            accepted_in_db = rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events WHERE "
   794	                                     "payload LIKE '%ACCEPTED%' OR payload "
   795	                                     "LIKE '%accepted%'")[0][0]
   796	            assert accepted_in_db == 0, "ACCEPTED 行被导入（不允许）"
   797	        finally:
   798	            conn.close()
   799	        recs = _sidecar_records(mig.sidecar_path)
   800	        q = [r for r in recs if r["decision"] == "quarantined"]
   801	        assert len(q) == 4
   802	        assert all("preview" in r for r in q), "quarantine 必须带原文预览（reopen 线索）"
   803	    return {"accepted_lines": 4, "quarantined": 4, "imported": 0,
   804	            "reopen_channel": "quarantine + sidecar 预览（人工再录）",
   805	            "boundary": "risk acceptance/reopen 事件子系统（FND-016）不在被测体"}
   806	
   807	
   808	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   809	# MIG-03：两次迁移 → 结果幂等一致
   810	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   811	def test_MIG_03_double_migration_idempotent():
   812	    """注入（合成旧账）：同一账本连续两次完整迁移（两个独立迁移器实例）。
   813	
   814	    期望：结果幂等一致——events 表行集逐字节相同（快照对比）；第二次
   815	    imported=0、原导入行全部计为 duplicate；两次守恒均成立；
   816	    源 JSONL 全程只读（前后字节一致）；sidecar 只追加。
   817	    """
   818	    with TmpDir("ac-m3-") as td:
   819	        src = os.path.join(td, "legacy"); os.makedirs(src)
   820	        lines = [
   821	            '{"status": "PASS", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z", "id": 1}',
   822	            '{"status": "FAILED", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:01:00Z", "id": 2}',
   823	            '{"status": "WARN", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:02:00Z", "id": 3}',
   824	            '{"status": "PASS", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z", "id": 1}',
   825	            '{"status": "MYSTERY", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:03:00Z"}',
   826	            'oops',
   827	        ]
   828	        src_file = _write_lines(src, "dup-ledger.jsonl", lines)
   829	        before_bytes = open(src_file, "rb").read()
   830	
   831	        db = os.path.join(td, "mig.db")
   832	        r1 = LedgerMigrator(src, db).migrate()
   833	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   834	        try:
   835	            snap1 = rows_of(conn, "SELECT line_sha, status, source_file, "
   836	                                  "source_line, payload FROM events ORDER BY id")
   837	        finally:
   838	            conn.close()
   839	        sc1 = open(os.path.join(td, "mig.db.migration-sidecar.jsonl"),
   840	                   "rb").read()
   841	
   842	        r2 = LedgerMigrator(src, db).migrate()     # 第二个实例、同一库
   843	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   844	        try:
   845	            snap2 = rows_of(conn, "SELECT line_sha, status, source_file, "
   846	                                  "source_line, payload FROM events ORDER BY id")
   847	        finally:
   848	            conn.close()
   849	        sc2 = open(os.path.join(td, "mig.db.migration-sidecar.jsonl"),
   850	                   "rb").read()
   851	
   852	        assert snap1 == snap2, "二次迁移改动了已导入行集（不幂等）"
   853	        assert sc2.startswith(sc1) and len(sc2) > len(sc1), "sidecar 必须只追加"
   854	        assert open(src_file, "rb").read() == before_bytes, "源账本被改动（M1 违规）"
   855	        c1, c2 = r1["conservation"], r2["conservation"]
   856	        assert c1["imported"] == 3 and c1["duplicates"] == 1 \
   857	            and c1["quarantined"] == 2 and c1["lines_total"] == 6
   858	        assert c2["imported"] == 0 and c2["duplicates"] == 4 \
   859	            and c2["quarantined"] == 2 and c2["lines_total"] == 6
   860	        assert r1["conserved"] is True and r2["conserved"] is True
   861	        assert r1["imported_status_counts"] == {
   862	            "CONDITIONAL": 1, "FAIL": 1, "PASS": 1}
   863	        # r2 无新导入，status 计数为空是正确表现（幂等：不再重复入账）
   864	        assert r2["imported_status_counts"] == {}
   865	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   866	        try:
   867	            assert rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_runs")[0][0] == 2
   868	        finally:
   869	            conn.close()
   870	    return {"run1": {"imported": 3, "duplicates": 1, "quarantined": 2},
   871	            "run2": {"imported": 0, "duplicates": 4, "quarantined": 2},
   872	            "events_table_identical": True, "source_bytewise_unchanged": True,
   873	            "sidecar_append_only": True}
   874	
   875	
   876	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   877	# MIG-04：源行数不守恒 → 迁移失败
   878	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   879	def test_MIG_04_source_conservation_enforced():
   880	    """注入（合成旧账 + 三类真实破坏）：
   881	      (a) 迁移后从 events 表直接删 3 行（账本少行=不守恒态）；
   882	      (b) 审计 sidecar 落在一个目录路径上（任何 OS/用户都写不开）；
   883	      (c) sqlite 目标落在一个目录路径上（根本开不了库）。
   884	
   885	    期望（被测体已实现面）：
   886	      (a) 重迁移精确补齐缺失行（imported=3、其余 duplicate），守恒恢复，
   887	          绝不静默少账——守恒等式 lines==imported+duplicates+quarantined
   888	          在 DB/sidecar 独立对账下成立；
   889	      (b) 审计不可写 → 迁移整体失败（异常抛出），events 零行、
   890	          migration_runs 零行——没有部分导入、没有假守恒记账；
   891	      (c) 开不了库 → 迁移失败（异常抛出）。
   892	    诚实边界：ConservationError 守卫本身是防御性的——migrate 的计数与
   893	    处理循环遍历同一 list，公共 API 构造不出账目不平（已代码通读+注入
   894	    尝试实证），故此处以同秩语义的运行时面做真实验证。
   895	    """
   896	    with TmpDir("ac-m4-") as td:
   897	        src = os.path.join(td, "legacy"); os.makedirs(src)
   898	        n_valid = 9
   899	        lines = ['{"status": "PASS", "ts": "2026-10-01T00:00:00Z", "id": %d}' % i
   900	                 for i in range(n_valid)]
   901	        lines += ['{"status": "GHOST", "ts": "t"}', '{bad']
   902	        _write_lines(src, "conserved.jsonl", lines)
   903	        db = os.path.join(td, "mig.db")
   904	
   905	        r1 = LedgerMigrator(src, db).migrate()
   906	        assert r1["conserved"] and r1["conservation"]["imported"] == n_valid
   907	
   908	        # 独立对账（不信 report，直接从源文件/DB/sidecar 重数）
   909	        src_nonblank = sum(1 for ln in open(os.path.join(src, "conserved.jsonl"),
   910	                                            encoding="utf-8") if ln.strip())
   911	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   912	        try:
   913	            db_rows = rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events")[0][0]
   914	            quarantined_shas = {r["line_sha"] for r in
   915	                                _sidecar_records(db + ".migration-sidecar.jsonl")
   916	                                if r["decision"] == "quarantined"}
   917	        finally:
   918	            conn.close()
   919	        src_shas = {r["line_sha"] for r in LedgerMigrator(src, db).scan()}
   920	        assert src_nonblank == db_rows + len(quarantined_shas)
   921	        assert quarantined_shas <= src_shas and db_rows == n_valid
   922	
   923	        # (a) 删行 → 不守恒态 → 重迁移必须补齐，不许静默少账
   924	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   925	        try:
   926	            conn.execute("DELETE FROM events WHERE source_line IN (2, 3, 4)")
   927	            conn.commit()
   928	        finally:
   929	            conn.close()
   930	        r2 = LedgerMigrator(src, db).migrate()
   931	        assert r2["conservation"]["imported"] == 3
   932	        assert r2["conservation"]["duplicates"] == n_valid - 3
   933	        assert r2["conserved"] is True
   934	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db)
   935	        try:
   936	            assert rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events")[0][0] == n_valid
   937	        finally:
   938	            conn.close()
   939	
   940	        # (b) sidecar 写不开（目录路径；对 root 也成立）→ 整体失败、零部分账
   941	        side_dir = os.path.join(td, "sidecar-is-dir"); os.makedirs(side_dir)
   942	        db_b = os.path.join(td, "fail-sidecar.db")
   943	        try:
   944	            LedgerMigrator(src, db_b, sidecar_path=side_dir).migrate()
   945	            raise AssertionError("sidecar 不可写时迁移竟成功（假绿）")
   946	        except (IsADirectoryError, PermissionError, OSError):
   947	            pass
   948	        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_b)
   949	        try:
   950	            assert rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events")[0][0] == 0
   951	            assert rows_of(conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_runs")[0][0] == 0
   952	        finally:
   953	            conn.close()
   954	
   955	        # (c) 目标库开不了（目录路径）→ 迁移失败
   956	        try:
   957	            LedgerMigrator(src, side_dir).migrate()
   958	            raise AssertionError("开不了库时迁移竟成功（假绿）")
   959	        except sqlite3.Error:
   960	            pass
   961	    return {"independent_recount": {"source_nonblank": src_nonblank,
   962	                                    "db_rows": n_valid, "quarantined": 2},
   963	            "deleted_rows_reimported": 3,
   964	            "unwritable_sidecar": "迁移失败，events=0、migration_runs=0",
   965	            "unopenable_db": "迁移失败（异常）",
   966	            "boundary": "ConservationError 为防御性守卫（公共 API 构造不出不平，"
   967	                        "已实证）；此处验证同秩运行时语义"}
   968	
   969	
   970	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   971	# 运行器 + 证据工件
   972	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   973	TESTS = [
   974	    ("STATE-01", "任意 stage/status → schema 拒绝",
   975	     test_STATE_01_arbitrary_stage_status_schema_rejected),
   976	    ("STATE-03", "100×100 并发写 → 零丢失/重复",
   977	     test_STATE_03_concurrent_100x100_no_loss_no_dup),
   978	    ("STATE-04", "事务各点 kill -9 → 全有或全无",
   979	     test_STATE_04_kill9_all_or_nothing),
   980	    ("STATE-06", "WAITING 终态残留 → SUPERSEDED 事件，不静默删",
   981	     test_STATE_06_waiting_residue_superseded_not_silently_deleted),
   982	    ("STATE-07", "普通事件把 PASSED 改回 RUNNING → 拒绝",
   983	     test_STATE_07_normal_event_cannot_flip_passed_to_running),
   984	    ("STATE-08", "FAILED 重试 → 新 attempt，旧证据不覆盖",
   985	     test_STATE_08_failed_retry_new_attempt_old_evidence_intact),
   986	    ("STATE-10", "WAITING/FAILED attempt 直接改回 RUNNING → 拒绝；"
   987	                 "追加新 attempt 后由 stage projection 指向它",
   988	     test_STATE_10_terminal_attempt_no_direct_running_new_attempt_ok),
   989	    ("MIG-01", "坏 JSON/未知状态 → quarantine+BLOCKED",
   990	     test_MIG_01_bad_json_unknown_status_quarantine),
   991	    ("MIG-02", "缺审批信息的 ACCEPTED → invalid/reopen",
   992	     test_MIG_02_accepted_missing_approval_invalid_reopen),
   993	    ("MIG-03", "两次迁移 → 结果幂等一致",
   994	     test_MIG_03_double_migration_idempotent),
   995	    ("MIG-04", "源行数不守恒 → 迁移失败",
   996	     test_MIG_04_source_conservation_enforced),
   997	]

  1000	def _git_head():
  1001	    try:
  1002	        out = subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=REPO_ROOT,
  1003	                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
  1004	        if out.returncode == 0:
  1005	            return out.stdout.strip()
  1006	    except Exception:                                   # noqa: BLE001
  1007	        pass
  1008	    return None
  1009	
  1010	
  1011	def write_artifact(started_at, finished_at, exit_code):
  1012	    results = {}
  1013	    for tid, name, ok, detail in RESULTS:
  1014	        results[tid] = {
  1015	            "name": name,
  1016	            "verdict": "PASS" if ok else "FAIL",
  1017	            "detail": detail,
  1018	            **({"coverage_boundary": COVERAGE_BOUNDARY[tid]}
  1019	               if tid in COVERAGE_BOUNDARY else {}),
  1020	        }
  1021	    for tid, info in NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_IN_SCOPE.items():
  1022	        results[tid] = {
  1023	            "name": f"{info['injection']} → {info['expectation']}",
  1024	            "verdict": "NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_IN_SCOPE",
  1025	            "reason": info["reason"],
  1026	        }
  1027	    n_pass = sum(1 for *_, ok, _d in
  1028	                 [(r[0], r[1], r[2], r[3]) for r in RESULTS] if ok)
  1029	    artifact = {
  1030	        "artifact": "ac-state-mig",
  1031	        "description": "§20.2/§20.5 STATE-01~11、MIG-01~05 验收 ID 专属测试"
  1032	                       "逐 ID 结果（被测体：wenqu_core/store.py + "
  1033	                       "wenqu_core/ledger_migrator.py；不依赖 wenqu_pipeline）",
  1034	        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
  1035	        "git_head": _git_head(),
  1036	        "test_file": "system/tests/test_ac_state_mig.py",
  1037	        "run_command": "python3 system/tests/test_ac_state_mig.py",
  1038	        "exit_code": exit_code,
  1039	        "started_at": started_at,
  1040	        "finished_at": finished_at,
  1041	        "fixture_note": "MIG 族旧账均为本测试内构造的小型合成旧账（临时目录），"
  1042	                        "非生产账本；STATE-04 崩溃注入为真实 SIGKILL 子进程。",
  1043	        "results": dict(sorted(results.items())),
  1044	        "summary": {
  1045	            "ids_total": 16,
  1046	            "implemented_and_passed": n_pass,
  1047	            "not_implementable_in_scope": len(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_IN_SCOPE),
  1048	            "failed": len(RESULTS) - n_pass,
  1049	        },
  1050	    }
  1051	    try:
  1052	        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(ARTIFACT_PATH), exist_ok=True)
  1053	        with open(ARTIFACT_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
  1054	            json.dump(artifact, fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)
  1055	        return ARTIFACT_PATH
  1056	    except OSError as e:
  1057	        print(f"  WARNING: 证据工件写入失败: {e}")
  1058	        return None
  1059	
  1060	
  1061	def main():
  1062	    global PASS_N, FAIL_N
  1063	    started_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1064	    print("§20 验收 ID 专属测试：STATE-01~11 / MIG-01~05（store + ledger_migrator）")
  1065	    print(f"被测体: {os.path.join('system', 'wenqu_core', 'store.py')} + "
  1066	          f"{os.path.join('system', 'wenqu_core', 'ledger_migrator.py')}"
  1067	          f"（不 import wenqu_pipeline）\n")
  1068	    for tid, name, fn in TESTS:
  1069	        try:
  1070	            detail = fn() or {}
  1071	            ok = True
  1072	            PASS_N += 1
  1073	            print(f"  ok   {tid}  {name}")
  1074	            RESULTS.append((tid, name, ok, detail))
  1075	        except Exception as e:                          # noqa: BLE001
  1076	            FAIL_N += 1
  1077	            import traceback
  1078	            print(f"  FAIL {tid}  {name}")
  1079	            print("       " + traceback.format_exc(limit=8).replace(
  1080	                "\n", "\n       "))
  1081	            RESULTS.append((tid, name, False, {"error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"}))
  1082	    print("\n不可实现登记（被测体无对应产品语义；不硬凑、不 xfail）：")
  1083	    for tid, info in NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_IN_SCOPE.items():
  1084	        print(f"  --   {tid}  {info['injection']} → {info['expectation']}"
  1085	              f"（详见证据工件 reason）")
  1086	    exit_code = 0 if FAIL_N == 0 else 1
  1087	    finished_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
  1088	    path = write_artifact(started_at, finished_at, exit_code)
  1089	    print(f"\n结果: {PASS_N} PASS / {FAIL_N} FAIL；"
  1090	          f"范围内不可实现 {len(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE_IN_SCOPE)} 条；exit={exit_code}")
  1091	    if path:
  1092	        print(f"证据工件: {path}")
  1093	    return exit_code
  1094	
  1095	
  1096	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1097	    sys.exit(main())

===== system/tests/test_ac_station_families.py =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	"""test_ac_station_families.py——§20 验收 ID 专属测试·六族（本文件负责 29 条）。
     4	
     5	负责族与 ID（正源：evidence/00-baseline/codex-external/方案.md §20.3/§20.5 表格，
     6	注入/期望逐字对齐；tools/ac_traceability.py 冻结目录同源）：
     7	
     8	  STA-01~02（站点结果/整线聚合）、DB-01~06（目录漂移）、SC-01~05（供应链）、
     9	  ADV-01~02（advisory/公告面）、TEN-01~08（租户分母）、PERF-01~06（性能有效性）。
    10	
    11	被测体（不改动任何产品码——本文件只做真实注入+真实断言）：
    12	  wenqu_core.bugscan_orchestrator（STA 统一入口语义：分母缩水守卫/结果校验/manifest 冻结）
    13	  wenqu_core.station2_static（SC 供应链 npm audit 适配、ADV 公告解析、STA 站2 聚合）
    14	  wenqu_core.station3_contract（DB 目录漂移、契约对账、TEN 租户分母）
    15	  wenqu_core.station4_behavior（STA 站4 腿、PERF HTTP 面）
    16	  wenqu_core.station7_runtime（STA 站7 腿、PERF 指标面）
    17	
    18	实现口径（诚实边界，逐条落在各测试 docstring）：
    19	- 全部走模块内既有的桩/合成数据通道（_StubRunner 罐头应答、结构化 payload、
    20	  本地 127.0.0.1 真实 HTTP 服务、fake-vitest 真子进程）——零外网、零真实 DB 连接。
    21	- 不可实现的 ID 不硬凑：SC-03/SC-05、TEN-04、PERF-03/PERF-04 共 5 条列入
    22	  NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE（原因见该常量）；它们不设测试函数，避免追踪矩阵假绿。
    23	- DB-06/PERF-02/PERF-05/PERF-06/TEN-01~03 为「本发行包可实现子面」的等价语义，
    24	  覆盖边界在各自 docstring 中显式声明。
    25	
    26	用法：
    27	  python3 system/tests/test_ac_station_families.py            # 全绿 exit 0
    28	  python3 system/tests/test_ac_station_families.py --json-out PATH
    29	      （额外落结构化结果，供 evidence/04-unit-property-mutation/ac-station-families.json）
    30	"""

   129	def _next_run_id(prefix):
   130	    return f"ac-{prefix}-{next(_SEQ):03d}"

   132	
   133	class _StubRunner(RawTrustedRunner):
   134	    """罐头应答桩：不执行任何真实命令，按 argv 匹配返回 canned Evidence+字节。
   135	
   136	    与站2 selftest 同范式：继承 RawTrustedRunner（argv 校验/安全构造血统不变），
   137	    仅覆写 run_captured 供解析通道使用。
   138	    """
   139	
   140	    def __init__(self, script):
   141	        super().__init__(timeout=30)
   142	        self._script = list(script)
   143	
   144	    def run_captured(self, argv, cwd=None):
   145	        for match, respond in self._script:
   146	            if match(list(argv)):
   147	                evidence, out, err = respond(list(argv))
   148	                return CapturedRun(evidence=evidence, stdout=out, stderr=err)
   149	        raise AssertionError(f"stub runner: no canned response for {argv}")
   150	
   151	
   152	def _ev(argv, rc, ts=None):
   153	    return Evidence(
   154	        argv=tuple(argv), cwd="/tmp", actual_exit_code=rc,
   155	        stdout_sha256=_sha256_hex(b""), stderr_sha256=_sha256_hex(b""),
   156	        argv_digest=_argv_digest(argv),
   157	        timestamp=ts if ts is not None else time.time(),
   158	        fresh_until=time.time() + 300.0, timed_out=False,
   159	    )
   160	
   161	
   162	def _ev_timeout(argv):
   163	    return Evidence(
   164	        argv=tuple(argv), cwd="/tmp", actual_exit_code=None,
   165	        stdout_sha256=_sha256_hex(b""), stderr_sha256=_sha256_hex(b""),
   166	        argv_digest=_argv_digest(argv), timestamp=time.time(),
   167	        fresh_until=time.time() + 300.0, timed_out=True,
   168	    )
   169	
   170	
   171	def _j(v):
   172	    return json.dumps(v).encode("utf-8")
   173

   176	    "project_id": "wenqu-ac-station-families",
   177	    "commit_sha": "e" * 40,
   178	    "environment": "local",
   179	    "scope_hash": "a" * 64,
   180	    "ruleset_hash": "b" * 64,
   181	    "data_config_hash": "c" * 64,
   182	}

   185	def _mk_st2_repo(tmp_path):
   186	    """站2 聚合测试用仓：3 个 src 源文件 + app/pages 路由入口（分母=2）。"""
   187	    repo = Path(tmp_path) / "repo"
   188	    (repo / "src").mkdir(parents=True)
   189	    (repo / "app" / "x").mkdir(parents=True)
   190	    (repo / "pages").mkdir(parents=True)
   191	    for name in ("a.ts", "b.ts", "c.ts"):
   192	        (repo / "src" / name).write_text("const x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
   193	    (repo / "app" / "x" / "page.tsx").write_text(
   194	        "export default function P(){}\n", encoding="utf-8")
   195	    (repo / "pages" / "y.tsx").write_text(
   196	        "export default function Y(){}\n", encoding="utf-8")
   197	    return repo

   200	JSCPD_REPORT = {
   201	    "duplicates": [
   202	        {"firstFile": {"name": "a.ts", "path": "src/a.ts", "lines": 10, "start": 1},
   203	         "secondFile": {"name": "b.ts", "path": "src/b.ts", "lines": 10, "start": 5},
   204	         "lines": 10, "tokens": 50},
   205	    ],
   206	    "statistics": {"total": {"clones": 1, "duplicates": 1, "files": 3,
   207	                             "lines": 1000, "percentage": "1.00%"}},
   208	}

   210	NPM_CLEAN = {
   211	    "auditReportVersion": 2, "vulnerabilities": {},
   212	    "metadata": {"vulnerabilities": {"info": 0, "low": 0, "moderate": 0,
   213	                                     "high": 0, "critical": 0},
   214	                 "dependencies": {"prod": 10, "dev": 2, "optional": 0,
   215	                                  "peer": 0, "total": 12}},
   216	}

   218	
   219	def _npm_vulns(entries, dep_total=12):
   220	    """entries: [(name, severity, range, title)] → npm audit JSON 载荷。"""
   221	    vulns = {}
   222	    for name, sev, rng, title in entries:
   223	        via = [{"title": title}] if title is not None else ["malformed-ref"]
   224	        vulns[name] = {"name": name, "severity": sev, "range": rng,
   225	                       "via": via, "effects": [], "fixAvailable": True}
   226	    counts = {"info": 0, "low": 0, "moderate": 0, "high": 0, "critical": 0}
   227	    for _, sev, _, _ in entries:
   228	        counts[sev if sev in counts else "high"] += 1
   229	    return {
   230	        "auditReportVersion": 2, "vulnerabilities": vulns,
   231	        "metadata": {"vulnerabilities": counts,
   232	                     "dependencies": {"prod": dep_total - 2, "dev": 2,
   233	                                      "optional": 0, "peer": 0,
   234	                                      "total": dep_total}},
   235	    }
   236	
   237	
   238	def _jscpd_respond(report):
   239	    def _respond(argv):
   240	        out_dir = Path(argv[argv.index("--output") + 1])
   241	        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   242	        (out_dir / JSCPD_REPORT_NAME).write_text(
   243	            json.dumps(report, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")),
   244	            encoding="utf-8")
   245	        return _ev(argv, 0), b"", b""
   246	    return _respond

   249	def _engine_cards(closed=True, drop=None):
   250	    cards = [
   251	        {"id": "ENUM-LITERALS", "slug": "enum", "status": "MERGED(PR#458 批A)"},
   252	        {"id": "CTX-PENETRATION", "slug": "ctx", "status": "MERGED(PR#459)——闭环"},
   253	        {"id": "MSG-STATIC", "slug": "msg", "status": "✅收官(七批)"},
   254	        {"id": "API-ACTION-DUALWRITE", "slug": "dualwrite", "status": "批A/B MERGED(#495)"},
   255	        {"id": "HARDCODED-DOMAIN-ENV", "slug": "envdomain", "status": "✅审计收官"},
   256	    ]
   257	    if drop is not None:
   258	        cards = [c for c in cards if c["slug"] != drop]
   259	    if not closed:
   260	        cards[0]["status"] = "IN-FLIGHT 批B 进行中"
   261	    return {"queue": cards, "cursor_inflight": None}

   274	def _mk_manifest(run_id, *, data_config=None, now=None):
   275	    now = now or _utc_now()
   276	    planner = BugscanPlanner(default_registry())
   277	    plan = planner.plan("STANDARD")
   278	    return planner.freeze_run_manifest(
   279	        plan, run_id=run_id, project_id="wenqu-ac-station-families",
   280	        commit_sha="e" * 40, environment="local",
   281	        scope=["src/**", "tests/**"],
   282	        ruleset={"version": "ac-tests", "rules": ["W6A-ST2", "W6A-ST3",
   283	                                                  "W6A-ST4", "W6A-ST7"]},
   284	        data_config=data_config if data_config is not None else {"profile": "default"},
   285	        now=now,
   286	    ), now
   287	
   288	
   289	def _mk_st3(data_config=None):
   290	    """返回 (contract, ts)：manifest 冻结 + 站3 总适配（now 固定防 TTL 误伤）。"""
   291	    manifest, now = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("st3"), data_config=data_config)
   292	    return Station3Contract(manifest, now=now), manifest.frozen_at

   295	def _drift_payload(ts, *, db_identity=None, data_config=None, objects_total=3,
   296	                   objects_scanned=None, changes=(), allowlist=(), **extra):
   297	    payload = {
   298	        "tool": {"name": "judge_drift.py", "version": "ac"},
   299	        "started_at": ts, "ended_at": ts,
   300	        "db_identity": dict(db_identity or AC_DB_ID),
   301	        "objects_total": objects_total,
   302	        "objects_scanned": objects_total if objects_scanned is None else objects_scanned,
   303	        # 注：非 list/tuple 的 changes 原样透传（DB-03 未知格式注入需要）
   304	        "changes": [dict(c) for c in changes]
   305	        if isinstance(changes, (list, tuple)) else changes,
   306	    }
   307	    if data_config is not None:
   308	        payload["data_config"] = data_config
   309	    if isinstance(allowlist, (list, tuple)) and allowlist:
   310	        payload["allowlist"] = [dict(a) for a in allowlist]
   311	    payload.update(extra)
   312	    return payload
   313	
   314	
   315	def _og_payload(ts, endpoints, *, principals=None):
   316	    payload = {
   317	        "tool": {"name": "org-guard", "version": "ac"},
   318	        "started_at": ts, "ended_at": ts,
   319	        "probe_identity": principals or {
   320	            "base_url": "http://app.local",
   321	            "positive_principal": "orgA/alice",
   322	            "negative_principal": "orgB/mallory",
   323	        },
   324	        "endpoints": endpoints,
   325	    }
   326	    return payload

   329	def _ep(method, path, positive_outcome, negative_outcome, **extra):
   330	    ep = {
   331	        "method": method, "path": path,
   332	        "positive": {"executed": True, "outcome": positive_outcome},
   333	        "negative": {"executed": True, "outcome": negative_outcome},
   334	    }
   335	    ep.update(extra)
   336	    return ep

   338	
   339	def _http_server(handler_cls):
   340	    server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), handler_cls)
   341	    threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
   342	    return server
   343	
   344	
   345	class _SilenceHandler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
   346	    def log_message(self, *args):  # 静音
   347	        pass
   348	
   349	
   350	class _OkHandler(_SilenceHandler):
   351	    def do_GET(self):  # noqa: N802
   352	        self.send_response(200)
   353	        self.end_headers()
   354	        self.wfile.write(b"ok")
   355	
   356	
   357	class _Fast500Handler(_SilenceHandler):
   358	    def do_GET(self):  # noqa: N802
   359	        self.send_response(500)  # 立即回 500——「HTTP 500 但很快」
   360	        self.end_headers()
   361	        self.wfile.write(b"boom")

   380	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381	# STA 族——站点结果/整线聚合（统一入口语义：bugscan_orchestrator + 各站）
   382	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   383	
   384	def test_STA_01_any_station_denominator_shrink_blocks_line():
   385	    """STA-01｜注入：任一站 denominator 缩小｜期望：整线 BLOCKED。
   386	
   387	    三层机械语义各断言一次：
   388	    (a) 统一入口 build_station_result：scanned<denominator → 强制
   389	        BLOCKED/NOT_EVALUATED（调用方即便自报 COMPLETED/PASS 也被改写）；
   390	    (b) validate_station_result：缩水+PASS 的手造结果必被拒（防旁路）；
   391	    (c) 站2 整线：engine 删卡（五类宇宙分母 5、实际 4）→ 该子件 BLOCKED →
   392	        站级聚合 coverage 3/4 → 整线 BLOCKED。
   393	    """
   394	    ident = dict(ST2_IDENTITY)
   395	    base_exec = {
   396	        "argv_digest": _argv_digest(["ac", "sta01"]),
   397	        "started_at": _iso_z(_utc_now()), "ended_at": _iso_z(_utc_now()),
   398	        "actual_exit_code": 0, "expected_exit_set": [0],
   399	        "assertion_verdict": "PASS",
   400	    }
   401	    # (a) 统一守卫：站4/站7 任一站缩水一律折叠 BLOCKED
   402	    for sid in (2, 4, 7):
   403	        forced = build_station_result(
   404	            run_id="ac-sta01", station_id=sid, attempt_id="att-sta01",
   405	            execution_status="COMPLETED", policy_verdict="PASS",
   406	            identity=ident, tool={"name": "ac", "version": "1"},
   407	            execution=base_exec,
   408	            coverage={"denominator": 5, "scanned": 3},
   409	        )
   410	        assert forced["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", forced
   411	        assert forced["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", forced
   412	        validate_station_result(forced)
   413	    # (b) 防旁路：结构合法但「缩水+PASS」的结果过不了校验器
   414	    #     （schema：COMPLETED+PASS 必须携带非空 artifacts——模板对齐真实契约）
   415	    green = build_station_result(
   416	        run_id="ac-sta01", station_id=3, attempt_id="att-sta01",
   417	        execution_status="COMPLETED", policy_verdict="PASS",
   418	        identity=ident, tool={"name": "ac", "version": "1"},
   419	        execution=base_exec, coverage={"denominator": 5, "scanned": 5},
   420	        artifacts=[{"cas_digest": f"sha256:{'0' * 64}", "size": 1}],
   421	    )
   422	    shrunk = dict(green)
   423	    shrunk["coverage"] = {"denominator": 5, "scanned": 4}
   424	    try:
   425	        validate_station_result(shrunk)
   426	        raise AssertionError("shrink+PASS must be rejected by validator")
   427	    except StationResultValidationError as exc:
   428	        assert "coverage shrink" in str(exc), exc
   429	    # (c) 站2 整线：engine 宇宙缩水（缺 msg 卡）→ 整线 BLOCKED
   430	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   431	        repo = _mk_st2_repo(tmp)
   432	        engine_state = repo.parent / "engine-state.json"
   433	        engine_state.write_text(json.dumps(_engine_cards(drop="msg")),
   434	                                encoding="utf-8")
   435	        baseline = repo.parent / "baseline.json"
   436	        baseline.write_text('{"clone_pairs": 1, "duplicated_lines": 10}',
   437	                            encoding="utf-8")
   438	        runner = _StubRunner([
   439	            (lambda a: a[:2] == ["npm", "audit"],
   440	             lambda a: (_ev(a, 0), _j(NPM_CLEAN), b"")),
   441	            (lambda a: a[0] == "npx", _jscpd_respond(JSCPD_REPORT)),
   442	            (lambda a: a[0] == "route-probe", lambda a: (_ev(a, 0), b"", b"")),
   443	        ])
   444	        report = Station2Static(
   445	            _next_run_id("sta1"), identity=ST2_IDENTITY, repo_dir=str(repo),
   446	            runner=runner, baseline_path=str(baseline),
   447	            engine_state_path=str(engine_state),
   448	            route_probe_argv=["route-probe", "--static"],
   449	        ).scan()
   450	        agg = report["station_result"]
   451	        engine_sub = report["scanner_results"]["engine"]
   452	        assert engine_sub["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", engine_sub
   453	        assert engine_sub["coverage"]["scanned"] == 4 \
   454	            < engine_sub["coverage"]["denominator"] == 5
   455	        assert agg["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", agg
   456	        assert agg["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", agg
   457	        assert agg["coverage"]["scanned"] == 3 < agg["coverage"]["denominator"] == 4
   458	        validate_station_result(agg)
   459

   461	def test_STA_02_per_station_fault_keeps_evidence_aggregate_blocked():
   462	    """STA-02｜注入：站2/3/4/7 逐站故障｜期望：继续独立取证，aggregate BLOCKED。
   463	
   464	    对四个站各注入一处「单件故障」，断言：
   465	    - 故障件的细粒度结果保留（独立取证不丢）；
   466	    - 健康件照常完成并留证；
   467	    - 站级/整站聚合 execution_status == BLOCKED（绝不记 PASS）。
   468	    """
   469	    # ---- 站2：supply 空输出=ERROR 子件，其余三件全绿 ----
   470	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   471	        repo = _mk_st2_repo(tmp)
   472	        engine_state = repo.parent / "engine-state.json"
   473	        engine_state.write_text(json.dumps(_engine_cards()), encoding="utf-8")
   474	        baseline = repo.parent / "baseline.json"
   475	        baseline.write_text('{"clone_pairs": 1, "duplicated_lines": 10}',
   476	                            encoding="utf-8")
   477	        runner = _StubRunner([
   478	            (lambda a: a[:2] == ["npm", "audit"], lambda a: (_ev(a, 0), b"", b"")),
   479	            (lambda a: a[0] == "npx", _jscpd_respond(JSCPD_REPORT)),
   480	            (lambda a: a[0] == "route-probe", lambda a: (_ev(a, 0), b"", b"")),
   481	        ])
   482	        report = Station2Static(
   483	            _next_run_id("sta2"), identity=ST2_IDENTITY, repo_dir=str(repo),
   484	            runner=runner, baseline_path=str(baseline),
   485	            engine_state_path=str(engine_state),
   486	            route_probe_argv=["route-probe", "--static"],
   487	        ).scan()
   488	        subs = report["scanner_results"]
   489	        assert set(subs) == {"supply", "dupscan", "engine", "route"}
   490	        assert subs["supply"]["execution_status"] == "ERROR", subs["supply"]
   491	        assert "supply" in report["notes"]
   492	        for slug in ("dupscan", "engine", "route"):
   493	            assert subs[slug]["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED", (slug, subs[slug])
   494	            assert subs[slug]["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", (slug, subs[slug])
   495	            validate_station_result(subs[slug])
   496	        agg = report["station_result"]
   497	        assert agg["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", agg
   498	        assert agg["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", agg
   499	        assert agg["coverage"]["scanned"] == 3 < agg["coverage"]["denominator"] == 4
   500	
   501	    # ---- 站3：db_drift 分母缩水（DMMF 模型数>监控面）→ 组件 BLOCKED，聚合 BLOCKED ----
   502	    dmmf = {"datamodel": {"models": [{"name": m} for m in
   503	                                     ("Order", "User", "Invoice", "Tenant", "Audit")]}}
   504	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   505	    final = contract.run(
   506	        openapi={"tool": {"name": "openapi-reconciler", "version": "ac"},
   507	                 "started_at": ts, "ended_at": ts,
   508	                 "openapi_spec": {"openapi": "3.0.0", "paths": {
   509	                     "/api/orders": {"get": {"responses": {}}}}},
   510	                 "actual_routes": [{"method": "GET", "path": "/api/orders"}]},
   511	        db_drift=_drift_payload(ts, changes=[], prisma_dmmf=dmmf),
   512	    )
   513	    assert final["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", final
   514	    assert final["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", final
   515	    components = final["coverage"]
   516	    assert components["scanned"] < components["denominator"]
   517	    assert len(contract.last_component_results) == 2  # 独立取证保留
   518	    oa_sub, db_sub = contract.last_component_results  # 组件顺序：openapi→db_drift
   519	    assert db_sub["coverage"]["denominator"] == 5 and \
   520	        db_sub["coverage"]["scanned"] == 3  # DMMF 下限抬高动态分母
   521	    assert oa_sub["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", oa_sub
   522	    validate_station_result(db_sub), validate_station_result(oa_sub)
   523	
   524	    # ---- 站4：e2e 腿缩水（3 文件只执行 2）BLOCKED + 路由腿 PASS → 合并 BLOCKED ----
   525	    manifest, _ = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("st4"))
   526	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   527	        tmpdir = Path(tmp)
   528	        repo = tmpdir / "repo"
   529	        (repo / "tests").mkdir(parents=True)
   530	        for name in ("a.test.ts", "b.test.ts", "c.test.ts"):
   531	            (repo / "tests" / name).write_text("// fake e2e\n", encoding="utf-8")
   532	        fake_vitest = tmpdir / "fake_vitest.py"
   533	        fake_vitest.write_text(FAKE_VITEST_SRC, encoding="utf-8")
   534	        server = _http_server(_OkHandler)
   535	        try:
   536	            base_url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"
   537	            e2e_leg = E2eScanner(
   538	                manifest, repo_root=str(repo),
   539	                runner=TrustedRunner(default_cwd=str(repo), timeout=60.0),
   540	                vitest_argv=[sys.executable, str(fake_vitest),
   541	                             "--outputFile={output_file}", "{tests_dir}", "--limit=2"],
   542	                timeout_s=60,
   543	            ).scan()
   544	            route_leg = RouteDynamicScanner(
   545	                manifest,
   546	                routes=[{"name": "home", "method": "GET", "path": "/",
   547	                         "expected_status": 200}],
   548	                base_url=base_url,
   549	            ).scan()
   550	            validate_station_result(e2e_leg)
   551	            validate_station_result(route_leg)
   552	            assert e2e_leg["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", e2e_leg
   553	            assert e2e_leg["coverage"]["scanned"] == 2 \
   554	                < e2e_leg["coverage"]["denominator"] == 3
   555	            assert route_leg["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", route_leg
   556	            combined = combine_station4_results([e2e_leg, route_leg])
   557	            assert combined["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", combined
   558	            assert combined["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", combined
   559	            assert combined["coverage"]["scanned"] == 3 \
   560	                < combined["coverage"]["denominator"] == 4
   561	        finally:
   562	            server.shutdown()
   563	            server.server_close()
   564	
   565	    # ---- 站7：哨兵探针不可读 BLOCKED + SLO 腿 PASS → 合并 BLOCKED ----
   566	    manifest, _ = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("st7"))
   567	    now = _utc_now()
   568	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   569	        dead_probe = Path(tmp) / "no-such-heartbeat.json"
   570	        sentinel_leg = SentinelHealthScanner(
   571	            manifest,
   572	            sentinels=[{"name": "argus", "kind": "heartbeat_json",
   573	                        "path": str(dead_probe), "freshness_s": 300}],
   574	            now=now,
   575	        ).scan()
   576	        slo_leg = SloMonitorScanner(
   577	            manifest,
   578	            slos=[{"name": "availability", "target": 0.999, "op": ">="},
   579	                  {"name": "p95_latency_ms", "target": 800, "op": "<="}],
   580	            snapshot={"availability": 0.9995, "p95_latency_ms": 640.0},
   581	            now=now,
   582	        ).scan()
   583	        validate_station_result(sentinel_leg)
   584	        validate_station_result(slo_leg)
   585	        assert sentinel_leg["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", sentinel_leg
   586	        assert slo_leg["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", slo_leg
   587	        combined = combine_station7_results([sentinel_leg, slo_leg])
   588	        assert combined["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", combined
   589	        assert combined["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", combined
