   596	def test_DB_01_all_change_directions_recognized():
   597	    """DB-01｜注入：[+]/[-]/[*]｜期望：全识别。
   598	
   599	    注入面映射说明：judge_drift 的方向标记在本适配器契约里对应 change_type
   600	    分类——[+]=新增类（CREATE/COLUMN_ADD）、[-]=删除类（COLUMN_DROP/TABLE_DROP/
   601	    DROP）、[*]=修改类（ALTER/MODIFY/RENAME）。断言：
   602	    - 九个方向各投一条 change → 恰好 9 条 finding（一条不漏=全识别）；
   603	    - 未知 change_type 不被丢弃且定级 HIGH（fail-closed 最坏处理）；
   604	    - 方向→严重级映射正源（DROP 类=HIGH）不被稀释。
   605	    """
   606	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["COLUMN_DROP"] == "HIGH"
   607	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["TABLE_DROP"] == "HIGH"
   608	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["DROP"] == "HIGH"
   609	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["CREATE"] == "MEDIUM"
   610	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["COLUMN_ADD"] == "MEDIUM"
   611	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["ALTER"] == "MEDIUM"
   612	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["MODIFY"] == "MEDIUM"
   613	    assert DRIFT_CHANGE_SEVERITY["RENAME"] == "LOW"
   614	    assert UNKNOWN_CHANGE_SEVERITY == "HIGH"
   615	    changes = [
   616	        {"object": "public.orders", "change_type": "CREATE"},          # [+]
   617	        {"object": "public.users", "change_type": "COLUMN_ADD"},       # [+]
   618	        {"object": "public.orders", "change_type": "COLUMN_DROP"},     # [-]
   619	        {"object": "public.audit_log", "change_type": "TABLE_DROP"},   # [-]
   620	        {"object": "public.orders_fk_users", "change_type": "DROP"},   # [-]
   621	        {"object": "public.invoices", "change_type": "ALTER"},         # [*]
   622	        {"object": "public.settings", "change_type": "MODIFY"},        # [*]
   623	        {"object": "public.tenants", "change_type": "RENAME"},         # [*]
   624	        {"object": "public.weird", "change_type": "SOMETHING_ELSE"},   # 未知
   625	    ]
   626	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   627	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   628	        _drift_payload(ts, objects_total=9, changes=changes))
   629	    validate_station_result(st)
   630	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
   631	    assert st["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED"
   632	    assert len(st["finding_ids"]) == 9, st["finding_ids"]  # 全识别：9 投 9 中
   633	    assert st["coverage"] == {"denominator": 9, "scanned": 9}
   634	    # 未知类型也产出独立 finding（含对象名），不静默吞掉
   635	    assert any("weird" in fid for fid in st["finding_ids"]), st["finding_ids"]
   636	
   637	
   638	def test_DB_02_single_drop_column_or_fk_still_fails():
   639	    """DB-02｜注入：仅一条 DROP COLUMN/删 FK｜期望：FAIL，不因数量少放行。
   640	
   641	    两条独立注入：单条 COLUMN_DROP；单条 FK 删除（约束对象 DROP）。
   642	    各自必须 FAIL 且恰 1 条 finding（exit 1=发现落账语义），绝不 PASS。
   643	    """
   644	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   645	    for change in (
   646	        {"object": "public.orders", "change_type": "COLUMN_DROP",
   647	         "expected_hash": "a" * 64},
   648	        {"object": "public.orders_fk_users", "change_type": "DROP",
   649	         "expected_hash": "b" * 64},
   650	    ):
   651	        st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   652	            _drift_payload(ts, changes=[change]))
   653	        validate_station_result(st)
   654	        assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", (change, st)
   655	        assert st["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED"
   656	        assert len(st["finding_ids"]) == 1, st["finding_ids"]
   657	        assert st["execution"]["actual_exit_code"] == 1  # 有发现→落账退出码
   658	
   659	
   660	def test_DB_03_unknown_format_empty_output_rejected():
   661	    """DB-03｜注入：未知格式/空输出/命令非零｜期望：ERROR。
   662	
   663	    适配器 fail-closed 语义=畸形载荷直接拒绝（抛 DriftReportMalformed/
   664	    ContractPayloadMalformed），不产出任何结果——ERROR 类且绝不折算 PASS。
   665	    - 未知格式：changes 非列表 / 条目缺 change_type / hash 非 64hex；
   666	    - 空输出：空载荷/缺必备字段（judge_drift 命令非零→无有效产物，走同一
   667	      拒绝面——本包适配器不执行命令，该子注入与空输出同型，属契约边界）。
   668	    """
   669	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   670	    scanner = contract.db_drift_scanner()
   671	    bad_payloads = [
   672	        _drift_payload(ts, changes="not-a-list"),                       # 未知格式
   673	        _drift_payload(ts, changes=[{"object": "public.orders"}]),      # 缺 change_type
   674	        _drift_payload(ts, changes=[{"object": "public.orders",
   675	                                     "change_type": "DROP",
   676	                                     "expected_hash": "zz"}]),          # hash 非 64hex
   677	        _drift_payload(ts, objects_total=0),                            # 分母非法
   678	        {},                                                             # 空输出
   679	    ]
   680	    for bad in bad_payloads:
   681	        try:
   682	            scanner.adapt(bad)
   683	            raise AssertionError(f"malformed payload must be rejected: {bad!r}")
   684	        except (DriftReportMalformed, ContractPayloadMalformed):
   685	            pass
   686	
   687	
   688	def test_DB_04_allowlist_expiry_or_hash_change_fails():
   689	    """DB-04｜注入：allowlist 到期/对象 hash 变化｜期望：FAIL。
   690	
   691	    - 白名单条目已过期（expires_at < now）→ 条目无效 → 漂移照常 FAIL；
   692	    - 对象内容 hash 变化（content_hash ≠ 漂移绑定 hash）→ 三绑定不中 → FAIL；
   693	    - 对照组：identity+hash+未过期全中 → 受治理漂移不落 finding（PASS）；
   694	    - 到期晚于 now+30d 日落窗 → 条目本身非法（AllowlistEntryInvalid）。
   695	    """
   696	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   697	    now = _utc_now()
   698	    drop = {"object": "public.orders", "change_type": "DROP",
   699	            "expected_hash": "a" * 64}
   700	    fresh = {"object_identity": "public.orders", "content_hash": "a" * 64,
   701	             "expires_at": _iso_z(now + timedelta(days=7)),
   702	             "approver": "dba-owner", "reason": "归档下线已审批"}
   703	    expired = dict(fresh, expires_at=_iso_z(now - timedelta(days=1)))
   704	    hash_changed = dict(fresh, content_hash="c" * 64)
   705	
   706	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   707	        _drift_payload(ts, changes=[drop], allowlist=[fresh]))
   708	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS" and not st["finding_ids"], st  # 对照
   709	
   710	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   711	        _drift_payload(ts, changes=[drop], allowlist=[expired]))
   712	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st  # 到期=无效
   713	    assert len(st["finding_ids"]) == 1
   714	
   715	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   716	        _drift_payload(ts, changes=[drop], allowlist=[hash_changed]))
   717	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st  # 对象 hash 变化=不匹配
   718	    assert len(st["finding_ids"]) == 1
   719	
   720	    beyond = dict(fresh, expires_at=_iso_z(now + timedelta(days=90)))
   721	    try:
   722	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
   723	            _drift_payload(ts, changes=[drop], allowlist=[beyond]))
   724	        raise AssertionError("beyond-30d sunset must be rejected")
   725	    except AllowlistEntryInvalid:
   726	        pass
   727	
   728	
   729	def test_DB_05_wrong_db_role_or_writable_source_blocked():
   730	    """DB-05｜注入：错 DB/role/transaction_read_only=off｜期望：BLOCKED。
   731	
   732	    本发行包的等价机械语义：证据身份绑定失败 → 适配器 fail-closed 抛错、
   733	    拒绝产出任何结果（含 PASS）——BLOCKED 的最强形态（铁律：绝不带可写/
   734	    异库身份出账）。四路注入：
   735	    - read_only=False（=transaction_read_only off）→ NonReadOnlyDataSource；
   736	    - data_config 与冻结 manifest 不一致（错 DB/配置漂移）→ EvidenceIdentityMismatch；
   737	    - diff_direction 非法（翻向证据不可混用）→ DriftReportMalformed；
   738	    - connection_identity 疑似带凭据 → 证据身份拒绝入库。
   739	    """
   740	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   741	    drift_ok = _drift_payload(ts, changes=[])
   742	
   743	    try:
   744	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   745	            ts, db_identity=dict(AC_DB_ID, read_only=False)))
   746	        raise AssertionError("writable identity must be refused")
   747	    except NonReadOnlyDataSource:
   748	        pass
   749	
   750	    try:
   751	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   752	            ts, data_config={"db": {"connection_identity": "host=other;db=wrong"}}))
   753	        raise AssertionError("data_config mismatch must be refused")
   754	    except EvidenceIdentityMismatch:
   755	        pass
   756	
   757	    try:
   758	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   759	            ts, db_identity=dict(AC_DB_ID, diff_direction="reversed")))
   760	        raise AssertionError("unknown diff direction must be refused")
   761	    except DriftReportMalformed:
   762	        pass
   763	
   764	    try:
   765	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   766	            ts, db_identity=dict(AC_DB_ID,
   767	                                 connection_identity="host=db;user=u;password=x")))
   768	        raise AssertionError("credential-ish identity must be refused")
   769	    except DriftReportMalformed:
   770	        pass
   771	
   772	    # 对照：只读+方向合法+data_config 一致 → 正常出结果（无阻断）
   773	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(drift_ok)
   774	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
   775	
   776	
   777	def test_DB_06_golden_normalization_order_canonical():
   778	    """DB-06｜注入：catalog 顺序、默认表达式、extension 对象｜期望：golden 规范化正确。
   779	
   780	    覆盖边界（诚实声明）：默认表达式/extension 对象的 golden 规范化属
   781	    judge_drift.py（上游工具，不在本发行包）；本包可测的规范化面为证据
   782	    绑定链的 canonical 归一：
   783	    - 成员（键）顺序无关：_hash_obj 对键序规范化，catalog 打乱顺序摘要不变；
   784	    - 确定性：同内容重复计算摘要稳定；
   785	    - 受监控对象全集必须是去重集合（重复 identity=golden 自相矛盾→拒绝）。
   786	    """
   787	    a = {"connection_identity": "host=db-ac;db=erp;user=ro_scan",
   788	         "read_only": True, "diff_direction": "actual_vs_expected"}
   789	    b = {k: a[k] for k in reversed(list(a))}  # 键序打乱
   790	    assert _hash_obj(a) == _hash_obj(b)
   791	    assert _hash_obj(a) == _hash_obj(a)  # 确定性
   792	
   793	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
   794	    st1 = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(ts, changes=[]))
   795	    # db_identity 键序不同的等价载荷 → 结果 coverage/verdict 一致（规范化无漂移）
   796	    st2 = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   797	        ts, db_identity={k: AC_DB_ID[k] for k in reversed(list(AC_DB_ID))},
   798	        changes=[]))
   799	    assert st1["policy_verdict"] == st2["policy_verdict"] == "PASS"
   800	    assert st1["coverage"] == st2["coverage"]
   801	
   802	    try:
   803	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(_drift_payload(
   804	            ts, objects_total=2,
   805	            expected_objects=["public.orders", "public.orders"]))
   806	        raise AssertionError("duplicate expected_objects must be rejected")
   807	    except DriftReportMalformed:
   808	        pass
   809

   811	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   812	# SC 族——供应链（station2.SupplyChainScanner：npm audit 适配）
   813	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   814	
   815	def _supply_scan(tmp_path, payload_bytes, rc=1):
   816	    repo = Path(tmp_path)
   817	    repo.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   818	    runner = _StubRunner([
   819	        (lambda a: a[:2] == ["npm", "audit"],
   820	         lambda a: (_ev(a, rc), payload_bytes, b"")),
   821	    ])
   822	    return SupplyChainScanner(runner=runner, run_id=_next_run_id("sc"),
   823	                              identity=ST2_IDENTITY, repo_dir=str(repo)).scan()
   824	
   825	
   826	def test_SC_01_valid_json_high_critical_fails():
   827	    """SC-01｜注入：有效 JSON+HIGH/CRITICAL｜期望：FAIL。
   828	
   829	    有效 JSON 载荷含 HIGH 与 CRITICAL 漏洞 → FAIL；findings 计数取解析列表
   830	    （非自报数字）；HIGH→P1、CRITICAL→P0 不降级；coverage 分母=metadata 自报
   831	    依赖总数（合法分母正源）。
   832	    """
   833	    payload = _npm_vulns([
   834	        ("lodash", "high", "<4.17.21", "Prototype Pollution"),
   835	        ("minimist", "critical", "<1.2.6", "Prototype Pollution"),
   836	        ("qs", "moderate", ">=6.0.0 <6.0.4", "DoS"),
   837	    ])
   838	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   839	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(payload))
   840	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   841	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", rep.result
   842	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED"
   843	        assert len(rep.findings) == 3  # 解析列表为正源（含 moderate 也落账）
   844	        highs = [f for f in rep.findings if f.severity == "HIGH"]
   845	        crits = [f for f in rep.findings if f.severity == "CRITICAL"]
   846	        assert len(highs) == 1 and highs[0].priority == "P1"
   847	        assert len(crits) == 1 and crits[0].priority == "P0"
   848	        assert rep.result["finding_ids"] == [f.finding_id for f in rep.findings]
   849	
   850	
   851	def test_SC_02_empty_json_or_registry_timeout_error():
   852	    """SC-02｜注入：空 JSON/registry 超时｜期望：ERROR。
   853	
   854	    - 空输出 → ERROR（T-07：空输出≠零发现）；
   855	    - registry 超时（Evidence.timed_out）→ TIMEOUT/退出码 124（ERROR 类，
   856	      绝不折算 PASS）；
   857	    - npm exit ≥2（registry 不可达等环境错）→ ERROR。
   858	    """
   859	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   860	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, b"", rc=0)  # 空输出
   861	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", rep.result
   862	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED"
   863	        assert "empty" in rep.note, rep.note
   864	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   865	
   866	        repo = Path(tmp)
   867	        runner = _StubRunner([
   868	            (lambda a: a[:2] == ["npm", "audit"],
   869	             lambda a: (_ev_timeout(a), b"", b"")),
   870	        ])
   871	        rep = SupplyChainScanner(runner=runner, run_id=_next_run_id("sc"),
   872	                                 identity=ST2_IDENTITY,
   873	                                 repo_dir=str(repo)).scan()
   874	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "TIMEOUT", rep.result
   875	        assert rep.result["execution"]["actual_exit_code"] == 124
   876	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED"
   877	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   878	
   879	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, b"network unreachable", rc=2)  # registry 环境错
   880	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", rep.result
   881	        assert "exit code 2" in rep.note, rep.note
   882	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   883

   885	def test_SC_04_skip_audit_title_no_bypass():
   886	    """SC-04｜注入：标题含 [skip-audit]｜期望：无绕过效果。
   887	
   888	    漏洞条目标题（via[0].title）带 [skip-audit] 标记 → 判定矩阵不变：
   889	    仍 FAIL、finding 照常落账；站2 聚合层同样 FAIL——标题标记零旁路效果。
   890	    """
   891	    payload = _npm_vulns([
   892	        ("lodash", "high", "<4.17.21", "[skip-audit] Prototype Pollution"),
   893	    ])
   894	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   895	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(payload))
   896	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", rep.result
   897	        assert len(rep.findings) == 1
   898	        assert rep.findings[0].severity == "HIGH"
   899	        assert "[skip-audit]" in rep.findings[0].message, rep.findings[0].message
   900	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   901	
   902	        repo = _mk_st2_repo(tmp)
   903	        runner = _StubRunner([
   904	            (lambda a: a[:2] == ["npm", "audit"],
   905	             lambda a: (_ev(a, 1), _j(payload), b"")),
   906	        ])
   907	        report = Station2Static(
   908	            _next_run_id("sc4"), identity=ST2_IDENTITY, repo_dir=str(repo),
   909	            runner=runner, scanners=("supply",),
   910	        ).scan()
   911	        agg = report["station_result"]
   912	        assert agg["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", agg  # 聚合层同样无绕过
   913	        assert agg["coverage"]["denominator"] == 1 and agg["coverage"]["scanned"] == 1
   914	        assert "dupscan" in agg["coverage"]["exclusions"]  # 显式缩件记账

   921	def test_ADV_01_version_parser_anomaly_unknown_not_silent():
   922	    """ADV-01｜注入：version parser 异常｜期望：UNKNOWN，不写 seen。
   923	
   924	    本发行包等价语义（站2 advisory 解析通道）：版本/区间不可解析或 severity
   925	    缺失的公告条目绝不静默当作「已看/干净」跳过——保守按 UNKNOWN 方向升
   926	    HIGH/P1 并写入 findings 账（后续轮次仍可见）；条目结构性损坏（非对象）
   927	    → 整份报告按证据损坏 ERROR。
   928	    """
   929	    anomaly = {
   930	        "auditReportVersion": 2,
   931	        "vulnerabilities": {
   932	            "weird-pkg": {  # severity 缺失 + range 非 semver 形态（parser 异常）
   933	                "name": "weird-pkg", "severity": "", "range": "!!!not-semver!!!",
   934	                "via": ["unparseable"], "effects": [], "fixAvailable": False},
   935	        },
   936	        "metadata": {"vulnerabilities": {"info": 0, "low": 0, "moderate": 0,
   937	                                         "high": 1, "critical": 0},
   938	                     "dependencies": {"prod": 5, "dev": 1, "optional": 0,
   939	                                      "peer": 0, "total": 6}},
   940	    }
   941	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   942	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(anomaly))
   943	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", rep.result
   944	        assert len(rep.findings) == 1
   945	        f = rep.findings[0]
   946	        assert f.severity == "HIGH" and f.priority == "P1"  # UNKNOWN→保守升
   947	        assert "weird-pkg" in f.message and "!!!not-semver!!!" in f.message
   948	        assert rep.result["finding_ids"] == [f.finding_id]  # 写入账：不静默跳过
   949	
   950	        corrupt = dict(anomaly)
   951	        corrupt["vulnerabilities"] = {"bad": "not-an-object"}
   952	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(corrupt))
   953	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", rep.result  # 损坏→ERROR
   954	
   955	
   956	def test_ADV_02_multi_page_multi_range_full_traversal():
   957	    """ADV-02｜注入：多页/多 range｜期望：全遍历。
   958	
   959	    多页公告合流成单份 stdout + 每条带多 union/跨度 range → 解析列表全量
   960	    遍历：6 投 6 中、finding_ids 互异且全在账；HIGH+ 计数交叉核对通过。
   961	    截断子例（分页不全：自报 7 条 HIGH+ 但解析仅 5）→ 按证据损坏 ERROR。
   962	    """
   963	    entries = [
   964	        ("lodash", "high", ">=1.0.0 <2.3.4 || 3.x", "Prototype Pollution"),
   965	        ("minimist", "critical", "<1.2.6", "Prototype Pollution"),
   966	        ("node-fetch", "high", ">=2.0.0 <2.6.7", "SSRF"),
   967	        ("qs", "moderate", "6.x", "DoS"),
   968	        ("ua-parser-js", "high", "0.7.18 - 0.7.29", "ReDoS"),
   969	        ("shell-quote", "high", ">=1.6.0 <1.7.3", "RCE"),
   970	    ]
   971	    payload = _npm_vulns(entries, dep_total=37)
   972	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   973	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(payload))
   974	        validate_station_result(rep.result)
   975	        assert rep.result["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", rep.result
   976	        assert len(rep.findings) == 6, rep.findings  # 全遍历：一条不漏
   977	        assert len(set(rep.result["finding_ids"])) == 6
   978	        assert rep.result["coverage"] == {"denominator": 37, "scanned": 37}
   979	        assert sum(1 for f in rep.findings
   980	                   if f.severity in ("HIGH", "CRITICAL")) == 5
   981	
   982	        truncated = _npm_vulns(entries, dep_total=37)
   983	        truncated["metadata"]["vulnerabilities"]["high"] = 6  # 自报 7 条 HIGH+
   984	        rep = _supply_scan(tmp, _j(truncated))
   985	        assert rep.result["execution_status"] == "ERROR", rep.result
   986	        assert "self-reported" in rep.note, rep.note
   987

   993	def test_TEN_01_prisma_alias_reexport_wrapper_in_denominator():
   994	    """TEN-01｜注入：Prisma alias/re-export/wrapper｜期望：纳入分析。
   995	
   996	    覆盖边界：本包不做源级 AST 分析；Prisma 侧分母正源=DMMF（编译后已解析
   997	    alias/re-export/wrapper 形态，统一表现为 datamodel.models 条目）。断言：
   998	    - 别名/包装形态的模型条目全部计入 prisma_dmmf_model_count 与动态分母；
   999	    - 监控面（objects_total）少于含别名的模型全集 → 分母缩水守卫 BLOCKED
  1000	      （纳入分析=不可因别名形态漏出分母）；
  1001	    - 重复名（假 alias 双计）被拒绝，防分母虚增。
  1002	    """
  1003	    models = ("Order", "User", "Invoice",
  1004	              "Organization",          # 正主 tenant model
  1005	              "OrganizationView",      # wrapper 视图模型
  1006	              "Org")                   # alias 形态
  1007	    dmmf = {"datamodel": {"models": [{"name": n} for n in models]}}
  1008	    assert prisma_dmmf_model_count(dmmf) == 6
  1009	    assert derive_dynamic_denominator(prisma_dmmf=dmmf)["denominator"] == 6
  1010	
  1011	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1012	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner(prisma_dmmf=dmmf).adapt(
  1013	        _drift_payload(ts, objects_total=4, changes=[]))
  1014	    assert st["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", st
  1015	    assert st["coverage"] == {"denominator": 6, "scanned": 4}
  1016	
  1017	    dup = {"datamodel": {"models": [{"name": "Organization"},
  1018	                                     {"name": "Organization"}]}}
  1019	    try:
  1020	        prisma_dmmf_model_count(dup)
  1021	        raise AssertionError("duplicate model names must be rejected")
  1022	    except Station3Error:
  1023	        pass
  1024	
  1025	
  1026	def test_TEN_02_comment_organizationId_cannot_fool():
  1027	    """TEN-02｜注入：注释含 organizationId｜期望：不能欺骗。
  1028	
  1029	    探针结果里夹带宣称「organizationId 已过滤」的注释字段——判定只认
  1030	    positive/negative 的真实执行结论：负例放行照样 FAIL（注释不能把越权
  1031	    说成安全），负例真拦时注释也不制造假阳性。
  1032	    """
  1033	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1034	    leaked = _og_payload(ts, [
  1035	        _ep("GET", "/api/orders", "ALLOWED", "ALLOWED",
  1036	            comment="// organizationId filter applied (see PR#123)"),
  1037	    ])
  1038	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(leaked)
  1039	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
  1040	    assert any("negative_allowed" in f for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1041	    assert st["coverage"] == {"denominator": 2, "scanned": 2}  # 注释不影响计量
  1042	
  1043	    honest = _og_payload(ts, [
  1044	        _ep("GET", "/api/orders", "ALLOWED", "DENIED",
  1045	            comment="// organizationId filter applied"),
  1046	    ])
  1047	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(honest)
  1048	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1049	    assert not st["finding_ids"]
  1050	    validate_station_result(st)
  1051

  1053	def test_TEN_03_long_builder_included_in_analysis():
  1054	    """TEN-03｜注入：builder 超 300 字符｜期望：纳入分析。
  1055	
  1056	    覆盖边界：本包无 token 级 builder 解析；「纳入分析」的等价机械语义=
  1057	    超长（>300 字符）builder 形态路径计入端点宇宙分母并被真实判定：
  1058	    - 全用例矩阵覆盖该端点 → scanned==denominator（PASS，确证纳入）；
  1059	    - 该端点负例放行 → finding 照常触发（分析真判它，不因长度跳过）。
  1060	    """
  1061	    long_path = "/api/org/" + "b" * 320 + "/relations?include=filters"
  1062	    assert len(long_path) > 300
  1063	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1064	    ok = _og_payload(ts, [_ep("GET", long_path, "ALLOWED", "DENIED")])
  1065	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(ok)
  1066	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1067	    assert st["coverage"] == {"denominator": 2, "scanned": 2}  # 计入分母
  1068	
  1069	    bad = _og_payload(ts, [_ep("GET", long_path, "ALLOWED", "ALLOWED")])
  1070	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(bad)
  1071	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
  1072	    assert any("negative_allowed" in f for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1073

  1075	def test_TEN_05_non_export_download_path_scanned():
  1076	    """TEN-05｜注入：路径不含 export/download 的下载｜期望：纳入扫描。
  1077	
  1078	    端点宇宙=spec∪实际探测（无 export/download 关键字白名单）——不含这
  1079	    两个关键词的取数路径照样进分母并被正/负例判定；其负例放行时 finding
  1080	    照发（扫描到位才会抓到），正例通负例拦时 PASS。
  1081	    """
  1082	    path_no_keywords = "/api/tenants/fetch-records"  # 不含 export/download
  1083	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1084	    leaked = _og_payload(ts, [
  1085	        _ep("GET", path_no_keywords, "ALLOWED", "ALLOWED"),
  1086	    ])
  1087	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(leaked)
  1088	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
  1089	    assert st["coverage"]["denominator"] == 2  # 纳入分母（正例+负例）
  1090	    assert any("fetch-records" in f or "negative_allowed" in f
  1091	               for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1092	
  1093	    ok = _og_payload(ts, [_ep("GET", path_no_keywords, "ALLOWED", "DENIED")])
  1094	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(ok)
  1095	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1096	    assert st["coverage"] == {"denominator": 2, "scanned": 2}
  1097

  1099	def test_TEN_06_org_a_access_org_b_dynamically_denied():
  1100	    """TEN-06｜注入：Org A 访问/导出 Org B｜期望：动态拒绝。
  1101	
  1102	    动态=运行时探针实测结论（非静态声明）：orgB 主体对 orgA 资源的负例
  1103	    放行（未动态拒绝）→ FAIL+negative_allowed finding；真实 DENIED → PASS。
  1104	    """
  1105	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1106	    principals = {"base_url": "http://app.local",
  1107	                  "positive_principal": "orgA/alice",
  1108	                  "negative_principal": "orgB/mallory"}
  1109	    leaked = _og_payload(ts, [
  1110	        _ep("GET", "/api/orgs/%7BorgA%7D/exports", "ALLOWED", "ALLOWED"),
  1111	    ], principals=principals)
  1112	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(leaked)
  1113	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
  1114	    assert any("negative_allowed" in f for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1115	    validate_station_result(st)
  1116	
  1117	    denied = _og_payload(ts, [
  1118	        _ep("GET", "/api/orgs/%7BorgA%7D/exports", "ALLOWED", "DENIED"),
  1119	    ], principals=principals)
  1120	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(denied)
  1121	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1122	    assert not st["finding_ids"]
  1123

  1125	def test_TEN_07_new_tenant_model_grows_denominator():
  1126	    """TEN-07｜注入：target SHA 新增 tenant model｜期望：分母自动增加。
  1127	
  1128	    同一监控面（objects_total=5）：DMMF 5 模型 → 分母 5、scanned 5 → PASS；
  1129	    target SHA 的 DMMF 新增 tenant model（6 个）→ 分母自动升至 6、scanned 5
  1130	    → 缩水守卫 BLOCKED（新模型不可静默漏出监控面）。
  1131	    """
  1132	    dmmf_5 = {"datamodel": {"models": [{"name": n} for n in
  1133	                                        ("Order", "User", "Invoice",
  1134	                                         "Tenant", "Audit")]}}
  1135	    dmmf_6 = {"datamodel": {"models": [{"name": n} for n in
  1136	                                        ("Order", "User", "Invoice",
  1137	                                         "Tenant", "Audit", "TenantProfile")]}}
  1138	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1139	    st5 = contract.db_drift_scanner(prisma_dmmf=dmmf_5).adapt(
  1140	        _drift_payload(ts, objects_total=5, changes=[]))
  1141	    assert st5["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st5
  1142	    assert st5["coverage"] == {"denominator": 5, "scanned": 5}
  1143	
  1144	    st6 = contract.db_drift_scanner(prisma_dmmf=dmmf_6).adapt(
  1145	        _drift_payload(ts, objects_total=5, changes=[]))
  1146	    assert st6["coverage"]["denominator"] == 6, st6  # 分母自动增加
  1147	    assert st6["coverage"]["scanned"] == 5
  1148	    assert st6["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", st6
  1149	    assert st6["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", st6
  1150

  1152	def test_TEN_08_admin_cross_tenant_passes_role_negative_rejected():
  1153	    """TEN-08｜注入：合法管理员跨租户正例/普通角色负例｜期望：前者通、后者拒。
  1154	
  1155	    正例=管理员跨租户合法访问（positive 必须 ALLOWED=通）；负例=普通角色
  1156	    跨租户（negative 必须 DENIED=拒）→ 双全时 PASS。任一翻转（正例被拒/
  1157	    负例放行）→ 对应 finding+FAIL。
  1158	    """
  1159	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(AC_DATA_CONFIG)
  1160	    principals = {"base_url": "http://app.local",
  1161	                  "positive_principal": "orgA/platform-admin",
  1162	                  "negative_principal": "orgA/viewer"}
  1163	    both_ok = _og_payload(ts, [
  1164	        _ep("GET", "/api/admin/tenant-export", "ALLOWED", "DENIED"),
  1165	    ], principals=principals)
  1166	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(both_ok)
  1167	    validate_station_result(st)
  1168	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st  # 前者通、后者拒
  1169	
  1170	    role_leak = _og_payload(ts, [
  1171	        _ep("GET", "/api/admin/tenant-export", "ALLOWED", "ALLOWED"),
  1172	    ], principals=principals)
  1173	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(role_leak)
  1174	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st  # 普通角色负例必须拒
  1175	    assert any("negative_allowed" in f for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1176	
  1177	    admin_rejected = _og_payload(ts, [
  1178	        _ep("GET", "/api/admin/tenant-export", "DENIED", "DENIED"),
  1179	    ], principals=principals)
  1180	    st = contract.org_guard_scanner().adapt(admin_rejected)
  1181	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st  # 合法管理员正例必须通
  1182	    assert any("positive_rejected" in f for f in st["finding_ids"])

  1188	
  1189	def test_PERF_01_http_500_fast_still_fails():
  1190	    """PERF-01｜注入：HTTP 500 但很快｜期望：FAIL。
  1191	
  1192	    本地真实 HTTP 服务立即回 500（快）；路由动态腿只认状态语义：探到但
  1193	    与期望不符 → FAIL+finding。快≠好——速度不参与判定，绝不因响应快折算
  1194	    通过（覆盖边界：本包不测延迟阈值，分位数面见文件头部不可实现清单）。
  1195	    """
  1196	    manifest, _ = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("perf1"))
  1197	    server = _http_server(_Fast500Handler)
  1198	    try:
  1199	        base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"
  1200	        t0 = time.time()
  1201	        st = RouteDynamicScanner(
  1202	            manifest,
  1203	            routes=[{"name": "orders", "method": "GET", "path": "/",
  1204	                     "expected_status": 200}],
  1205	            base_url=base, request_timeout_s=5.0,
  1206	        ).scan()
  1207	        elapsed = time.time() - t0
  1208	        assert elapsed < 3.0, elapsed  # 响应确实很快
  1209	        validate_station_result(st)
  1210	        assert st["coverage"]["scanned"] == 1  # 探到了（拿到真实 500 响应）
  1211	        assert st["policy_verdict"] == "FAIL", st
  1212	        assert st["execution_status"] == "COMPLETED"
  1213	        assert st["finding_ids"] and st["finding_ids"][0].startswith("fnd_route_")
  1214	    finally:
  1215	        server.shutdown()
  1216	        server.server_close()
  1217	
  1218	
  1219	def test_PERF_02_null_metrics_error_class():
  1220	    """PERF_02｜注入：SSH/TCC/null 指标｜期望：ERROR。
  1221	
  1222	    覆盖边界：SSH/TCC 采集通道不在本包；等价机械语义=指标采集缺口
  1223	    （null/非数值/NaN）按未扫计：assertion=ERROR、策略 NOT_EVALUATED、
  1224	    缩水守卫折叠 BLOCKED——绝不折算 PASS，也绝不当作达标值。
  1225	    （函数名用下划线形态 PERF_02 与连字符形态同时登记追踪。）
  1226	    """
  1227	    manifest, now = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("perf2"))
  1228	    slos = [{"name": "availability", "target": 0.999, "op": ">="},
  1229	            {"name": "p95_latency_ms", "target": 800, "op": "<="}]
  1230	    for bad_value in (None, "640ms", float("nan")):
  1231	        st = SloMonitorScanner(
  1232	            manifest, slos=slos,
  1233	            snapshot={"availability": 0.9995, "p95_latency_ms": bad_value},
  1234	            now=now,
  1235	        ).scan()
  1236	        validate_station_result(st)
  1237	        assert st["coverage"]["scanned"] == 1 < st["coverage"]["denominator"] == 2
  1238	        assert st["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", st
  1239	        assert st["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", st
  1240	        assert st["execution"]["assertion_verdict"] == "ERROR", st
  1241	        assert any(f.startswith("fnd_slo_missing") for f in st["finding_ids"])
  1242	    # 对照：数值齐全且达标 → PASS（缺口语义没有误伤正常面）
  1243	    st = SloMonitorScanner(
  1244	        manifest, slos=slos,
  1245	        snapshot={"availability": 0.9995, "p95_latency_ms": 640.0},
  1246	        now=now,
  1247	    ).scan()
  1248	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1249	
  1250	
  1251	def test_PERF_05_baseline_threshold_tamper_blocked_by_hash():
  1252	    """PERF-05｜注入：修改 baseline/阈值试图洗绿｜期望：policy hash 阻断。
  1253	
  1254	    覆盖边界：本包无独立性能基线文件；「基线/阈值冻结哈希阻断」的等价
  1255	    机械语义=两道真实哈希绑定：
  1256	    - run manifest 冻结 data_config（含阈值）后，载荷携带被改阈值 →
  1257	      data_config_hash 不一致 → EvidenceIdentityMismatch 拒绝（洗绿失败）；
  1258	    - 落盘 manifest 被改字节 → manifest_hash 校验失败 → ManifestFreezeError。
  1259	    """
  1260	    frozen = {"db": dict(AC_DB_ID),
  1261	              "perf_thresholds": {"p95_ms": 800, "error_rate": 0.01}}
  1262	    contract, ts = _mk_st3(frozen)
  1263	    tampered = {"db": dict(AC_DB_ID),
  1264	                "perf_thresholds": {"p95_ms": 8000, "error_rate": 0.5}}  # 洗绿
  1265	    try:
  1266	        contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
  1267	            _drift_payload(ts, data_config=tampered, changes=[]))
  1268	        raise AssertionError("threshold tamper must be blocked by frozen hash")
  1269	    except EvidenceIdentityMismatch:
  1270	        pass
  1271	    # 原封不动的冻结配置可通过（阻断只针对篡改）
  1272	    st = contract.db_drift_scanner().adapt(
  1273	        _drift_payload(ts, data_config=frozen, changes=[]))
  1274	    assert st["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", st
  1275	
  1276	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
  1277	        path = Path(tmp) / "run-manifest.json"
  1278	        contract.manifest.save(path)
  1279	        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1280	        data["identity"]["data_config_hash"] = "f" * 64  # 篡改冻结件
  1281	        path.write_text(json.dumps(data), encoding="utf-8")
  1282	        try:
  1283	            type(contract.manifest).load(path)
  1284	            raise AssertionError("tampered manifest must fail hash check")
  1285	        except ManifestFreezeError:
  1286	            pass
  1287	
  1288	
  1289	def test_PERF_06_endpoint_denominator_shrink_blocks_promotion():
  1290	    """PERF-06｜注入：样本/时长/endpoint 分母缩水｜期望：不得晋升 baseline。
  1291	
  1292	    等价机械语义（覆盖边界见 docstring 顶部族说明）：endpoint 分母 2、实际
  1293	    只探到 1（另一端点不可达=样本缩水）→ 缩水守卫强制 BLOCKED/NOT_EVALUATED
  1294	    ——结果不可作为通过/晋升依据；直接构造 scanned<denominator 的结果同样
  1295	    被统一入口折叠 BLOCKED。
  1296	    """
  1297	    manifest, _ = _mk_manifest(_next_run_id("perf6"))
  1298	    server = _http_server(_OkHandler)
  1299	    try:
  1300	        base = f"http://127.0.0.1:{server.server_address[1]}"
  1301	        st = RouteDynamicScanner(
  1302	            manifest,
  1303	            routes=[
  1304	                {"name": "home", "method": "GET", "path": "/",
  1305	                 "expected_status": 200},
  1306	                {"name": "ghost", "method": "GET", "path": "/missing",
  1307	                 "expected_status": 404},
  1308	            ],
  1309	            base_url="http://127.0.0.1:1",  # 不可达 → ghost 缩水
  1310	            request_timeout_s=1.0,
  1311	        ).scan()
  1312	        # home 也走不可达 base：整腿 scanned 0/2 → 缩水 BLOCKED
  1313	        assert st["coverage"]["scanned"] < st["coverage"]["denominator"] == 2
  1314	        assert st["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", st
  1315	        assert st["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", st  # 不得晋升（非通过）
  1316	        assert st["execution"]["assertion_verdict"] == "ERROR", st
  1317	        validate_station_result(st)
  1318	
  1319	        live = RouteDynamicScanner(
  1320	            manifest,
  1321	            routes=[{"name": "home", "method": "GET", "path": "/",
  1322	                     "expected_status": 200}],
  1323	            base_url=base,
  1324	        ).scan()
  1325	        assert live["policy_verdict"] == "PASS", live  # 对照：可达时不误伤
  1326	    finally:
  1327	        server.shutdown()
  1328	        server.server_close()
  1329	
  1330	    # 统一入口同型注入：scanned<denominator 一律折叠（晋升通道不存在）
  1331	    forced = build_station_result(
  1332	        run_id="ac-perf6", station_id=4, attempt_id="att-perf6",
  1333	        execution_status="COMPLETED", policy_verdict="PASS",
  1334	        identity=dict(ST2_IDENTITY), tool={"name": "ac", "version": "1"},
  1335	        execution={"argv_digest": _argv_digest(["ac", "perf6"]),
  1336	                   "started_at": _iso_z(_utc_now()),
  1337	                   "ended_at": _iso_z(_utc_now()),
  1338	                   "actual_exit_code": 0, "expected_exit_set": [0],
  1339	                   "assertion_verdict": "PASS"},
  1340	        coverage={"denominator": 100, "scanned": 60},  # 样本/时长分母缩水
  1341	    )
  1342	    assert forced["execution_status"] == "BLOCKED", forced
  1343	    assert forced["policy_verdict"] == "NOT_EVALUATED", forced
  1344

  1402	assert len(IMPLEMENTED_IDS) == len(set(IMPLEMENTED_IDS)) == 24, IMPLEMENTED_IDS
  1403	assert not (set(IMPLEMENTED_IDS) & set(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE)), "清单互斥被破坏"

  1405	    "STA-01", "STA-02",

  1411	}, "29 条 ID 集合等式不成立"
  1412	
  1413	
  1414	def main(json_out=None):
  1415	    results = []
  1416	    pass_n = fail_n = 0
  1417	    for name, fn in TESTS:
  1418	        test_id = name.split()[0]
  1419	        t0 = time.time()
  1420	        try:
  1421	            fn()
  1422	            pass_n += 1
  1423	            status = "PASS"
  1424	            print(f"  ok  {name}")
  1425	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001——失败必须留痕并继续跑完
  1426	            fail_n += 1
  1427	            status = "FAIL"
  1428	            print(f"  FAIL {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
  1429	        results.append({"id": test_id, "name": name, "status": status,
  1430	                        "duration_ms": round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 1)})
  1431	
  1432	    print(f"\n§20 六族验收测试: {pass_n} PASS / {fail_n} FAIL "
  1433	          f"（负责 29 条；实现 {len(IMPLEMENTED_IDS)}；"
  1434	          f"不可实现 {len(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE)}）")
  1435	    for tid, reason in sorted(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE.items()):
  1436	        print(f"  [不可实现] {tid}: {reason}")
  1437	
  1438	    if json_out:
  1439	        payload = {
  1440	            "artifact_kind": "ac-station-families/unit-run",
  1441	            "command": "python3 system/tests/test_ac_station_families.py",
  1442	            "exit_code": 1 if fail_n else 0,
  1443	            "totals": {
  1444	                "responsible": 29,
  1445	                "implemented": len(IMPLEMENTED_IDS),
  1446	                "not_implementable": len(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE),
  1447	                "passed": pass_n,
  1448	                "failed": fail_n,
  1449	            },
  1450	            "implemented_ids": IMPLEMENTED_IDS,
  1451	            "not_implementable": NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE,
  1452	            "results": results,
  1453	        }
  1454	        out = Path(json_out)
  1455	        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1456	        out.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2),
  1457	                       encoding="utf-8")
  1458	        print(f"结构化结果: {out}")
  1459	    return 1 if fail_n else 0
  1460	
  1461	
  1462	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1463	    argv = sys.argv[1:]
  1464	    out_path = None
  1465	    if argv and argv[0] == "--json-out":
  1466	        out_path = argv[1] if len(argv) > 1 else None
  1467	    raise SystemExit(main(out_path))

===== system/tests/test_ac_ops_families.py =====
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
     3	"""§20 验收 ID 专属测试——调度/告警/健康/文档治理四族（P0-10 追踪缺口销账件）。
     4	
     5	覆盖 §20.4/§20.5 中本件负责的 14 个验收 ID（注入/期望逐字对齐
     6	evidence/00-baseline/codex-external/方案.md §20 表格）：
     7	
     8	    SCH-01  launchd 无 Documents 权限                -> ERROR+告警
     9	    SCH-02  脚本 FAIL 后 touch heartbeat             -> 测试抓获、last_success 不变
    10	    SCH-03  睡眠错过周期                             -> 唤醒补跑
    11	    SCH-04  catch-up 与正常调度并发                  -> 只有一个 logical run
    12	    SCH-05  scheduler 整体故障                       -> 独立 watchdog 仍告警
    13	    SCH-06  one-shot job                             -> 只写 wenqu namespace
    14	    SCH-07  COMPLETED+NOT_APPLICABLE 或过期/异指纹 PASS -> 不刷新任何实体的 last_success
    15	    ALT-01  provider 500/超时                        -> outbox 重试/dead-letter
    16	    ALT-02  waiting 到期但零 send attempt/receipt    -> ERROR+告警，不能以 watchdog exit0 记健康
    17	    HLT-01  聚合期间输入 watermark 变化              -> snapshot 作废重算
    18	    DOC-01  one-shot 停等数量/枚举漂移               -> CI 阻断
    19	    DOC-02  bug skill 版本/清单/验收数量漂移         -> CI 阻断
    20	    DOC-03  policy/Skill 出现 L4、--admin、force-with-lease 或 skip 绕闸文本 -> CI 阻断
    21	    DOC-04  文档宣称现役/PASS，但 registry 或 Gate 为 BLOCKED -> CI 阻断
    22	
    23	被测体（全部真实注入、真实断言，零 mock 产品行为）：
    24	    system/wenqu_core/scheduler.py   ScheduleRegistry / JobRunner / NotificationOutbox /
    25	                                     HeartbeatMonitor（DST/重试/幂等/退避/死信）
    26	    system/dashboard/server.py       /api/v1/health 健康原子性 + gate 快照输入水印
    27	    system/wenqu_core/wenqu_pipeline.py 九类停等枚举 + system/docs/specs 契约 + docs/
    28	    probes/stations.json + cli/wenqu VERSION + tests/acceptance.sh   文档注册表对账
    29	
    30	运行：python3 system/tests/test_ac_ops_families.py   （独立 exit 0 = 全绿）
    31	证据：evidence/04-unit-property-mutation/ac-ops-families.json（每次运行覆写）。
    32	
    33	诚实边界（无法在本机沙箱真实注入、不以假绿硬凑的部分，另见证据 JSON
    34	not_implementable 字段）：
    35	    1. SCH-01 的「launchd 无 Documents 权限」需要真实 launchd 会话 + TCC 授权弹窗
    36	       环境；本件以等价代码接缝注入（作业无法访问其执行环境 -> spawn 失败）覆盖
    37	       「ERROR+告警」判定链，真实 launchd 环境标注未验证。
    38	    2. SCH-03 的真实系统睡眠/唤醒（pmset/14 天时序）无法在单测内发生；使用
    39	       scheduler 模块自带的 now= 时钟注入接缝（模块文档声明的时间纪律正源）。
    40	    3. SCH-05 的「不同故障域独立 watchdog 宿主」（跨进程/跨机）以同库独立
    41	       HeartbeatMonitor 组件覆盖进程内独立性；跨故障域声明为未验证。
    42	    4. HLT-01 的聚合器侧重算属 gate_aggregator 域（并行工作面，本件不碰）；
    43	       本件覆盖 dashboard 正源侧：watermark 变化 -> 快照作废（503 结构化 ERROR，
    44	       绝不透传旧结论）+ 聚合器重算产物（重写快照）即刻生效。bin/wenqu-dashboard.py
    45	       的 /api/health 为遗留自算卡（无 gate 快照水印面，且会写用户真实走势文件），
    46	       水印契约自 W8 起由 system/dashboard/server.py 承载，本件覆盖正源。
    47	"""
    48	from __future__ import annotations
    49	
    50	import http.client
    51	import json
    52	import logging
    53	import os
    54	import re
    55	import shutil
    56	import socket
    57	import sqlite3
    58	import subprocess
    59	import sys
    60	import tempfile
    61	import threading
    62	import time
    63	from datetime import datetime, timezone

   119	# 通用小工具（不含任何 §20 ID 字面量，避免追踪矩阵误归属）
   120	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121	def _record(test_id: str, detail: str) -> None:
   122	    _RESULTS[test_id] = {"pass": True, "detail": detail}
   123	
   124	
   125	def _mk_job(job_id: str, argv, *, station: int, every: float = 60.0,
   126	            due_window: float = 300.0, catch_up: str = "COALESCE",
   127	            max_catch_up: int = 8, freshness: float = 900.0,
   128	            commit_sha=None, cwd=None) -> JobDefinition:
   129	    return JobDefinition(
   130	        job_id=job_id, owner="ac-ops-families", argv=tuple(argv),
   131	        schedule=ScheduleSpec.every(every), environment="local",
   132	        probe_id=f"probe-{job_id}", station_id=station, cwd=cwd,
   133	        due_window_seconds=due_window, catch_up=catch_up,
   134	        max_catch_up_runs=max_catch_up,
   135	        watchdog=WatchdogPolicy(freshness_seconds=freshness, grace_seconds=0.0),
   136	        ttl_seconds=300.0, commit_sha=commit_sha,
   137	    )
   138	
   139	
   140	def _free_port() -> int:
   141	    s = socket.socket()
   142	    s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
   143	    port = s.getsockname()[1]
   144	    s.close()
   145	    return port
   146	
   147	
   148	def _true_bin() -> str:
   149	    """跨平台定位 true（本机 macOS 无 /bin/true，只有 /usr/bin/true）。"""
   150	    return shutil.which("true") or "/usr/bin/true"
   151	
   152	
   153	def _http_get(port: int, path: str, timeout: float = 5.0):
   154	    conn = http.client.HTTPConnection("127.0.0.1", port, timeout=timeout)
   155	    try:
   156	        conn.request("GET", path)
   157	        resp = conn.getresponse()
   158	        body = resp.read()
   159	    finally:
   160	        conn.close()
   161	    try:
   162	        parsed = json.loads(body)
   163	    except ValueError:
   164	        parsed = None
   165	    return resp.status, parsed
   166	
   167	
   168	def _write_gate_snapshot(path: Path, verdict: str | None, *, age_seconds: float = 0.0,
   169	                         raw: str | None = None) -> str:
   170	    """按 read_gate_aggregate 契约写一份聚合器快照；返回 generated_at。"""
   171	    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc).timestamp() - age_seconds
   172	    gen = datetime.fromtimestamp(ts, timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
   173	    if raw is not None:
   174	        path.write_text(raw, encoding="utf-8")
   175	        return gen
   176	    payload = {
   177	        "generated_at": gen, "policy_verdict": verdict,
   178	        "aggregate_outcome": verdict, "reason": "ac-ops-families probe",
   179	        "counts": {}, "stations": {},
   180	    }
   181	    path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
   182	    return gen
   183	
   184	
   185	class _DashboardServer:
   186	    """在随机空闲端口起 system/dashboard/server.py 子进程，退出时清理。"""
   187	
   188	    def __init__(self, sandbox: Path, ttl_seconds: int = 2):
   189	        self.sandbox = sandbox
   190	        self.snap = sandbox / "gate-aggregate.json"
   191	        self.history = sandbox / "health-history.jsonl"
   192	        self.port = _free_port()
   193	        self.ttl = ttl_seconds
   194	        self.proc: subprocess.Popen | None = None
   195	
   196	    def __enter__(self) -> "_DashboardServer":
   197	        env = dict(os.environ,
   198	                   WENQU_GATE_AGGREGATE=str(self.snap),
   199	                   WENQU_AGGREGATE_TTL=str(self.ttl),
   200	                   WENQU_HOME=str(self.sandbox / "wenqu-home"),
   201	                   WENQU_HEALTH_HISTORY=str(self.history))
   202	        self.proc = subprocess.Popen(
   203	            [sys.executable, str(SYSTEM / "dashboard" / "server.py"),
   204	             "--port", str(self.port)],
   205	            env=env, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   206	        deadline = time.monotonic() + 15.0
   207	        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
   208	            if self.proc.poll() is not None:
   209	                raise AssertionError(
   210	                    f"dashboard server 提前退出 rc={self.proc.returncode}")
   211	            try:
   212	                status, _ = _http_get(self.port, "/api/v1/ping")
   213	                if status == 200:
   214	                    return self
   215	            except OSError:
   216	                pass
   217	            time.sleep(0.15)
   218	        raise AssertionError("dashboard server 15s 内未就绪")
   219	
   220	    def __exit__(self, *exc) -> None:
   221	        if self.proc is None:
   222	            return
   223	        self.proc.terminate()
   224	        try:
   225	            self.proc.wait(timeout=5)
   226	        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
   227	            self.proc.kill()
   228	            self.proc.wait(timeout=5)
   229	
   230	    def health(self):
   231	        return _http_get(self.port, "/api/v1/health")
   232

   237	def test_SCH_01_env_permission_denied_yields_error_and_alert():
   238	    """SCH-01 | 注入: launchd 无 Documents 权限 | 期望: ERROR+告警。
   239	
   240	    真实 launchd+TCC 环境不可注入（见模块 docstring 边界 1），此处注入等价
   241	    故障：作业的执行环境不可访问（argv[0] 不存在；cwd 无读权限），断言产品
   242	    判定链完整落在 ERROR + WARNING 告警投递上，心跳不刷新。
   243	    """
   244	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch01_") as tmp:
   245	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   246	        try:
   247	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   248	            delivered: list[dict] = []
   249	
   250	            def recorder(payload: dict) -> None:
   251	                delivered.append(payload)
   252	
   253	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store, critical_channels=("primary", "fallback"))
   254	            outbox.register_channel("primary", recorder)
   255	            outbox.register_channel("fallback", recorder)
   256	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   257	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   258	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   259	
   260	            # 注入 A：可执行文件不存在（环境残缺——launchd 拿不到 PATH/权限时的等价形态）
   261	            reg.register(_mk_job("sch01-missing", ("/nonexistent/wenqu-probe", "--run"),
   262	                                station=1), now=1000.0)
   263	            rec = runner.run_job("sch01-missing", scheduled_at=1060.0, now=1061.0)
   264	            assert rec.execution_status == "ERROR", \
   265	                f"环境不可达应为 ERROR，实为 {rec.execution_status}"
   266	            assert rec.policy_verdict != "PASS", "环境错误绝不得折算为 PASS"
   267	            assert rec.refreshed_heartbeat is False, "ERROR 不得刷新 last_success"
   268	            assert any("spawn failed" in w for w in rec.warnings), \
   269	                f"应捕获 spawn 失败警告，实得 {rec.warnings}"
   270	
   271	            # 注入 B：cwd 无读权限（Documents 目录被 TCC 拒绝的等价形态；root 下豁免）
   272	            ran_perm = False
   273	            if os.geteuid() != 0:
   274	                locked = os.path.join(tmp, "locked-cwd")
   275	                os.makedirs(locked, exist_ok=True)
   276	                os.chmod(locked, 0o000)
   277	                try:
   278	                    reg.register(_mk_job("sch01-noperm", (_true_bin(),), station=1,
   279	                                        cwd=locked), now=2000.0)
   280	                    rec2 = runner.run_job("sch01-noperm", scheduled_at=2060.0, now=2061.0)
   281	                    ran_perm = True
   282	                    assert rec2.execution_status == "ERROR", \
   283	                        f"cwd 不可读应为 ERROR，实为 {rec2.execution_status}"
   284	                    assert rec2.policy_verdict != "PASS"
   285	                finally:
   286	                    os.chmod(locked, 0o755)
   287	
   288	            # 期望：ERROR+告警——WARNING 通知已入 outbox 并真实投递到通道
   289	            result = outbox.dispatch_due(now=time.time() + 3600.0)
   290	            assert result["sent"] >= 1, f"应有告警真实投递，dispatch={result}"
   291	            subjects = " | ".join(d["subject"] for d in delivered)
   292	            assert "sch01-missing" in subjects, f"告警应点名作业，实得 {subjects}"
   293	            assert delivered, "告警必须真实送出（不只入队）"
   294	            rows = store.query(
   295	                "SELECT last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats WHERE job_id=?",
   296	                ("sch01-missing",))
   297	            assert rows and rows[0][0] is None, "ERROR 后 last_success 必须保持未确立"
   298	            _record("SCH-01",
   299	                    f"spawn 不可达/无权限 cwd -> ERROR+NOT_EVALUATED，WARNING 告警真实投递 "
   300	                    f"({result['sent']} 条)；last_success=None；perm-cwd 注入={'已跑' if ran_perm else 'root 豁免'}")
   301	        finally:
   302	            store.close()
   303	
   304	
   305	def test_SCH_02_fake_touch_heartbeat_after_fail_keeps_last_success():
   306	    """SCH-02 | 注入: 脚本 FAIL 后 touch heartbeat | 期望: 测试抓获、last_success 不变。"""
   307	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch02_") as tmp:
   308	        hbfile = os.path.join(tmp, "heartbeat.file")
   309	        Path(hbfile).write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
   310	        before_mtime = os.stat(hbfile).st_mtime
   311	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   312	        try:
   313	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   314	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store)
   315	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   316	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   317	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   318	            argv = ("/bin/sh", "-c", f"touch '{hbfile}'; sleep 0.05; exit 1")
   319	            reg.register(_mk_job("sch02", argv, station=2), now=0.0)
   320	            rec = runner.run_job("sch02", scheduled_at=60.0, now=61.0)
   321	
   322	            # 注入生效证明：脚本确实 touch 了 heartbeat 文件
   323	            assert os.stat(hbfile).st_mtime > before_mtime, \
   324	                "注入失败：脚本未 touch heartbeat 文件"
   325	            # 期望：产品以业务结果为准——FAIL 不刷新，touch 不被采信
   326	            assert (rec.execution_status, rec.policy_verdict) == ("COMPLETED", "FAIL")
   327	            assert rec.refreshed_heartbeat is False
   328	            row = store.query(
   329	                "SELECT last_success_at, last_policy_verdict, consecutive_failures "
   330	                "FROM scheduler_heartbeats WHERE job_id='sch02'")
   331	            assert row and row[0][0] is None, \
   332	                f"FAIL+touch 后 last_success 必须不变（None），实为 {row[0][0]}"
   333	            assert row[0][1] == "FAIL" and row[0][2] == 1
   334	            # 看门狗视角：进程活（结果持续产出）但功能死——CRITICAL，不被 touch 骗
   335	            reports = {r.job_id: r for r in hb.check_health(now=62.0)}
   336	            r = reports["sch02"]
   337	            assert (r.state, r.healthy, r.severity) == ("FUNCTION_DEAD", False, "CRITICAL"), \
   338	                f"假心跳必须被抓为 FUNCTION_DEAD/CRITICAL，实为 {r.state}/{r.severity}"
   339	            _record("SCH-02",
   340	                    "脚本 exit 1 且 touch heartbeat：mtime 前进被实证，last_success 保持 None，"
   341	                    "FUNCTION_DEAD/CRITICAL 告警照发——touch 假心跳零效果")
   342	        finally:
   343	            store.close()
   344	
   345	
   346	def test_SCH_03_sleep_missed_period_wakes_up_and_catches_up():
   347	    """SCH-03 | 注入: 睡眠错过周期 | 期望: 唤醒补跑。"""
   348	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch03_") as tmp:
   349	        ran = os.path.join(tmp, "ran.log")
   350	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   351	        try:
   352	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   353	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store)
   354	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   355	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   356	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   357	
   358	            # COALESCE：注册后"睡眠"95s（错过 9 个 10s 周期），唤醒在 t=1095
   359	            reg.register(_mk_job(
   360	                "sch03-coalesce",
   361	                ("/bin/sh", "-c", f"echo run >> '{ran}'"),
   362	                station=3, every=10.0, due_window=60.0, catch_up="COALESCE"),
   363	                now=1000.0)
   364	            occ = [o for o in reg.due_jobs(now=1095.0) if o.job_id == "sch03-coalesce"]
   365	            runs = [o for o in occ if o.run]
   366	            skips = [o for o in occ if not o.run]
   367	            assert len(runs) == 1 and runs[0].reason == "coalesced", \
   368	                f"唤醒后应恰补跑最新一次（coalesced），实得 {[(o.scheduled_at, o.run, o.reason) for o in occ]}"
   369	            assert runs[0].scheduled_at == 1090.0, "补跑目标必须是最新到期出现"
   370	            assert runs[0].missed_count >= 8, f"应累计错过次数，实得 {runs[0].missed_count}"
   371	            assert skips, "更早出现必须显式记跳过（coalesced/stale），不得静默消失"
   372	            # 消费：补跑真执行 + 跳过项落审计 -> 该作业补跑收口，无二次补跑
   373	            rec = runner.run_job("sch03-coalesce", scheduled_at=runs[0].scheduled_at,
   374	                                 now=1095.0)
   375	            for o in skips:
   376	                reg.mark_skipped(o.job_id, o.scheduled_at, o.reason, now=1095.0)
   377	            assert (rec.execution_status, rec.policy_verdict) == ("COMPLETED", "PASS")
   378	            assert Path(ran).read_text().count("run") == 1, "补跑恰执行一次"
   379	            assert reg.due_jobs(now=1095.0) == [], "消费后同一时刻不得再次补跑"
   380	            state = reg.job_state("sch03-coalesce")
   381	            assert state["next_due_at"] > 1095.0, "next_due 只前进"
   382	
   383	            # ALL：显式回填最近 3 次（max_catch_up_runs=3），更早 overrun 记审计
   384	            reg.register(_mk_job(
   385	                "sch03-all", (_true_bin(),), station=3, every=10.0,
   386	                due_window=0.0, catch_up="ALL", max_catch_up=3),
   387	                now=2000.0)
   388	            occ2 = [o for o in reg.due_jobs(now=2095.0) if o.job_id == "sch03-all"]
   389	            runs2 = [o for o in occ2 if o.run]
   390	            overruns = [o for o in occ2 if o.reason == "overrun"]
   391	            assert len(runs2) == 3 and all(o.reason == "catch_up_all" for o in runs2), \
   392	                f"ALL 应补跑最近 3 次，实得 {[(o.scheduled_at, o.run, o.reason) for o in occ2]}"
   393	            assert runs2 == sorted(runs2, key=lambda o: o.scheduled_at), "补跑按时间正序"
   394	            assert overruns, "超出 max_catch_up_runs 的出现必须 overrun 落审计"
   395	            _record("SCH-03",
   396	                    f"错过 9 周期唤醒：COALESCE 恰补最新 1 次（missed={runs[0].missed_count}），"
   397	                    f"跳过全落审计、消费后零重复；ALL 回填恰 3 次+overrun 审计")
   398	        finally:
   399	            store.close()
   400	
   401	
   402	def test_SCH_04_catchup_and_normal_race_single_logical_run():
   403	    """SCH-04 | 注入: catch-up 与正常调度并发 | 期望: 只有一个 logical run。
   404	
   405	    用两个独立调度拥有者（各自持有同一运行态库的独立连接——补跑 worker 与
   406	    正常 scheduler 的真实形态）对同一 (job_id, scheduled_at) 并发 run_job。
   407	    """
   408	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch04_") as tmp:
   409	        db = os.path.join(tmp, "s.db")
   410	        side = os.path.join(tmp, "side.log")
   411	        boot = SchedulerStore(db)
   412	        ScheduleRegistry(boot).register(_mk_job(
   413	            "sch04", ("/bin/sh", "-c", "echo x >> " + side), station=4), now=0.0)
   414	        boot.close()
   415	
   416	        def owner() -> object:
   417	            st = SchedulerStore(db)
   418	            try:
   419	                rec, last_err = None, None
   420	                for _ in range(10):
   421	                    try:
   422	                        rec = JobRunner(
   423	                            st, TrustedRunner(), registry=ScheduleRegistry(st),
   424	                            outbox=NotificationOutbox(st),
   425	                            heartbeat=HeartbeatMonitor(st, NotificationOutbox(st)),
   426	                        ).run_job("sch04", scheduled_at=60.0, now=61.0)
   427	                        break
   428	                    except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:  # BEGIN IMMEDIATE 撞锁重试
   429	                        last_err = exc
   430	                        time.sleep(0.05)
   431	                if rec is None:
   432	                    raise AssertionError(f"并发 run_job 未成功: {last_err}")
   433	                return rec
   434	            finally:
   435	                st.close()
   436	
   437	        results: list = [None, None]
   438	        barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
   439	
   440	        def worker(i: int) -> None:
   441	            barrier.wait()
   442	            results[i] = owner()
   443	
   444	        threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker, args=(i,)) for i in range(2)]
   445	        for t in threads:
   446	            t.start()
   447	        for t in threads:
   448	            t.join()
   449	        assert all(r is not None for r in results), "两个拥有者都应拿到返回记录"
   450	
   451	        st = SchedulerStore(db)
   452	        try:
   453	            rows = st.query(
   454	                "SELECT run_id FROM scheduler_runs WHERE job_id='sch04' AND scheduled_at=60.0")
   455	            assert len(rows) == 1, \
   456	                f"同一逻辑出现必须只有一个 logical run，实得 {len(rows)} 行: {rows}"
   457	            run_ids = {r.run_id for r in results}
   458	            assert run_ids == {rows[0][0]}, \
   459	                f"两个拥有者必须归属同一 run_id，实得 {run_ids} vs {rows[0][0]}"
   460	            # 顺序重放：完成后的重复消费直接命中幂等键，不再执行
   461	            lines_before = Path(side).read_text().count("x") if os.path.exists(side) else 0
   462	            st2 = SchedulerStore(db)
   463	            replay = JobRunner(st2, TrustedRunner(), registry=ScheduleRegistry(st2),
   464	                               outbox=NotificationOutbox(st2),
   465	                               heartbeat=HeartbeatMonitor(st2, NotificationOutbox(st2))
   466	                               ).run_job("sch04", scheduled_at=60.0, now=70.0)
   467	            st2.close()
   468	            assert replay.replayed is True, "重放必须标记 replayed=True"
   469	            lines_after = Path(side).read_text().count("x")
   470	            assert lines_after == lines_before, "重放不得再次执行副作用"
   471	            note = (f"并发双拥有者：runs 表恰 1 行、run_id 归一；顺序重放 replayed=True 零副作用。"
   472	                    f"边界（如实记录）：物理子进程执行了 {lines_before} 次——模块契约以 "
   473	                    f"(job_id, scheduled_at) 唯一键保证账本级幂等，事务前预检为咨询性")
   474	            _record("SCH-04", note)
   475	        finally:
   476	            st.close()
   477	
   478	
   479	def test_SCH_05_scheduler_down_independent_watchdog_still_alerts():
   480	    """SCH-05 | 注入: scheduler 整体故障 | 期望: 独立 watchdog 仍告警。"""
   481	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch05_") as tmp:
   482	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   483	        try:
   484	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   485	            delivered: list[dict] = []
   486	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store, critical_channels=("primary", "fallback"))
   487	
   488	            def recorder(payload: dict) -> None:
   489	                delivered.append(payload)
   490	
   491	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   492	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   493	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   494	            reg.register(_mk_job("sch05", (_true_bin(),), station=5,
   495	                                 freshness=900.0), now=1000.0)
   496	            ok = runner.run_job("sch05", scheduled_at=1060.0, now=1060.0)
   497	            assert (ok.execution_status, ok.policy_verdict) == ("COMPLETED", "PASS")
   498	            assert hb.check_health(now=1061.0)[0].state == "FRESH"
   499	
   500	            # 注入：scheduler 整体故障——此后不再有任何 run/推进（进程视为已死），
   501	            # 独立 watchdog 只读持久化运行态做判定。
   502	            outbox.register_channel("primary", recorder)
   503	            outbox.register_channel("fallback", recorder)
   504	            reports = hb.check_health(now=10660.0, notify=True)
   505	            r = {x.job_id: x for x in reports}["sch05"]
   506	            assert r.healthy is False and r.severity == "CRITICAL", \
   507	                f"调度器死亡 9000s 后 watchdog 必须 CRITICAL，实为 {r.state}/{r.severity}"
   508	            assert r.state in ("STALE_NO_RESULT", "FUNCTION_DEAD", "STALE_NO_SUCCESS")
   509	            result = outbox.dispatch_due(now=10670.0)
   510	            assert result["sent"] >= 1, f"watchdog 告警必须真实投递，dispatch={result}"
   511	            assert any("心跳不健康" in d["subject"] and "sch05" in d["subject"]
   512	                       for d in delivered), f"告警应点名作业与状态，实得 {[d['subject'] for d in delivered]}"
   513	            # scheduler 故障期间无任何新 run 产生（运行账本静止）
   514	            n_runs = store.query(
   515	                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scheduler_runs WHERE job_id='sch05'")[0][0]
   516	            assert n_runs == 1, "watchdog 判定不得伪造新 run"
   517	            _record("SCH-05",
   518	                    f"调度器停摆后独立 HeartbeatMonitor 仍从持久化运行态判出 "
   519	                    f"{r.state}/CRITICAL 并真实投递告警；runs 账本静止（1 行）")
   520	        finally:
   521	            store.close()
   522

   524	def test_SCH_06_one_shot_job_writes_only_wenqu_namespace():
   525	    """SCH-06 | 注入: one-shot job | 期望: 只写 wenqu namespace。"""
   526	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch06_") as tmp:
   527	        root = Path(tmp)
   528	        fake_home = root / "home"          # 模拟用户命名空间（不得被写入）
   529	        job_cwd = root / "workdir"          # 作业自己的工作区（允许写）
   530	        fake_home.mkdir(); job_cwd.mkdir()
   531	        db = root / "state" / "scheduler.db"
   532	        db.parent.mkdir()
   533	
   534	        def snapshot_fs() -> set:
   535	            out = set()
   536	            for p in root.rglob("*"):
   537	                if not p.is_file():        # 目录 mtime 噪声不参与命名空间判定
   538	                    continue
   539	                st = p.stat()
   540	                out.add((str(p.relative_to(root)), st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_size))
   541	            return out
   542	
   543	        before = snapshot_fs()
   544	        store = SchedulerStore(str(db))
   545	        try:
   546	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   547	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store)
   548	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   549	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   550	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   551	            reg.register(_mk_job(
   552	                "sch06", ("/bin/sh", "-c", "echo out > ./out.txt; exit 0"),
   553	                station=6, cwd=str(job_cwd)), now=0.0)
   554	            state_before = reg.job_state("sch06")["next_due_at"]
   555	            # one-shot：手工单次执行（scheduled_at=None，不参与周期幂等键）
   556	            rec = runner.run_job("sch06", now=5.0)
   557	            assert rec.scheduled_at is None and "manual" in rec.run_id, \
   558	                f"one-shot 运行必须是 manual 形态，实为 {rec.run_id}"
   559	            assert (rec.execution_status, rec.policy_verdict) == ("COMPLETED", "PASS")
   560	
   561	            # 期望 1：调度器状态库内只有 wenqu scheduler 命名空间的对象
   562	            tables = [r[0] for r in store.query(
   563	                "SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' "
   564	                "AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'")]
   565	            assert tables and all(t.startswith("scheduler_") for t in tables), \
   566	                f"状态库必须只含 scheduler_* 命名空间，实得 {tables}"
   567	            # 期望 2：one-shot 不推进周期游标（不劫持正排调度）
   568	            assert reg.job_state("sch06")["next_due_at"] == state_before, \
   569	                "one-shot 不得推进 next_due_at"
   570	            # 期望 3：文件系统面——新增/变更只允许出现在 作业工作区 与 调度器自有 DB
   571	            allowed_prefixes = ("workdir/", "state/")
   572	            violations = [e for e in snapshot_fs() - before
   573	                         if not e[0].startswith(allowed_prefixes)]
   574	            assert not violations, f"命名空间外出现写入: {violations}"
   575	            home_touched = any(e[0].startswith("home/") for e in snapshot_fs() - before)
   576	            assert not home_touched, "用户命名空间（home）被写入"
   577	            assert (job_cwd / "out.txt").exists(), "作业工作区产物应在"
   578	            _record("SCH-06",
   579	                    f"one-shot manual 运行：状态库仅 {len(tables)} 张 scheduler_* 表；"
   580	                    f"next_due 不变；文件系统新增仅限 workdir/ 与 state/（home 零写入）")
   581	        finally:
   582	            store.close()
   583	
   584	
   585	def test_SCH_07_non_pass_stale_or_foreign_fingerprint_never_refreshes():
   586	    """SCH-07 | 注入: COMPLETED+NOT_APPLICABLE 或过期/异指纹 PASS | 期望: 不刷新任何实体的 last_success。"""
   587	    fp_a = compute_input_fingerprint(argv_digest="a" * 64, commit_sha="1" * 40)
   588	    fp_b = compute_input_fingerprint(argv_digest="b" * 64, commit_sha="2" * 40)
   589	    assert fp_a != fp_b
   590	
   591	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_sch07_") as tmp:
   592	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   593	        try:
   594	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store)
   595	            kw = dict(job_id="sch07", probe_id="p7", station_id=7)
   596	
   597	            # 注入 1：COMPLETED+NOT_APPLICABLE——只声明"不适用"，不证明健康
   598	            na = hb.record_result(input_fingerprint=fp_a, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   599	                                  policy_verdict="NOT_APPLICABLE", finished_at=100.0,
   600	                                  ttl_seconds=300.0, now=100.0, **kw)
   601	            assert na is False, "NOT_APPLICABLE 不得刷新成功时间"
   602	            row = store.query("SELECT last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats")[0]
   603	            assert row[0] is None, f"NA 后 last_success 应为 None，实为 {row[0]}"
   604	
   605	            # 注入 2：过期 PASS（finished_at 距 now 超 TTL）
   606	            stale = hb.record_result(input_fingerprint=fp_a, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   607	                                     policy_verdict="PASS", finished_at=200.0,
   608	                                     ttl_seconds=300.0, now=5000.0, **kw)
   609	            assert stale is False, "过期 PASS 不得刷新成功时间"
   610	            assert store.query(
   611	                "SELECT last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats")[0][0] is None
   612	
   613	            # 基线：同指纹新鲜 PASS 确立成功（证明后续"不刷新"不是从未能刷新）
   614	            ok = hb.record_result(input_fingerprint=fp_a, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   615	                                  policy_verdict="PASS", finished_at=5000.0,
   616	                                  ttl_seconds=300.0, now=5001.0, **kw)
   617	            assert ok is True
   618	            assert store.query(
   619	                "SELECT last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats")[0][0] == 5001.0
   620	
   621	            # 注入 3：异指纹过期 PASS——旧成功对新指纹无效，不得随指纹带过去
   622	            foreign = hb.record_result(input_fingerprint=fp_b, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   623	                                       policy_verdict="PASS", finished_at=4700.0,
   624	                                       ttl_seconds=300.0, now=5002.0, **kw)
   625	            assert foreign is False, "异指纹过期 PASS 不得刷新"
   626	            row = store.query(
   627	                "SELECT input_fingerprint, last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats")[0]
   628	            assert row == (fp_b, None), \
   629	                f"指纹切换后旧成功必须作废重置，实为 {row}"
   630	
   631	            # 反向自证：新指纹上的新鲜 PASS 才重新确立成功
   632	            ok2 = hb.record_result(input_fingerprint=fp_b, execution_status="COMPLETED",
   633	                                   policy_verdict="PASS", finished_at=5003.0,
   634	                                   ttl_seconds=300.0, now=5003.0, **kw)
   635	            assert ok2 is True
   636	
   637	            # 集成面：真实 run_job 走同一条铁律（commit_sha 变化 -> 新指纹）
   638	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   639	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   640	                               outbox=NotificationOutbox(store),
   641	                               heartbeat=HeartbeatMonitor(store))
   642	            reg.register(_mk_job("sch07i", (_true_bin(),), station=6,
   643	                                 commit_sha="a" * 40), now=0.0)
   644	            p1 = runner.run_job("sch07i", scheduled_at=60.0, now=60.0)
   645	            assert p1.refreshed_heartbeat is True
   646	            reg.register(_mk_job("sch07i", ("/bin/sh", "-c", "exit 1"), station=6,
   647	                                 commit_sha="b" * 40), replace=True, now=70.0)
   648	            p2 = runner.run_job("sch07i", scheduled_at=120.0, now=120.0)
   649	            assert (p2.execution_status, p2.policy_verdict) == ("COMPLETED", "FAIL")
   650	            assert p2.refreshed_heartbeat is False
   651	            row = store.query(
   652	                "SELECT last_success_at FROM scheduler_heartbeats WHERE job_id='sch07i'")[0]
   653	            assert row[0] is None, "异指纹 FAIL 后旧 PASS 成功时间必须清零（不变量 #3）"
   654	            _record("SCH-07",
   655	                    "NA/过期 PASS/异指纹过期 PASS 三注入均不刷新；异指纹切换重置旧成功；"
   656	                    "run_job 集成：commit_sha 变更后 FAIL 将 last_success 清零")
   657	        finally:
   658	            store.close()
   659
