   661	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   662	# ALT 族：通知 outbox
   663	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   664	def test_ALT_01_provider_500_and_timeout_retry_then_dead_letter():
   665	    """ALT-01 | 注入: provider 500/超时 | 期望: outbox 重试/dead-letter。"""
   666	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_alt01_") as tmp:
   667	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   668	        try:
   669	            def provider_500(payload: dict) -> None:
   670	                raise RuntimeError("HTTP 500 from provider")
   671	
   672	            def provider_timeout(payload: dict) -> None:
   673	                raise TimeoutError("provider connect timeout")
   674	
   675	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store, critical_channels=("primary", "fallback"),
   676	                                        backoff_base_seconds=2.0,
   677	                                        backoff_cap_seconds=8.0, max_attempts=3)
   678	            outbox.register_channel("primary", provider_500)
   679	            outbox.register_channel("fallback", provider_timeout)
   680	            mid = outbox.notify("WARNING", "job failed", "body",
   681	                                channels=("primary",), now=100.0)[0]
   682	            assert mid, "消息必须成功入队（waiting）"
   683	
   684	            r1 = outbox.dispatch_due(now=100.0)
   685	            assert r1["failed"] == 1 and "500" in "".join(r1["errors"]), \
   686	                f"首次投递失败必须 surfaced 为 ERROR，实得 {r1}"
   687	            attempts, next_at, status = store.query(
   688	                "SELECT attempts, next_attempt_at, status FROM scheduler_outbox "
   689	                "WHERE message_id=?", (mid,))[0]
   690	            assert (attempts, status) == (1, "PENDING")
   691	            assert next_at == 102.0, f"指数退避 base*2^0=2s，实得 next={next_at}"
   692	
   693	            # 退避窗口内重试不得发生
   694	            r_early = outbox.dispatch_due(now=101.0)
   695	            assert r_early == {"sent": 0, "failed": 0, "dead_lettered": 0, "errors": []}, \
   696	                f"退避窗口内不得重试，实得 {r_early}"
   697	
   698	            row_max = store.query(
   699	                "SELECT max_attempts FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE message_id=?",
   700	                (mid,))[0][0]
   701	            t = 102.0
   702	            for _ in range(12):
   703	                res = outbox.dispatch_due(now=t)
   704	                stats = outbox.stats()
   705	                if stats.get("DEAD_LETTER"):
   706	                    break
   707	                t += 4.0
   708	            stats = outbox.stats()
   709	            assert stats.get("DEAD_LETTER") == 1, f"达到 max_attempts 后必须死信，实得 {stats}"
   710	            row = store.query(
   711	                "SELECT attempts, status, last_error, dead_lettered_at FROM scheduler_outbox "
   712	                "WHERE message_id=?", (mid,))[0]
   713	            assert row[0] == row_max and row[1] == "DEAD_LETTER", \
   714	                f"死信时 attempts 必须达到行内 max_attempts={row_max}，实得 attempts={row[0]} status={row[1]}"
   715	            assert "500" in (row[2] or ""), "死信必须留最后错误回执"
   716	            dl = outbox.dead_letters()
   717	            assert dl and dl[0]["message_id"] == mid
   718	
   719	            # 死信自愈：CRITICAL 双通道「管线断裂」系统告警自动入队
   720	            notices = store.query(
   721	                "SELECT channel, severity FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE meta_json "
   722	                "LIKE '%system_dead_letter_notice%'")
   723	            assert {(c, s) for c, s in notices} == {("primary", "CRITICAL"),
   724	                                                    ("fallback", "CRITICAL")}, \
   725	                f"死信必须触发 CRITICAL 双通道告警，实得 {notices}"
   726	
   727	            # 通道恢复后系统告警真实送出（receipt）
   728	            got: list[dict] = []
   729	            outbox.register_channel("primary", lambda p: got.append(p))
   730	            outbox.register_channel("fallback", lambda p: got.append(p))
   731	            r_ok = outbox.dispatch_due(now=t + 3600.0)
   732	            assert r_ok["sent"] == 2, f"恢复后两条通道都应送达，实得 {r_ok}"
   733	            sent_rows = store.query(
   734	                "SELECT sent_at FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE meta_json "
   735	                "LIKE '%system_dead_letter_notice%'")
   736	            assert all(x[0] is not None for x in sent_rows), "SENT 必须落 sent_at 回执"
   737	
   738	            # 环保护：双通道全灭时死信链有界（原消息+系统告警，不无限递归）
   739	            store2 = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s2.db"))
   740	            try:
   741	                calls = {"n": 0}
   742	
   743	                def always_down(payload: dict) -> None:
   744	                    calls["n"] += 1
   745	                    raise RuntimeError("pipeline down")
   746	
   747	                ob2 = NotificationOutbox(store2, critical_channels=("primary", "fallback"),
   748	                                         backoff_base_seconds=1.0,
   749	                                         backoff_cap_seconds=2.0, max_attempts=2)
   750	                ob2.register_channel("primary", always_down)
   751	                ob2.register_channel("fallback", always_down)
   752	                ob2.notify("CRITICAL", "x", "y", now=0.0)
   753	                t2 = 0.0
   754	                for _ in range(60):
   755	                    ob2.dispatch_due(now=t2)
   756	                    if not ob2.stats().get("PENDING"):
   757	                        break
   758	                    t2 += 1.0
   759	                final = ob2.stats()
   760	                total_rows = len(store2.query("SELECT message_id FROM scheduler_outbox"))
   761	                assert final.get("PENDING", 0) == 0, f"应收敛到无 PENDING，实得 {final}"
   762	                assert final.get("DEAD_LETTER") == 6, \
   763	                    f"2 原始 + 4 系统告警 = 6 死信封顶（递归有环保护），实得 {final}"
   764	                ob2.dispatch_due(now=t2 + 100000.0)
   765	                assert len(store2.query(
   766	                    "SELECT message_id FROM scheduler_outbox")) == total_rows, \
   767	                    "收敛后不得再产生新消息（递归终止）"
   768	            finally:
   769	                store2.close()
   770	            _record("ALT-01",
   771	                    f"500/超时->ERROR+指数退避（窗口内零重试）->{row_max} 次后死信留痕，"
   772	                    f"CRITICAL 双通道管线告警入队并在恢复后真实送达；全灭时死信链 "
   773	                    f"6 条封顶收敛（环保护）")
   774	        finally:
   775	            store.close()
   776	
   777	
   778	def test_ALT_02_waiting_due_zero_attempt_is_error_not_watchdog_health():
   779	    """ALT-02 | 注入: waiting 到期但零 send attempt/receipt | 期望: ERROR+告警，不能以 watchdog exit0 记健康。"""
   780	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_alt02_") as tmp:
   781	        store = SchedulerStore(os.path.join(tmp, "s.db"))
   782	        try:
   783	            reg = ScheduleRegistry(store)
   784	            delivered: list[dict] = []
   785	
   786	            def recorder(payload: dict) -> None:
   787	                delivered.append(payload)
   788	
   789	            outbox = NotificationOutbox(store, critical_channels=("primary", "fallback"))
   790	            hb = HeartbeatMonitor(store, outbox)
   791	            runner = JobRunner(store, TrustedRunner(), registry=reg,
   792	                               outbox=outbox, heartbeat=hb)
   793	            reg.register(_mk_job("alt02", ("/bin/sh", "-c", "exit 1"),
   794	                                 station=5, freshness=900.0), now=0.0)
   795	            runner.run_job("alt02", scheduled_at=60.0, now=61.0)
   796	
   797	            # 注入：waiting 到期但 dispatcher 从未运行（零 attempt、零 receipt）
   798	            waiting = store.query(
   799	                "SELECT severity, status, attempts, sent_at, next_attempt_at "
   800	                "FROM scheduler_outbox")
   801	            assert waiting and all(w[1] == "PENDING" and w[2] == 0 and w[3] is None
   802	                                   for w in waiting), \
   803	                f"waiting 消息必须零 attempt/零 receipt，实得 {waiting}"
   804	            assert all(w[0] == "CRITICAL" for w in waiting), "FAIL 作业告警必须是 CRITICAL"
   805	            zero_attempt_due = store.query(
   806	                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE status='PENDING' "
   807	                "AND attempts=0 AND next_attempt_at<=61.0")[0][0]
   808	            assert zero_attempt_due >= 2, "到期零 attempt 状态必须可被机器检出（>=2 条双通道）"
   809	
   810	            # 期望：watchdog 自身跑完（exit0 等价）绝不制造 attempt/receipt/SENT
   811	            reports = hb.check_health(now=62.0, notify=True)   # 无异常 = 干净返回
   812	            assert reports, "watchdog 正常运行"
   813	            stats_after_watchdog = outbox.stats()
   814	            assert stats_after_watchdog.get("SENT", 0) == 0, \
   815	                f"watchdog exit0 不得记任何送达，实得 {stats_after_watchdog}"
   816	            assert store.query(
   817	                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE sent_at IS NOT NULL")[0][0] == 0, \
   818	                "不得伪造 sent_at 回执"
   819	            assert {r.state: r.severity for r in reports}.get("FUNCTION_DEAD") == "CRITICAL", \
   820	                "健康事实仍必须来自业务结果（FUNCTION_DEAD/CRITICAL）"
   821	
   822	            # ERROR 面 surfaced：dispatch 时通道未注册 -> 结构化 ERROR + attempt 留痕
   823	            res = outbox.dispatch_due(now=70.0)
   824	            assert res["failed"] >= 1 and res["errors"], \
   825	                f"投递障碍必须 surfaced 为 ERROR 列表，实得 {res}"
   826	            assert store.query(
   827	                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE last_error IS NOT NULL "
   828	                "AND attempts>=1")[0][0] >= 1, "失败 attempt 必须留 last_error 回执"
   829	
   830	            # 告警送达闭环：注册真实通道后 receipt 才成立，零 attempt 违例归零
   831	            outbox.register_channel("primary", recorder)
   832	            outbox.register_channel("fallback", recorder)
   833	            done = outbox.dispatch_due(now=time.time() + 3600.0)
   834	            assert done["sent"] >= 1, f"通道恢复后 waiting 告警必须真实送达，实得 {done}"
   835	            assert any("alt02" in d["subject"] for d in delivered)
   836	            left = store.query(
   837	                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM scheduler_outbox WHERE status='PENDING' "
   838	                "AND attempts=0 AND next_attempt_at<=?", (time.time() + 3600.0,))[0][0]
   839	            assert left == 0, "dispatch 后不得残留零 attempt 的到期 waiting"
   840	            _record("ALT-02",
   841	                    "waiting 到期零 attempt/零 receipt 可机器检出；watchdog 干净跑完不产生 "
   842	                    "SENT/sent_at（健康不凭 exit0）；dispatch 将障碍 surfaced 为 ERROR+"
   843	                    "last_error 回执；通道恢复后告警真实送达、违例归零")
   844	        finally:
   845	            store.close()
   846	
   847	
   848	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   849	# HLT 族：健康聚合 watermark
   850	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   851	def test_HLT_01_input_watermark_change_invalidates_snapshot():
   852	    """HLT-01 | 注入: 聚合期间输入 watermark 变化 | 期望: snapshot 作废重算。"""
   853	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_hlt01_") as tmp:
   854	        sandbox = Path(tmp)
   855	        snap = sandbox / "gate-aggregate.json"
   856	        ttl = 2
   857	        written: set = set()
   858	
   859	        with _DashboardServer(sandbox, ttl_seconds=ttl) as srv:
   860	            # 基线：新鲜快照 PASS -> 200 透传判定（dashboard 不自算，不变量 10）
   861	            gen1 = _write_gate_snapshot(snap, "PASS")
   862	            written.add(("PASS", gen1))
   863	            status, body = srv.health()
   864	            assert status == 200 and body["policy_verdict"] == "PASS" and \
   865	                body["source"] == "gate-aggregator", f"新鲜快照应 200 透传，实得 {status} {body}"
   866	
   867	            # 注入 1：watermark 变旧（聚合器停摆/时钟回拨）-> 快照作废，绝不透传旧结论
   868	            time.sleep(ttl + 1.5)
   869	            status, body = srv.health()
   870	            assert status == 503 and body["error"]["code"] == "AGGREGATOR_STALE", \
   871	                f"过期 watermark 必须 503 作废，实得 {status} {body}"
   872	
   873	            # 注入 2：聚合期间半写快照（watermark 字段残缺/JSON 断裂）-> 原子性拒读
   874	            _write_gate_snapshot(snap, None, raw="{ this is not json")
   875	            status, body = srv.health()
   876	            assert status == 503 and body["error"]["code"] == "AGGREGATOR_MALFORMED", \
   877	                f"残缺快照必须 503 MALFORMED（绝不能 200 空集/半绿），实得 {status} {body}"
   878	            _write_gate_snapshot(snap, None, raw=json.dumps({"policy_verdict": "PASS"}))
   879	            status, body = srv.health()
   880	            assert status == 503 and body["error"]["code"] == "AGGREGATOR_MALFORMED", \
   881	                f"缺 watermark 字段同样 MALFORMED，实得 {status} {body}"
   882	
   883	            # 期望：重算——聚合器产出新快照后新判定即刻生效，旧 PASS 不缓存
   884	            gen2 = _write_gate_snapshot(snap, "BLOCKED")
   885	            written.add(("BLOCKED", gen2))
   886	            status, body = srv.health()
   887	            assert status == 200 and body["policy_verdict"] == "BLOCKED" and \
   888	                body["generated_at"] == gen2, \
   889	                f"重算产物必须即刻生效并携带新 watermark，实得 {status} {body}"
   890	
   891	            # 注入 3：聚合进行中高频换 watermark——任意时刻读到的要么是某次完整
   892	            # 快照的（verdict, watermark）对，要么是结构化 503；绝不出现杂交/残缺 200
   893	            stop = threading.Event()
   894	
   895	            def aggregator_sim() -> None:
   896	                flip = True
   897	                while not stop.is_set():
   898	                    verdict = "PASS" if flip else "BLOCKED"
   899	                    flip = not flip
   900	                    g = _write_gate_snapshot(snap, verdict)
   901	                    written.add((verdict, g))
   902	                    time.sleep(0.01)
   903	
   904	            writer = threading.Thread(target=aggregator_sim, daemon=True)
   905	            writer.start()
   906	            try:
   907	                observed = set()
   908	                for _ in range(40):
   909	                    s, b = srv.health()
   910	                    if s == 200:
   911	                        pair = (b["policy_verdict"], b["generated_at"])
   912	                        assert pair in written, \
   913	                            f"读到了从未写入的杂交快照: {pair}"
   914	                        assert b["policy_verdict"] in ("PASS", "BLOCKED", "CONDITIONAL")
   915	                        observed.add(pair)
   916	                    else:
   917	                        assert s == 503 and isinstance(b.get("error"), dict) and \
   918	                            b["error"].get("code") in ("AGGREGATOR_STALE",
   919	                                                       "AGGREGATOR_MALFORMED",
   920	                                                       "AGGREGATOR_UNAVAILABLE"), \
   921	                            f"非 200 必须是结构化 503，实得 {s} {b}"
   922	                assert observed, "高频换 watermark 期间应能读到若干完整快照"
   923	            finally:
   924	                stop.set()
   925	                writer.join(timeout=5)
   926	            _record("HLT-01",
   927	                    f"watermark 过期->503 STALE 作废；半写/缺字段->503 MALFORMED；"
   928	                    f"重算新快照（BLOCKED+新 watermark）即刻生效；高频换 watermark 40 连读"
   929	                    f"全部命中完整 (verdict, watermark) 对或结构化 503（观测 {len(observed)} 对）")
   930	        # 子进程已清理（_DashboardServer.__exit__ terminate+wait）
   931	        assert not srv.proc or srv.proc.poll() is not None, "服务子进程必须被清理"
   932

   935	# DOC 族：文档/注册表一致性（红探针本体——CI 阻断判定函数 + 真实文件对账）

   947	    (r"force-with-lease", "force-with-lease 绕闸推送"),
   948	    (r"--skip-(audit|gate|checks?)", "skip 绕闸旗标"),
   949	    (r"skip[ \t-]*(绕闸|过闸|gate)", "skip 绕闸文本"),

   961	def _parse_stop_types_from_contract(text: str) -> list:
   962	    m6 = re.search(r"^## 6\. 九类停等\s*$", text, re.M)
   963	    m7 = re.search(r"^## 7\.", text, re.M)
   964	    if not m6 or not m7:
   965	        return []
   966	    section = text[m6.start():m7.start()]
   967	    rows = re.findall(r"^\|\s*(\d+)\s*\|\s*([a-z-]+)\s*\|", section, re.M)
   968	    return [name for _, name in rows]
   969	
   970	
   971	def _doc_enum_consistency(doc_enum: list, code_enum: list) -> dict:
   972	    """CI 阻断判定：one-shot 停等数量/枚举漂移即 block。"""
   973	    problems = []
   974	    if len(doc_enum) != len(code_enum):
   975	        problems.append(f"数量漂移: 契约 {len(doc_enum)} 类 vs 实现 {len(code_enum)} 类")
   976	    if doc_enum != code_enum:
   977	        only_doc = [t for t in doc_enum if t not in code_enum]
   978	        only_code = [t for t in code_enum if t not in doc_enum]
   979	        if only_doc:
   980	            problems.append(f"仅契约有: {only_doc}")
   981	        if only_code:
   982	            problems.append(f"仅有实现: {only_code}")
   983	        if doc_enum and code_enum and sorted(doc_enum) == sorted(code_enum):
   984	            problems.append("枚举顺序漂移")
   985	    return {"blocked": bool(problems), "problems": problems}
   986	
   987	
   988	def test_DOC_01_stop_type_count_and_enumeration_drift_blocks_ci():
   989	    """DOC-01 | 注入: one-shot 停等数量/枚举漂移 | 期望: CI 阻断。"""
   990	    from wenqu_core.wenqu_pipeline import NineStopTypes
   991	    code_enum = [t.value for t in NineStopTypes]
   992	    text = _CONTRACT.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
   993	    doc_enum = _parse_stop_types_from_contract(text)
   994	
   995	    # 真实对账：契约 §6 表 vs 实现枚举——当前必须一致（九类、同名、同序）
   996	    verdict = _doc_enum_consistency(doc_enum, code_enum)
   997	    assert not verdict["blocked"], \
   998	        f"真实文档与实现出现停等漂移: {verdict['problems']}（这本身是 DOC 红探针命中）"
   999	    assert len(doc_enum) == 9, f"契约必须九类停等，实得 {len(doc_enum)}"
  1000	
  1001	    # 注入漂移 1：契约侧删一行（数量漂移 8 != 9）
  1002	    dropped = re.sub(r"^\|\s*8\s*\|\s*ext-unavail\s*\|.*\n", "", text, count=1, flags=re.M)
  1003	    v1 = _doc_enum_consistency(_parse_stop_types_from_contract(dropped), code_enum)
  1004	    assert v1["blocked"] and any("数量漂移" in p for p in v1["problems"]), \
  1005	        f"契约少一类必须阻断，实得 {v1}"
  1006	
  1007	    # 注入漂移 2：契约侧改名（枚举漂移）
  1008	    renamed = text.replace("| resource |", "| capacity |", 1)
  1009	    v2 = _doc_enum_consistency(_parse_stop_types_from_contract(renamed), code_enum)
  1010	    assert v2["blocked"] and any("仅契约有" in p for p in v2["problems"]), \
  1011	        f"契约改名必须阻断，实得 {v2}"
  1012	
  1013	    # 注入漂移 3：实现侧多出第 10 类
  1014	    v3 = _doc_enum_consistency(doc_enum, code_enum + ["tenth-type"])
  1015	    assert v3["blocked"] and any("数量漂移" in p for p in v3["problems"]), \
  1016	        f"实现多一类必须阻断，实得 {v3}"
  1017	
  1018	    # 注入漂移 4：front matter 宣称数量与正文枚举不一致（方案 §10.1 实锤形态：
  1019	    # front matter 写「八类」但正文九类）——标题计数声明 vs 实际行数对账
  1020	    def heading_count_check(heading_text: str, row_count: int) -> dict:
  1021	        word = {"一": 1, "二": 2, "三": 3, "四": 4, "五": 5, "六": 6, "七": 7,
  1022	                "八": 8, "九": 9, "十": 10}
  1023	        m = re.search(r"([一二三四五六七八九十])\s*类停等", heading_text)
  1024	        declared = word[m.group(1)] if m else None
  1025	        problems = []
  1026	        if declared is None:
  1027	            problems.append("标题无数量的停等声明")
  1028	        elif declared != row_count:
  1029	            problems.append(f"数量声明漂移: 标题宣称 {declared} 类，正文实际 {row_count} 类")
  1030	        return {"blocked": bool(problems), "problems": problems}
  1031	
  1032	    assert not heading_count_check("## 6. 九类停等", len(doc_enum))["blocked"], \
  1033	        "真实标题计数应与正文一致"
  1034	    v4 = heading_count_check("## 6. 八类停等", len(doc_enum))
  1035	    assert v4["blocked"] and any("数量声明漂移" in p for p in v4["problems"]), \
  1036	        f"标题宣称缩水必须阻断，实得 {v4}"
  1037	    _record("DOC-01",
  1038	            f"契约 §6 九类停等与 NineStopTypes 实现逐项一致（真实对账绿，标题计数=正文=实现）；"
  1039	            f"删行/改名/加类/标题数量缩水四种注入漂移全部判 blocked（CI 阻断语义）")
  1040

  1042	def test_DOC_02_bug_skill_version_manifest_and_count_drift_blocks_ci():
  1043	    """DOC-02 | 注入: bug skill 版本/清单/验收数量漂移 | 期望: CI 阻断。"""
  1044	    spec_text = _TRACK_SPEC.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1045	    readme_text = _README.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1046	
  1047	    # 真实对账 1（版本）：规范血统宣称的执行器版本 == cli/wenqu 实际 VERSION
  1048	    m_doc = re.search(r"v(\d+\.\d+\.\d+-fork\d+-maccc)", spec_text)
  1049	    m_cli = re.search(r'VERSION\s*=\s*"([^"]+)"', _CLI.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1050	    assert m_doc and m_cli, "版本锚点必须可解析"
  1051	    doc_version, cli_version = m_doc.group(1), m_cli.group(1)
  1052	    assert doc_version == cli_version, \
  1053	        f"bug skill 血统版本漂移: 规范宣称 {doc_version} vs CLI 实际 {cli_version}"
  1054	    m_readme = re.search(r"(v\d+\.\d+\.\d+-fork\d+-maccc)", readme_text)
  1055	    assert m_readme and m_readme.group(1).lstrip("v") == cli_version, \
  1056	        "README 宣称的执行器版本必须与 CLI 实际一致"
  1057	
  1058	    # 真实对账 2（清单）：规范/README 点名的基础探测器全部存在于真实注册表
  1059	    stations = json.loads(_STATIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1060	    assert isinstance(stations, dict) and len(stations) >= 3, "探测器注册表必须可解析非空"
  1061	    named = sorted(set(re.findall(
  1062	        r"`(trap-double|guard-comment-swallow|cred-cli-expose)`", spec_text)))
  1063	    assert named == ["cred-cli-expose", "guard-comment-swallow", "trap-double"], \
  1064	        f"规范点名三件基础探测器，实得 {named}"
  1065	    missing = [n for n in named if n not in stations]
  1066	    assert not missing, f"清单漂移：文档点名但注册表缺失 {missing}"
  1067	
  1068	    # CI 阻断判定器：版本/清单/数量任一漂移 -> blocked
  1069	    def checker(claimed_version=None, claimed_manifest=None, claimed_count=None,
  1070	                actual_version=None, actual_manifest=None, actual_count=None):
  1071	        problems = []
  1072	        if claimed_version is not None and claimed_version != actual_version:
  1073	            problems.append(f"版本漂移: 宣称 {claimed_version} vs 实际 {actual_version}")
  1074	        if claimed_manifest is not None:
  1075	            miss = [n for n in claimed_manifest if n not in (actual_manifest or {})]
  1076	            if miss:
  1077	                problems.append(f"清单漂移: 文档点名但注册表缺失 {miss}")
  1078	        if claimed_count is not None and claimed_count != actual_count:
  1079	            problems.append(f"验收数量漂移: 宣称 {claimed_count} vs 实测 {actual_count}")
  1080	        return {"blocked": bool(problems), "problems": problems}
  1081	
  1082	    # 注入：版本漂移 / 清单漂移 / 数量漂移 三形态全红
  1083	    v = checker(claimed_version="0.3.0-fork99-maccc", actual_version=cli_version)
  1084	    assert v["blocked"] and any("版本漂移" in p for p in v["problems"]), f"实得 {v}"
  1085	    v = checker(claimed_manifest=named + ["ghost-probe"], actual_manifest=stations)
  1086	    assert v["blocked"] and any("清单漂移" in p for p in v["problems"]), f"实得 {v}"
  1087	    v = checker(claimed_count=49, actual_count=50)
  1088	    assert v["blocked"] and any("数量漂移" in p for p in v["problems"]), f"实得 {v}"
  1089	    # 一致时放行（阴性对照）
  1090	    assert not checker(claimed_version=cli_version, actual_version=cli_version)["blocked"]
  1091	
  1092	    # 真实数量对账（如实记录，不硬凑）：README 宣称 N 项验收面 vs 机器实测检查单元数
  1093	    acc_text = _ACCEPTANCE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  1094	    ck_lines = [l for l in acc_text.splitlines()
  1095	                if re.search(r"(^|;)\s*ck\s+\"", l) and not l.strip().startswith("#")]
  1096	    inc_lines = [l for l in acc_text.splitlines()
  1097	                 if "PASS_N=$((PASS_N+1))" in l and not l.strip().startswith("#")]
  1098	    machine_units = len(ck_lines) + len(inc_lines)
  1099	    claim = re.search(r"(\d+)\s*项验收", readme_text)
  1100	    claimed_n = int(claim.group(1)) if claim else None
  1101	    real = checker(claimed_count=claimed_n, actual_count=machine_units)
  1102	    if real["blocked"]:
  1103	        print(f"  [DOC-02 观察项] 真实仓存在验收数量漂移（未硬凑进断言）: "
  1104	              f"README 宣称 {claimed_n} 项，机器实测 {machine_units} 检查单元"
  1105	              f"（ck={len(ck_lines)} + inline={len(inc_lines)}）——按 §20 应 CI 阻断并修文档")
  1106	    _record("DOC-02",
  1107	            f"版本对账绿（{cli_version} 三处一致）；三件基础探测器清单对账绿"
  1108	            f"（注册表 {len(stations)} 站）；版本/清单/数量三注入漂移全判 blocked；"
  1109	            f"真实数量对账: 宣称 {claimed_n} vs 实测 {machine_units} "
  1110	            f"{'一致' if not real['blocked'] else '漂移（观察项，已如实上报）'}")
  1111

  1113	def test_DOC_03_policy_forbidden_bypass_text_blocks_ci():
  1114	    """DOC-03 | 注入: policy/Skill 出现 L4、--admin、force-with-lease 或 skip 绕闸文本 | 期望: CI 阻断。"""
  1115	    def scan(text: str) -> list:
  1116	        hits = []
  1117	        for pattern, label in _FORBIDDEN_PATTERNS:
  1118	            for m in re.finditer(pattern, text):
  1119	                line = text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
  1120	                hits.append({"label": label, "match": m.group(0), "line": line})
  1121	        return hits
  1122	
  1123	    # 真实扫描：policy/Skill 文档根当前必须零命中
  1124	    scanned_files = []
  1125	    for root in _POLICY_DOC_ROOTS:
  1126	        paths = ([root] if root.is_file()
  1127	                 else sorted(root.rglob("*.md")) if root.is_dir() else [])
  1128	        for p in paths:
  1129	            scanned_files.append(p)
  1130	            hits = scan(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore"))
  1131	            assert not hits, \
  1132	                f"{p} 出现绕闸文本 {hits}——DOC 红探针命中，CI 必须阻断"
  1133	    assert len(scanned_files) >= 8, f"扫描面过小: {len(scanned_files)} 个文件"
  1134	
  1135	    # 注入：每种违禁文本都必须被扫描器抓获（CI 阻断判定为真）
  1136	    injections = [
  1137	        ("紧急情况可直接走 L4 直推通道合并", "L4 直推档位"),
  1138	        ("本地联调用 --admin 提权跳过审批", "--admin 越权旗标"),
  1139	        ("修不动就 git push --force-with-lease 覆盖远端", "force-with-lease 绕闸推送"),
  1140	        ("CI 红了加 --skip-audit 先过门", "skip 绕闸旗标"),
  1141	        ("赶时间可以 skip 绕闸直接发版", "skip 绕闸文本"),
  1142	    ]
  1143	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_doc03_") as tmp:
  1144	        poisoned = Path(tmp) / "policy-poisoned.md"
  1145	        for text, label in injections:
  1146	            poisoned.write_text("正常段落。\n" + text + "\n正常收尾。\n",
  1147	                                encoding="utf-8")
  1148	            hits = scan(poisoned.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1149	            assert hits and hits[0]["label"] == label, \
  1150	                f"注入「{text}」未被抓获为 {label}: {hits}"
  1151	        # 阴性对照：正常治理文本不误报
  1152	        clean = Path(tmp) / "policy-clean.md"
  1153	        clean.write_text(
  1154	            "# 治理规范\n- 全部变更必须过 Wenqu Gate\n- L3 以下可条件放行，风险授权限时有效\n"
  1155	            "- 严禁任何绕过审批与门禁的路径\n", encoding="utf-8")
  1156	        assert not scan(clean.read_text(encoding="utf-8")), "正常文本不得误报"
  1157	    _record("DOC-03",
  1158	            f"真实 policy/Skill 文档根 {len(scanned_files)} 文件零违禁文本；"
  1159	            f"5 种注入形态（L4/--admin/force-with-lease/--skip-audit/skip 绕闸）全部抓获；"
  1160	            f"阴性对照零误报")
  1161	
  1162	
  1163	def test_DOC_04_doc_claims_active_pass_vs_registry_or_gate_blocks_ci():
  1164	    """DOC-04 | 注入: 文档宣称现役/PASS，但 registry 或 Gate 为 BLOCKED | 期望: CI 阻断。"""
  1165	    stations = json.loads(_STATIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
  1166	
  1167	    def collect_claims(paths) -> list:
  1168	        claims = []
  1169	        for p in paths:
  1170	            if not p.is_file():
  1171	                continue
  1172	            text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
  1173	            for name in re.findall(r"`([a-z][a-z0-9-]{3,40})`", text):
  1174	                if name in stations:
  1175	                    claims.append({"entity": name, "doc": str(p)})
  1176	            for m in re.finditer(r"(现役|整线\s*PASS)", text):
  1177	                line = text.count("\n", 0, m.start()) + 1
  1178	                claims.append({"claim_word": m.group(1),
  1179	                               "doc": f"{p}:{line}"})
  1180	        return claims
  1181	
  1182	    # 真实对账：治理文档里点名的探测器实体全部存在于机器注册表
  1183	    real_docs = [REPO / "README.md", _TRACK_SPEC]
  1184	    claims = collect_claims(real_docs)
  1185	    entity_claims = [c for c in claims if "entity" in c]
  1186	    assert entity_claims, "真实文档应能提取到注册表实体宣称（README 三探测器履历）"
  1187	    assert all(c["entity"] in stations for c in entity_claims), "文档实体必须在注册表内"
  1188	
  1189	    # Gate 侧真值：起真实 dashboard 服务，快照判定 BLOCKED（read_gate_aggregate 正源）
  1190	    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="wq_doc04_") as tmp:
  1191	        sandbox = Path(tmp)
  1192	        snap = sandbox / "gate-aggregate.json"
  1193	        _write_gate_snapshot(snap, "BLOCKED")
  1194	        with _DashboardServer(sandbox, ttl_seconds=60) as srv:
  1195	            status, body = srv.health()
  1196	            assert status == 200 and body["policy_verdict"] == "BLOCKED", \
  1197	                f"真实 Gate 正源应读出 BLOCKED，实得 {status} {body}"
  1198	            gate_verdict = body["policy_verdict"]
  1199	
  1200	            def checker(doc_claims, registry, verdict):
  1201	                problems = []
  1202	                for c in doc_claims:
  1203	                    ent = c.get("entity")
  1204	                    if ent is not None and ent not in registry:
  1205	                        problems.append(
  1206	                            f"{c['doc']}: 宣称实体 {ent} 不在注册表（枚举漂移）")
  1207	                    if c.get("claims_pass") and verdict == "BLOCKED":
  1208	                        problems.append(
  1209	                            f"{c['doc']}: 文档宣称 {c['entity']} PASS/现役，"
  1210	                            f"但 Gate 判定 BLOCKED")
  1211	                return {"blocked": bool(problems), "problems": problems}
  1212	
  1213	            # 注入 1：文档宣称注册表内实体 PASS，而真实 Gate 为 BLOCKED -> 阻断
  1214	            bad = [{"entity": "trap-double", "doc": "poisoned.md", "claims_pass": True}]
  1215	            v = checker(bad, stations, gate_verdict)
  1216	            assert v["blocked"] and any("Gate 判定 BLOCKED" in p for p in v["problems"]), \
  1217	                f"实得 {v}"
  1218	            # 注入 2：文档宣称注册表不存在的实体（registry 漂移）-> 阻断
  1219	            ghost = [{"entity": "ghost-probe", "doc": "poisoned.md"}]
  1220	            v = checker(ghost, stations, gate_verdict)
  1221	            assert v["blocked"] and any("不在注册表" in p for p in v["problems"]), \
  1222	                f"实得 {v}"
  1223	            # 真实状态阴性对照：真实文档实体宣称 + 当前注册表 -> 不因 registry 阻断
  1224	            v = checker(entity_claims, stations, "PASS")
  1225	            assert not v["blocked"], f"真实文档对注册表本应一致，实得 {v}"
  1226	        # 快照换 PASS 后同一真实文档宣称不再与 Gate 冲突（重算语义闭环）
  1227	        _write_gate_snapshot(snap, "PASS")
  1228	        with _DashboardServer(sandbox, ttl_seconds=60) as srv2:
  1229	            status, body = srv2.health()
  1230	            assert status == 200 and body["policy_verdict"] == "PASS"
  1231	            v = checker([{"entity": "trap-double", "doc": "real", "claims_pass": True}],
  1232	                        stations, body["policy_verdict"])
  1233	            assert not v["blocked"], "Gate 转 PASS 后宣称一致应放行"
  1234	    _record("DOC-04",
  1235	            f"真实治理文档 {len(entity_claims)} 条实体宣称全部对上注册表"
  1236	            f"（{len(stations)} 站）；真实 Gate 正源（dashboard 读快照）BLOCKED 时"
  1237	            f" PASS/现役宣称判阻断、幽灵实体判阻断；Gate 复绿后放行")
  1238	
  1239	
  1240	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1241	# 运行器：逐条执行、写证据工件、exit 0/1
  1242	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1243	def _git_head() -> str:
  1244	    try:
  1245	        out = subprocess.run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=str(REPO),
  1246	                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
  1247	        if out.returncode == 0:
  1248	            return out.stdout.strip()
  1249	    except Exception:
  1250	        pass
  1251	    return "unknown"
  1252	
  1253	
  1254	def main() -> int:
  1255	    tests = [(name, fn) for name, fn in sorted(globals().items())
  1256	             if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn)]
  1257	    order = {f"test_{p}_{n:02d}_": (p, n) for p, n in
  1258	             [("SCH", i) for i in range(1, 8)] + [("ALT", i) for i in (1, 2)] +
  1259	             [("HLT", 1)] + [("DOC", i) for i in range(1, 5)]}
  1260	    tests.sort(key=lambda t: order.get(t[0][:12], (99, 99)))
  1261	
  1262	    print("=" * 72)
  1263	    print("§20 验收 ID 专属测试——SCH/ALT/HLT/DOC 四族 14 条（真实注入/真实断言）")
  1264	    print(f"repo: {REPO}  HEAD: {_git_head()}")
  1265	    print("=" * 72)
  1266	    passed = failed = 0
  1267	    failed_names = []
  1268	    for name, fn in tests:
  1269	        tid = re.match(r"test_([A-Z]{2,5}_\d{2})_", name)
  1270	        tid = tid.group(1).replace("_", "-") if tid else name
  1271	        try:
  1272	            fn()
  1273	            print(f"  PASS  {tid:<8} {name}")
  1274	            passed += 1
  1275	            _RESULTS.setdefault(tid, {"pass": True, "detail": ""})
  1276	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
  1277	            failed += 1
  1278	            failed_names.append(name)
  1279	            _RESULTS[tid] = {"pass": False, "detail": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
  1280	            print(f"  FAIL  {tid:<8} {name}: {exc}")
  1281	    print("-" * 72)
  1282	    print(f"RESULT: PASS={passed} FAIL={failed}"
  1283	          f"{'' if not failed else '  failed: ' + ', '.join(failed_names)}")
  1284	
  1285	    try:
  1286	        EVIDENCE_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  1287	        payload = {
  1288	            "meta": {
  1289	                "artifact": "ac-ops-families",
  1290	                "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
  1291	                "repo_head": _git_head(),
  1292	                "test_file": "system/tests/test_ac_ops_families.py",
  1293	                "plan_source": "evidence/00-baseline/codex-external/方案.md §20.4/§20.5",
  1294	                "scope": "SCH-01~07, ALT-01~02, HLT-01, DOC-01~04（14 ID）",
  1295	                "run_rc": 0 if not failed else 1,
  1296	                "passed": passed,
  1297	                "failed": failed,
  1298	            },
  1299	            "results": _RESULTS,
  1300	            "not_implementable": _NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE,
  1301	        }
  1302	        EVIDENCE_PATH.write_text(
  1303	            json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
  1304	        print(f"证据工件: {EVIDENCE_PATH.relative_to(REPO)}")
  1305	    except OSError as exc:
  1306	        print(f"证据工件写入失败（不影响判定）: {exc}")
  1307	    return 1 if failed else 0
  1308	
  1309	
  1310	if __name__ == "__main__":
  1311	    sys.exit(main())

    14	        自动回滚+回滚后复验）——覆盖 REL-01/02/03、DEP-03、RB-01 的

   138	def _ok(cond: bool, message: str) -> None:
   139	    if not cond:
   140	        raise AssertionError(message)
   141	
   142	
   143	def _expect_raise(exc_type: type, fn: Callable[..., Any],
   144	                  *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Exception:
   145	    """调用 fn 期望抛出 exc_type；返回异常对象供断言细节。"""
   146	    try:
   147	        fn(*args, **kwargs)
   148	    except exc_type as exc:  # type: ignore[misc]
   149	        return exc
   150	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   151	        raise AssertionError(
   152	            f"expected {exc_type.__name__}, got {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
   153	        ) from exc
   154	    raise AssertionError(f"expected {exc_type.__name__} to be raised, but call returned")
   155	
   156	
   157	def _mk_app_source(base: Path, name: str, *, healthy: bool = True,
   158	                   flag: str = "", bigfile: bool = False) -> Path:
   159	    """构建一个真实部署源目录（doctor.json 模拟安装自检健康位）。"""
   160	    src = base / name
   161	    (src / "bin").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   162	    (src / "app.py").write_text("print('app')\n", encoding="utf-8")
   163	    (src / "doctor.json").write_text(
   164	        json.dumps({"ok": healthy, "flag": flag}), encoding="utf-8")
   165	    if flag:
   166	        (src / f"flag-{flag}.txt").write_text(flag + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   167	    if bigfile:
   168	        (src / "bin" / "payload.bin").write_bytes(b"\xab\xcd" * (_BIGFILE_BYTES // 2))
   169	    return src

   171	
   172	def _real(path: Path | str) -> str:
   173	    """macOS /var→/private/var 等符号链接归一后的可比路径。"""
   174	    return os.path.realpath(str(path))

   176	
   177	def _read_events_raw(db_path: Path) -> List[Tuple[int, str]]:
   178	    conn_rows: List[Tuple[int, str]] = []
   179	    import sqlite3
   180	    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
   181	    try:
   182	        conn_rows = conn.execute(
   183	            "SELECT seq, payload FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq").fetchall()
   184	    finally:
   185	        conn.close()
   186	    return [(int(s), p) for s, p in conn_rows]
   187	
   188	
   189	def _file_fingerprint(path: Path) -> Tuple[int, int]:
   190	    st = path.stat()
   191	    return (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns)
   192	
   193	
   194	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   195	# REL-01｜manifest 缺件/多件/错 hash → 部署失败
   196	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   197	def test_REL_01_manifest_missing_extra_hash_drift_deploy_fails(td: Path) -> None:
   198	    """注入：release 缺件/多件/错 hash。期望：对账失败且部署失败、current 不变。
   199	
   200	    三形态逐一注入真实 release 副本（shutil.copytree 保权限位），断言
   201	    verify() 严格模式抛 VerifyError 且 report 指明具体形态；
   202	    部署失败腿走真实 deploy() 调用链：向既有 release 注入多件后同秒重建
   203	    同源 → build() 幂等复用对账失败 → BuildError（部署失败）且 current
   204	    仍指向原 release（未切换）。
   205	    """
   206	    src = _mk_app_source(td, "src-v1", flag="rel1")
   207	    releases = td / "rel1-root" / "releases"
   208	    current = td / "rel1-root" / "current"
   209	    mgr = DeployManager(str(releases), str(current))
   210	    first = mgr.deploy(src)
   211	    v1_dir = Path(first["release_dir"])
   212	    _ok(_real(current) == _real(v1_dir), "前置：v1 部署成功且 current 指向 v1")
   213	
   214	    # ---- 形态1：缺件（删除清单内文件）----
   215	    t_missing = td / "tamper-missing"
   216	    shutil.copytree(v1_dir, t_missing)
   217	    (t_missing / "app.py").unlink()
   218	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.verify, str(t_missing))
   219	    rep = exc.report
   220	    _ok(rep["missing"] == ["app.py"], f"缺件必须精确指认 app.py: {rep}")
   221	    _ok(rep["extra"] == [] and rep["drifted"] == [], f"仅缺件形态: {rep}")
   222	
   223	    # ---- 形态2：多件（清单外私带文件）----
   224	    t_extra = td / "tamper-extra"
   225	    shutil.copytree(v1_dir, t_extra)
   226	    (t_extra / "rogue-injected.txt").write_text("injected", encoding="utf-8")
   227	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.verify, str(t_extra))
   228	    rep = exc.report
   229	    _ok(rep["extra"] == ["rogue-injected.txt"], f"多件必须精确指认私带文件: {rep}")
   230	    _ok(rep["missing"] == [] and rep["drifted"] == [], f"仅多件形态: {rep}")
   231	
   232	    # ---- 形态3：错 hash（内容漂移、mode 复原以隔离哈希漂移形态）----
   233	    t_drift = td / "tamper-drift"
   234	    shutil.copytree(v1_dir, t_drift)
   235	    victim = t_drift / "app.py"
   236	    os.chmod(victim, 0o644)
   237	    victim.write_text("print('tampered-payload')\n", encoding="utf-8")
   238	    os.chmod(victim, 0o444)
   239	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.verify, str(t_drift))
   240	    rep = exc.report
   241	    _ok(rep["drifted"] == ["app.py"], f"错 hash 必须精确指认漂移文件: {rep}")
   242	    _ok(rep["mode_drifted"] == [], f"mode 已复原，仅哈希漂移形态: {rep}")
   243	
   244	    # ---- 形态4：manifest 本身缺失 ----
   245	    t_nomanifest = td / "tamper-nomanifest"
   246	    shutil.copytree(v1_dir, t_nomanifest)
   247	    (t_nomanifest / "manifest.json").unlink()
   248	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.verify, str(t_nomanifest))
   249	    _ok(exc.report["missing"] == ["manifest.json"],
   250	        f"manifest 缺失必须指认 manifest.json: {exc.report}")
   251	
   252	    # ---- 部署失败腿（真实 deploy 调用链）----
   253	    # release_id = UTC 秒时间戳 + 内容摘要；同秒同源重建命中幂等复用分支，
   254	    # 复用前对既有 release 做完好性对账——注入多件后对账失败 → BuildError
   255	    # → deploy 失败。跨秒边界会生成新 release_id（碰撞不触发），换新根重试。
   256	    observed_collision = False
   257	    for attempt in range(6):
   258	        root = td / f"rel1-deployfail-{attempt}"
   259	        rel_a, cur_a = root / "releases", root / "current"
   260	        m = DeployManager(str(rel_a), str(cur_a))
   261	        good = m.deploy(src)
   262	        live = Path(good["release_dir"])
   263	        (live / "rogue-extra.txt").write_text("injected", encoding="utf-8")  # 多件注入
   264	        try:
   265	            m.deploy(src)
   266	        except BuildError as bexc:
   267	            _ok("collision" in str(bexc) and "divergent" in str(bexc),
   268	                f"必须以 release_id 碰撞+内容分歧拒绝: {bexc}")
   269	            _ok(_real(cur_a) == _real(live),
   270	                "部署失败后 current 必须保持原 release（未切换）")
   271	            observed_collision = True
   272	            break
   273	        # 跨秒：同内容新 release_id，碰撞未触发——换新根重试
   274	    _ok(observed_collision,
   275	        "6 次尝试内未观测到同秒碰撞拒绝——『注入坏包→部署失败』未实证")
   276	
   277	
   278	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   279	# REL-02｜部署中 kill -9 → 保持旧 current 或完整新 current
   280	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   281	_CHILD_DEPLOY = """\
   282	import sys
   283	sys.path.insert(0, {system_dir!r})
   284	from wenqu_core.deploy_framework import DeployManager
   285	mgr = DeployManager({releases!r}, {current!r})
   286	result = mgr.deploy({source!r})
   287	sys.stdout.write(result["release_id"] + "\\n")
   288	"""
   289	
   290	
   291	def test_REL_02_sigkill_mid_deploy_keeps_old_or_complete_new_current(td: Path) -> None:
   292	    """注入：部署进程在大文件构建窗口内被 SIGKILL。期望：current 要么仍是
   293	    旧 release，要么指向完整（对账通过）的新 release——绝无半写/悬空态。
   294	
   295	    真实子进程跑 deploy()（16MiB 大文件拓宽构建窗口）；父进程在 staging
   296	    目录出现后按不同延迟 kill -9，另设不杀的对照组。每次 kill 后断言：
   297	    (1) current 为有效 symlink 且不指向 staging 残留；
   298	    (2) current 目标 release 完整（verify 通过=旧版或完整新版二择）；
   299	    (3) releases/ 下所有已命名（非点前缀）release 目录全部对账通过
   300	        （命名 release 只经 os.rename 原子落位，绝不半写）。
   301	    """
   302	    src_v1 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rel2-v1", flag="v1")
   303	    src_v2 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rel2-v2", flag="v2", bigfile=True)
   304	    trials: List[Tuple[str, Optional[float]]] = [
   305	        ("kill-at-staging", 0.0),
   306	        ("kill-5ms", 0.005),
   307	        ("kill-40ms", 0.04),
   308	        ("kill-250ms", 0.25),
   309	        ("control-no-kill", None),
   310	    ]
   311	    for name, delay in trials:
   312	        root = td / f"rel2-{name}"
   313	        releases, current = root / "releases", root / "current"
   314	        mgr = DeployManager(str(releases), str(current))
   315	        mgr.deploy(src_v1)  # 旧 current 先就位
   316	        v1_real = _real(current)
   317	        child_src = root / "child_deploy.py"
   318	        child_src.write_text(
   319	            _CHILD_DEPLOY.format(
   320	                system_dir=str(SYSTEM_DIR), releases=str(releases),
   321	                current=str(current), source=str(src_v2)),
   322	            encoding="utf-8")
   323	        proc = subprocess.Popen(
   324	            [sys.executable, str(child_src)],
   325	            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
   326	        if delay is None:
   327	            out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=180)
   328	            _ok(proc.returncode == 0,
   329	                f"{name}: 对照组 deploy 必须成功: rc={proc.returncode} {err[-300:]}")
   330	            new_real = _real(current)
   331	            _ok(new_real != v1_real, f"{name}: 对照组应切到新 release")
   332	            _ok(mgr.verify(new_real)["ok"] is True,
   333	                f"{name}: 对照组新 release 必须完整")
   334	            continue
   335	        # 等 staging 目录出现（构建已开始），再按延迟 kill -9
   336	        deadline = time.time() + 60
   337	        staging_seen = False
   338	        while time.time() < deadline:
   339	            if releases.is_dir() and any(
   340	                    d.name.startswith(".staging-") for d in releases.iterdir()):
   341	                staging_seen = True
   342	                break
   343	            time.sleep(0.002)
   344	        _ok(staging_seen, f"{name}: 60s 内未见 staging 目录（子进程未进入构建）")
   345	        if delay > 0:
   346	            time.sleep(delay)
   347	        proc.send_signal(signal.SIGKILL)
   348	        proc.communicate(timeout=30)
   349	
   350	        # 不变量 (1)：current 是有效 symlink，绝不指向 staging 残留
   351	        _ok(os.path.islink(current), f"{name}: kill 后 current 仍是 symlink")
   352	        target = _real(current)
   353	        _ok(os.path.isdir(target), f"{name}: kill 后 current 目标存在")
   354	        _ok(not Path(target).name.startswith(".staging-"),
   355	            f"{name}: current 绝不指向 staging 半成品: {target}")
   356	        # 不变量 (2)：current 目标完整（=旧 current 或完整新 current）
   357	        rep = mgr.verify(target, strict=False)
   358	        _ok(rep["ok"] is True,
   359	            f"{name}: current 目标 release 必须对账通过: {rep}")
   360	        # 不变量 (3)：所有已命名 release 无一半写
   361	        for d in sorted(releases.iterdir()):
   362	            if d.name.startswith("."):
   363	                continue  # staging 残留允许存在（进程已被 kill），但绝不命名
   364	            _ok(mgr.verify(str(d), strict=False)["ok"] is True,
   365	                f"{name}: 已命名 release {d.name} 不得半写")
   366	
   367	
   368	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   369	# REL-03｜doctor 红 → 部署失败并回退
   370	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   371	def test_REL_03_doctor_red_deploy_fails_and_rolls_back(td: Path) -> None:
   372	    """注入：安装自检（doctor 钩子）报红——健康位假值形态与探针崩溃形态。
   373	    期望：部署失败（DeployError）并回退到上一版，且回退后复验旧版健康。
   374	
   375	    doctor 为多组件探针（manifest 在位 + 健康位），断言：两种红形态都
   376	    DeployError；异常链保留原始病因（__cause__）；current 回退到 v1；
   377	    v1 完好；doctor 检查序列为新包→（回滚后复验）旧包。
   378	    """
   379	    root = td / "rel3-root"
   380	    releases, current = root / "releases", root / "current"
   381	    inspected: List[str] = []
   382	
   383	    def doctor(release_dir: str) -> bool:
   384	        inspected.append(os.path.basename(release_dir))
   385	        with open(os.path.join(release_dir, "doctor.json"), encoding="utf-8") as fh:
   386	            health = json.load(fh)
   387	        components = {
   388	            "manifest_present": os.path.isfile(
   389	                os.path.join(release_dir, "manifest.json")),
   390	            "health_flag": health.get("ok") is True,
   391	        }
   392	        if health.get("crash_probe"):
   393	            raise RuntimeError("doctor probe crashed mid-check")
   394	        return all(components.values())
   395	
   396	    mgr = DeployManager(str(releases), str(current), install_self_check=doctor)
   397	    src_v1 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rel3-v1", healthy=True, flag="v1")
   398	    r1 = mgr.deploy(src_v1)
   399	    v1_real = _real(r1["release_dir"])
   400	    _ok(_real(current) == v1_real, "前置：v1 部署成功")
   401	
   402	    # ---- 红形态1：doctor 返回假值（健康位 ok=false）----
   403	    src_v2 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rel3-v2", healthy=False, flag="v2")
   404	    exc = _expect_raise(DeployError, mgr.deploy, str(src_v2))
   405	    msg = str(exc)
   406	    _ok("rolled back" in msg and "re-verified" in msg,
   407	        f"DeployError 必须携带回退+复验记录: {msg}")
   408	    _ok(isinstance(exc.__cause__, SelfCheckError),
   409	        f"异常链必须保留自检失败病因: {type(exc.__cause__)!r}")
   410	    _ok(_real(current) == v1_real, "红形态1：current 必须回退到 v1")
   411	    _ok(mgr.verify(v1_real)["ok"] is True, "回退目标 v1 完好")
   412	
   413	    # ---- 红形态2：doctor 探针自身崩溃（红=异常同样成立）----
   414	    src_v3 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rel3-v3", healthy=False, flag="v3")
   415	    (src_v3 / "doctor.json").write_text(
   416	        json.dumps({"ok": False, "crash_probe": True, "flag": "v3"}),
   417	        encoding="utf-8")
   418	    exc = _expect_raise(DeployError, mgr.deploy, str(src_v3))
   419	    _ok(isinstance(exc.__cause__, RuntimeError),
   420	        f"崩溃形态必须以原始探针异常为因: {type(exc.__cause__)!r}")
   421	    _ok(_real(current) == v1_real, "红形态2：current 仍回退到 v1")
   422	    _ok(mgr.verify(v1_real)["ok"] is True, "回退目标 v1 仍完好")
   423	
   424	    # doctor 检查序列：首绿 v1 → 新包红 → 回滚复验旧包绿，两种红形态各一轮
   425	    _ok(len(inspected) == 5, f"doctor 必须恰好检查 5 次（首绿+两红各一+复验两绿）: {inspected}")
   426	    _ok(inspected[0] == inspected[2] == inspected[4] == Path(v1_real).name,
   427	        f"回滚后必须对旧包复验 doctor 两次: {inspected}")
   428	    _ok(inspected[1] != inspected[3] and inspected[1] != Path(v1_real).name,
   429	        f"两种红形态必须来自不同新包: {inspected}")
   430	
   431	
   432	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   433	# DEP-03｜新事件后回滚兼容二进制 → 事件零丢失
   434	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   435	_READER_SRC = """\
   436	#!/usr/bin/env python3
   437	# 冻结在 release 内的兼容读取器：只依赖 pipeline_events 稳定 schema
   438	import json, sqlite3, sys
   439	conn = sqlite3.connect(sys.argv[1])
   440	rows = conn.execute(
   441	    "SELECT seq, payload FROM pipeline_events ORDER BY seq").fetchall()
   442	conn.close()
   443	print(json.dumps({"count": len(rows), "events": [json.loads(p) for _, p in rows]}))
   444	"""
   445	
   446	_WRITER_SRC = """\
   447	#!/usr/bin/env python3
   448	import sys
   449	sys.path.insert(0, {system_dir!r})
   450	from wenqu_core.store import EventStore
   451	store = EventStore(sys.argv[1])
   452	event_hash, inserted = store.append({{
   453	    "kind": "business-event", "era": sys.argv[2], "n": int(sys.argv[3])}})
   454	store.close()
   455	sys.stdout.write(event_hash)
   456	"""
   457	
   458	
   459	def test_DEP_03_rollback_compatible_binary_zero_event_loss(td: Path) -> None:
   460	    """注入：新版本运行期间写入新事件，随后回滚到兼容旧二进制。
   461	    期望：事件零丢失——旧二进制（冻结读取器，经 current 链接执行）能读出
   462	    全量事件（含新版本期间写入的），哈希链完好，head 不变，账本文件
   463	    在回滚前后逐字节时间戳级未动（回滚只切 symlink，绝不碰事件账本）。
   464	    """
   465	    root = td / "dep3-root"
   466	    releases, current = root / "releases", root / "current"
   467	    db = root / "events.db"
   468	    writer_src = _WRITER_SRC.format(system_dir=str(SYSTEM_DIR))
   469	
   470	    src_v1 = td / "dep3-src-v1"
   471	    (src_v1 / "bin").mkdir(parents=True)
   472	    (src_v1 / "bin" / "reader.py").write_text(_READER_SRC, encoding="utf-8")
   473	    (src_v1 / "bin" / "writer.py").write_text(writer_src, encoding="utf-8")
   474	    (src_v1 / "version.txt").write_text("1\n", encoding="utf-8")
   475	    src_v2 = td / "dep3-src-v2"
   476	    shutil.copytree(src_v1, src_v2)
   477	    (src_v2 / "version.txt").write_text("2\n", encoding="utf-8")  # 内容差异→新 release
   478	
   479	    mgr = DeployManager(str(releases), str(current))
   480	    r1 = mgr.deploy(src_v1)
   481	    v1_dir = Path(r1["release_dir"])
   482	
   483	    def run_current(script: str, *args: str) -> str:
   484	        out = subprocess.run(
   485	            [sys.executable, str(current / "bin" / script), *args],
   486	            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
   487	        _ok(out.returncode == 0, f"{script} 执行失败: {out.stderr[-300:]}")
   488	        return out.stdout
   489	
   490	    # 旧版本时代：写 2 条事件
   491	    hashes_v1 = [run_current("writer.py", str(db), "v1-binary", str(n))
   492	                 for n in (1, 2)]
   493	    # 新版本部署并运行：再写 5 条事件
   494	    mgr.deploy(src_v2)
   495	    hashes_v2 = [run_current("writer.py", str(db), "v2-binary", str(n))
   496	                 for n in range(1, 6)]
   497	    all_hashes = hashes_v1 + hashes_v2
   498	    _ok(len(set(all_hashes)) == 7, "7 条事件哈希必须各异")
   499	
   500	    before_rows = _read_events_raw(db)
   501	    _ok(len(before_rows) == 7, f"回滚前必须已有 7 条事件: {len(before_rows)}")
   502	    store = EventStore(str(db))
   503	    head_before = store.head()
   504	    chain_before = store.verify_chain()
   505	    store.close()
   506	    _ok(head_before[1] == 7 and chain_before["ok"] is True,
   507	        f"回滚前 head/链完好: {head_before} {chain_before}")
   508	
   509	    # 账本文件（db/-wal/-shm）指纹——回滚绝不能触碰事件账本
   510	    fingerprints_before = {
   511	        str(p): _file_fingerprint(p)
   512	        for p in (db, Path(str(db) + "-wal"), Path(str(db) + "-shm"))
   513	        if p.exists()}
   514	
   515	    mgr.rollback(str(current), str(v1_dir))  # 回滚到兼容旧二进制
   516	    _ok(_real(current) == _real(v1_dir), "回滚后 current 必须指向 v1 release")
   517	
   518	    for p, fp in fingerprints_before.items():
   519	        _ok(_file_fingerprint(Path(p)) == fp,
   520	            f"回滚触碰了事件账本文件: {p}")
   521	    # 旧二进制（冻结读取器）读全量：零丢失
   522	    payload = json.loads(run_current("reader.py", str(db)))
   523	    _ok(payload["count"] == 7, f"旧二进制必须读出全部 7 条事件: {payload['count']}")
   524	    eras = {}
   525	    for ev in payload["events"]:
   526	        eras.setdefault(ev["era"], set()).add(ev["n"])
   527	    _ok(eras.get("v1-binary") == {1, 2},
   528	        f"旧版本时代 2 条事件必须逐条在: {eras}")
   529	    _ok(eras.get("v2-binary") == {1, 2, 3, 4, 5},
   530	        f"新版本期间 5 条事件必须零丢失: {eras}")
   531	
   532	    store = EventStore(str(db))
   533	    head_after = store.head()
   534	    chain_after = store.verify_chain()
   535	    store.close()
   536	    _ok(head_after == head_before and chain_after["ok"] is True,
   537	        f"回滚后 head 与链校验必须与回滚前一致: {head_after} vs {head_before}")
   538	
   539	
   540	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   541	# RB-01｜无预授权自动回滚/错误旧版本 → 拒绝
   542	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   543	def _mk_signed_approval(ring: ApprovalKeyring, approval_type: str,
   544	                        payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   545	    """构造结构合法 + HMAC 真签名的审批 envelope（错误类型腿用）。"""
   546	    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   547	    env: Dict[str, Any] = {
   548	        "schema_version": "2.0",
   549	        "approval_id": f"apr_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}",
   550	        "approval_type": approval_type,
   551	        "key_id": "key_rb01",
   552	        "run_id": "run-rb01-missing",
   553	        "task_id": "task-rb01",
   554	        "stop_event_id": "stop-rb01",
   555	        "stop_type": "release",
   556	        "stage": "S4_IMPLEMENT",
   557	        "environment": "production",
   558	        "authorized_scope": "deploy/rollback-drill",
   559	        "policy_hash": "p" * 64,
   560	        "ruleset_hash": "r" * 64,
   561	        "input_watermark": _SHA1,
   562	        "expected_state_version": 1,
   563	        "actor": "human-approver",
   564	        "issued_at": now.isoformat(),
   565	        "expires_at": (now + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat(),
   566	        "nonce": uuid.uuid4().hex + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8],
   567	        "decision": "approve",
   568	        "payload": payload,
   569	        "signature": "",
   570	    }
   571	    env["signature"] = sign_envelope(ring.get("key_rb01"), env)
   572	    return env
   573	
   574	
   575	def test_RB_01_no_preauth_rollback_and_wrong_previous_refused(td: Path) -> None:
   576	    """注入：无预授权的回滚动作 / 错误类型的合法签名审批 / 错误旧版本回滚。
   577	    期望：全部拒绝，且零落账、current 绝不切到坏包。
   578	
   579	    腿A（无预授权→拒绝）：RunManager.reserve_action("rollback") 无审批 →
   580	    ActionError；持合法 HMAC 签名但类型为 release 的审批 → ApprovalRejected
   581	    （类型不符）；两次拒绝后事件账本零条（绝不落事件）。
   582	    腿B（错误旧版本→拒绝）：previous 内容漂移 / 目标不存在 / 非 release
   583	    目录三种错误旧版本 → VerifyError/SwitchError 拒绝回切，current 保持 v2。
   584	    """
   585	    # ---- 腿A：无预授权的回滚动作预留一律拒绝（F4-AUTH-001）----
   586	    db = td / "rb01-events.db"
   587	    store = EventStore(str(db))
   588	    ring = ApprovalKeyring.generate("key_rb01")
   589	    rm = RunManager(store, keyring=ring)
   590	    target = "deploy/prod-42"
   591	
   592	    exc = _expect_raise(ActionError, rm.reserve_action,
   593	                        "run-rb01-missing", "rollback", target)
   594	    _ok("审批" in str(exc) or "approval" in str(exc),
   595	        f"无授权预留必须以审批缺失拒绝: {exc}")
   596	    _ok(store.head() == (None, 0), f"拒绝路径必须零落账: {store.head()}")
   597	
   598	    release_payload = {
   599	        "release_id": "20261008T000000Z-deadbeefcafe",
   600	        "artifact_sha256": "a" * 64,
   601	        "manifest_sha256": "b" * 64,
   602	        "environment": "production",
   603	        "previous_release_id": "20261001T000000Z-000000000000",
   604	    }
   605	    wrong_type = _mk_signed_approval(ring, "release", release_payload)
   606	    exc = _expect_raise(ApprovalRejected, rm.reserve_action,
   607	                        "run-rb01-missing", "rollback", target, wrong_type)
   608	    _ok("approval_type" in str(exc) and "rollback" in str(exc),
   609	        f"类型不符必须明确拒绝 rollback 预留: {exc}")
   610	    _ok(store.head() == (None, 0), f"类型不符拒绝仍零落账: {store.head()}")
   611	    store.close()
   612	
   613	    # ---- 腿B：错误旧版本回滚一律拒绝（deploy_framework）----
   614	    root = td / "rb01-root"
   615	    releases, current = root / "releases", root / "current"
   616	    mgr = DeployManager(str(releases), str(current))
   617	    src_v1 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rb-v1", flag="v1")
   618	    src_v2 = _mk_app_source(td, "src-rb-v2", flag="v2")
   619	    r1 = mgr.deploy(src_v1)
   620	    v1_dir = Path(r1["release_dir"])
   621	    mgr.deploy(src_v2)
   622	    v2_real = _real(current)
   623	    _ok(v2_real != _real(v1_dir), "前置：current 已在 v2")
   624	
   625	    # B1：previous 内容漂移（错误旧版本：坏包）
   626	    victim = v1_dir / "app.py"
   627	    os.chmod(victim, 0o644)
   628	    victim.write_text("print('poisoned-previous')\n", encoding="utf-8")
   629	    os.chmod(victim, 0o444)
   630	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.rollback, str(current), str(v1_dir))
   631	    _ok(exc.report["drifted"] == ["app.py"],
   632	        f"坏旧版必须以内容漂移被拒: {exc.report}")
   633	    _ok(_real(current) == v2_real, "B1 拒绝后 current 仍必须停在 v2（绝不切坏包）")
   634	
   635	    # B2：回滚目标不存在
   636	    exc = _expect_raise(SwitchError, mgr.rollback,
   637	                        str(current), str(root / "no-such-release"))
   638	    _ok("rollback target missing" in str(exc),
   639	        f"不存在旧版必须以目标缺失拒绝: {exc}")
   640	    _ok(_real(current) == v2_real, "B2 拒绝后 current 仍停在 v2")
   641	
   642	    # B3：非 release 目录（无 manifest 的伪旧版本）
   643	    fake_prev = root / "fake-previous"
   644	    fake_prev.mkdir(parents=True)
   645	    (fake_prev / "app.py").write_text("print('fake')\n", encoding="utf-8")
   646	    exc = _expect_raise(VerifyError, mgr.rollback, str(current), str(fake_prev))
   647	    _ok(exc.report["missing"] == ["manifest.json"],
   648	        f"伪旧版必须以 manifest 缺失拒绝: {exc.report}")
   649	    _ok(_real(current) == v2_real, "B3 拒绝后 current 仍停在 v2")
   650	
   651	    # 正控：previous 完好时 rollback 正常放行（拒绝是因「错误旧版本」而非误杀）
   652	    os.chmod(victim, 0o644)
   653	    victim.write_text("print('app')\n", encoding="utf-8")
   654	    os.chmod(victim, 0o444)
   655	    mgr.rollback(str(current), str(v1_dir))
   656	    _ok(_real(current) == _real(v1_dir), "正控：完好旧版回滚必须放行")
   657	
   658	
   659	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   660	# 运行器
   661	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   662	def _collect_tests() -> List[Tuple[str, Callable[[Path], None]]]:
   663	    import re
   664	    name_re = re.compile(r"^test_(REL|DEP|RB)_(\d{2})_")
   665	    return [
   666	        (name, fn)
   667	        for name, fn in sorted(globals().items())
   668	        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn) and name_re.match(name)
   669	    ]
   670	
   671	
   672	def main(argv: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
   673	    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
   674	    json_out: Optional[str] = None
   675	    if "--json" in argv:
   676	        json_out = argv[argv.index("--json") + 1]
   677	    import re
   678	    name_re = re.compile(r"^test_(REL|DEP|RB)_(\d{2})_")
   679	
   680	    tests = _collect_tests()
   681	    print("=" * 72)
   682	    print(f"§20 DEP/RB/REL 族专属验收测试（实现 {len(tests)} 条；"
   683	          f"跳过 {len(NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE)} 条见 NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE 登记表）")
   684	    for tid, reason in NOT_IMPLEMENTABLE.items():
   685	        print(f"  SKIP {tid}: {reason}")
   686	    print("-" * 72)
   687	    records: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   688	    failed: List[str] = []
   689	    for name, fn in tests:
   690	        match = name_re.match(name)
   691	        assert match is not None
   692	        test_id = f"{match.group(1)}-{match.group(2)}"
   693	        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="ac-deploy-release-") as td:
   694	            try:
   695	                fn(Path(td))
   696	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": True})
   697	                print(f"  PASS {test_id}  {name}")
   698	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   699	                import traceback
   700	                traceback.print_exc()
   701	                failed.append(name)
   702	                records.append({"id": test_id, "test": name, "passed": False})
   703	                print(f"  FAIL {test_id}  {name}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
   704	    print("-" * 72)
   705	    print(f"结果: {len(records) - len(failed)}/{len(records)} passed"
   706	          f"{'' if not failed else '；失败: ' + ', '.join(failed)}")
   707	    out_path = Path(json_out) if json_out else ARTIFACT_PATH
   708	    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   709	    out_path.write_text(
   710	        json.dumps(records, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
   711	    print(f"逐 ID 结果已写: {out_path}")
   712	    print("=" * 72)
   713	    return 0 if not failed else 1
   714	
   715	
   716	if __name__ == "__main__":
   717	    raise SystemExit(main())
